The empty result of a failed feed is never written to the per-channel cache (D1a-2), so a refetch after the panel recovers downloads the feed again. D1b: a playback-gate deferral returns empty WITHOUT caching and records the deferral; a new `isPlayingNotifier` listener bumps the guide version when playback stops so deferred rows re-fetch (the "retries when playback stops" comment finally has the mechanism it assumed — `_EpgFeedDeferred`, a StateError with the gate's old message). D8: a `force` flag is threaded guide retry → `fetchEpgForChannel` → `_xmltvGuideForKey`, bypassing the 5-min negative cache; a forced retry also forgets the remembered failure. Only an EXPLICIT force bypasses it — a plain re-fetch after a failure still respects the negative cache (the pre-existing iptv_epg_cache_test pins that: a failed feed must not be re-downloaded per channel). The first cut tried auto-detecting retries (a fetch for a channel whose last fetch failed bypassed the cache); that broke the cache test, so it was removed — the service layer cannot tell an automatic re-fetch from a tap, only the explicit flag can. The research probe's D8 test used a plain-call retry; the folded test uses force: true (the guide retry's real shape) and pins the plain-call boundary too. D9: "Last updated" is stamped only when ≥1 programme was stored (pref `iptv_epg_last_updated` + in-memory `lastEpgUpdatedAt`); the settings tile gains a "Last attempt" line (new pref `iptv_epg_last_attempt`, stamped on every fresh fetch) so the two can never be confused again. Cancellations still return [] (the build #28 gate test pins it) — only genuine feed failures throw. The M3U feed defers the same way the Xtream feed does; its failure path already threw.
- 40.3 (tests) — the research probes folded into `test/services/iptv/iptv_epg_reliability_test.dart` as the D1a/D1a-2/D1b/D8 regression suite plus C1/C2/C3 controls, two D9 pref assertions (empty fetch stamps attempt, not updated; data fetch stamps both), and a second D8 test proving the explicit force flag bypasses the cache for a channel that never failed. The guide harness's fake feed now loads EMPTY instead of throwing (1.1.2 D1a: a throwing feed is a failure, so "no data" must be modelled as a loading-empty feed); the two failure tests opt into the throwing feed with a `failXmltv` flag. The settings screen test gains the "Last attempt" line assertion (one combined "Last attempt: Never" line, so the bare "Never" finder still matches only the data line) and the About guard is extended to 1.1.2. D2/D3/D4/D7 stay red in the research probe file for build 3 (not this build). Slang regen (en-only addition: `epgLastAttemptLine`, `changelog2285`).
- Version: 1.1.2-2.25.0+193. Changelog `about.changelog2285`. UNPROVEN (phone-only): the guide shows real programmes on his phone (M1 vs M2 — feed failing vs loaded-but-matching-nothing — still needs the coverage diagnostic / one copied refusal entry to disambiguate); tap-to-retry works on his phone within 5 min of a failure; the guide heals after he stops watching TV; "Last updated" no longer stamps on an empty fetch.

## 41. Third-party EPG sources + per-channel assignment (v1.2.0-2.25.0, versionCode 194)

- 41.1 (the report) — the owner, 2026-11-12/13: "Wheres the option to add 3rd part and see my assigned epgs per channel like for tivimate like i said" — the option did not exist (Settings showed only built-in guide controls). Research: `~/workspace/tmp_work/plezy-tv-research-epg-area/RESEARCH_EPG_AREA.md` §3.1/§3.2 + `probe_epg_area_test.dart` (base 8d443cec9): 8 fail / 4 pass. Build order per §7: 1.1.2 (D1/D1b/D8/D9) first, then this build, then D4/D7/D2/D3.
- 41.2 (the fix) — NEW `lib/services/iptv/iptv_epg_sources.dart`: `IptvEpgSourceConfig` (provider `'provider'` / user `'user_<uuid>'`, kind, url, name, enabled, nullable per-source time shift), `IptvEpgSourceStatus` (last success/failure + reason, programme/channel counts, size, duration; display state computed: upToDate/needsRefresh/failed/disabled), `IptvEpgMapping` (xmltvId + sourceId), `IptvEpgSourcesStore` (per-connection SharedPreferences keys `iptv_epg_sources:<id>`, `iptv_epg_added_sources_first:<id>`, `iptv_epg_mapping:<id>`, `iptv_epg_source_status:<id>`, `iptv_epg_channel_fingerprint:<id>`; corrupt rows read as missing, never crash). `IptvLiveTvSupport` gains the multi-source fetch: `_fetchFeedPrograms` replaces `_fetchXtreamXmltvPrograms` (deleted, with the `_xmltvGuide`/`_xtreamXmltvGuide` wrappers it subsumed) — per source in priority order, manual assignment first, then tvg-id candidates (epg_channel_id, tvg-id, stream id), then normalized name; first source with data wins; per-source offset overrides the global one; a failed source is recorded in its status and skipped, never blocking the next; all-sources-deferred returns `feedDeferred` uncached (D1b preserved); all-sources-failed rethrows the last error with its stack (D1a preserved). `_matchInGuide` is the shared match chain (fetch, details probe, without-guide). `fetchEpgForChannel` / `_fetchXtreamEpg(WithAbort)` / `_fetchM3uEpg` thread a 6-field record carrying sourceId/matchedXmltvId/matchMethod into the per-channel cache entry (old files read back null). `guideCoverage()` gains the per-source `bySourceId` breakdown. Public API: `epgSources`, `addEpgSource` (validates first — http(s) URL, 15 s timeout, 256 KB cap, HTTP 200, bytes parse as XMLTV with a `<tv>` root; a truncated prefix is accepted when the cut is the only parse problem; throws StateError with the reason), `updateEpgSource`, `deleteEpgSource` (provider refused; mappings + status rows for the source go with it), `moveEpgSource`, precedence toggle (default off — provider first), `epgSourceStatuses`, `assignEpg`/`clearEpgAssignment`/`epgAssignmentFor`, `channelEpgDetail` (cached entry, else a local probe of loaded feeds, else a reason from feed state), `epgFeedChannels` (lazy load of all enabled feeds, distinct xmltvIds sorted), `channelsWithoutGuide` (one directory listing for persisted keys, not one stat per channel — the catalog runs to 55k), `reloadTvDetails` (refreshLiveChannelsFromNetwork + refreshEpgCache + feed-match counting; NOT a full catalog sync), `maybeRefreshForQuietHour` (once per boundary, never twice, never on manual-only, never when update time is off). `IptvClient` gains `refreshLiveChannelsFromNetwork` (Xtream: re-fetch get_live_streams, rewrite disk catalog, set memo, store the fingerprint; M3U: drop the playlist cache and re-download) and the playlist-change fingerprint in `iptvChannels()` (raw rows before hidden filtering; first load stores without refreshing; a changed stored fingerprint + setting on refreshes the guide; the new fingerprint is always stored). Settings prefs: `iptvEpgUpdateTimeMinutes` (default 240 = 04:00) and `iptvEpgUpdateOnPlaylistChange` (default true). UI: NEW `iptv_epg_sources_screen.dart` (per-connection: precedence switch, source rows with status chip + enable + shift + reorder + delete, add dialog with validation error, Reload TV details with result dialog, Channels without guide nav), NEW `iptv_epg_no_guide_screen.dart` (grouped by reason, Assign per row), NEW `iptv_epg_assign_screen.dart` (searchable local feed-channel list, tap to assign, Clear when mapped), NEW `epg_channel_detail_sheet.dart` (guide long-press "EPG details" item, IPTV-only). Settings → IPTV gains Update time (Off/00:00/02:00/04:00/06:00) + Update on playlist change + an "EPG sources" group (one tile per IPTV connection). Guide tab runs the quiet-hour check once per guide open (post-frame callback, first IPTV support).
- 41.3 (tests) — the research probes folded into `test/services/iptv/iptv_epg_area_test.dart` (16 tests): beIN proving case (no auto match; assign → serves, survives refreshEpgCache, clears; details name the manual match + noMatch reason); third-party sources via a loopback HttpServer (add validates + appears after provider; rejects non-XMLTV/404/non-URL; user source serves a channel the provider can't; provider-first default vs precedence toggle; disabled never fetched/matched — hit-count invariant is relative, not absolute; per-source +60 shift moves only that source; failing source records failed status with '404' reason while the fetch still answers; reorder/delete, provider cannot be deleted; Assign lists feed channels from all enabled sources); without-guide lists the unmatched channel with its reason; reload TV details reports counts; quiet hour refreshes once per boundary, never twice, never on manual, never when off; playlist change refreshes the guide on a changed list, never on an unchanged one, never when the setting is off. Failing-first on the 1.1.2 base: the file does not compile (methods do not exist). Deviations from the research doc: (a) NO URL dedupe on add — the reorder test adds the same URL twice with different names and expects both; the IPTVnator dedupe applies to playlist-declared URLs, not user-added sources. (b) The disabled-source hit assertion is relative (count before disable vs after), not absolute `== 1` — the doc's validation spec ("fetch the first bytes") means the add itself issues a GET, so an absolute 1 is unreachable; the test file also needed per-test isolation (`hits.clear()` + `userEpgStatus` reset in setUp) or the failing-source test's 404 poisoned every later add. (c) The M3U tvgId-null gate is kept except when a manual mapping exists — the mapping IS the fix for id-less channels (D2 itself stays queued for 1.1.3). (d) `reloadTvDetails`/`channelsWithoutGuide` "with guide" counts feed matches only — short-EPG-only channels count once browsed. Slang regen (en-only additions incl. `changelog2286` + ~35 `epg*` keys).
- Version: 1.2.0-2.25.0+194. Changelog `about.changelog2286`. UNPROVEN (phone-only): adding a real third-party source on his phone (validation over his connection, the source serving channels); the beIN assignment surviving a real refresh/sync; Reload TV details counts on his panel; the 04:00 quiet-hour refresh firing; the EPG sources screen on a Fold viewport; non-English strings for the new screens (en-only keys, slang falls back to base locale).

## 42. EPG accuracy tail — D4/D7/D2/D3 (v1.2.1-2.25.0, versionCode 195)

- 42.1 (the report) — RESEARCH_EPG_AREA.md §7.3, the last four audit defects. D4: short-EPG at the 500 limit truncates the tail days (500 × 15-min slots ≈ 5.2 days); the feed tail is never consulted — live candidate for the owed 1.0.18 day-picker +4..+7 gate. D7: a refreshed feed never reaches already-visited channels (stale per-channel entries stay fresh 24h). D2: an M3U channel without a tvg-id never tries the name fallback (early return before the feed is fetched). D3: duplicate feed programmes served twice with identical keys. Research probe (`probe_epg_area_test.dart`, base 1.2.0 18323c3bd): +7 -5 — D2/D3/D4/D7 fail, D1/C pass, D8 fails as the known 1.1.2 deviation (plain retry; the folded force-flag tests pass).
- 42.2 (the fix) — `lib/media/iptv_live_tv.dart` only. D4: `_shortEpgLimit = 500` constant; `_fetchXtreamShortEpg` returns `(programs, atLimit)` (raw listing count == limit, before window filtering); when short-EPG is non-empty at the limit, `_mergeFeedTail` fetches the feed for [lastShortEnd, to] and merges programmes starting at/after the frontier (boundary-spanning feed programmes dropped, so the same show never renders twice); a failing/deferred feed loses only the tail, a cancellation rethrows. D7: per-channel entries are stamped with the feed generations consulted (`_CachedChannelEpg.feedGenerations`: cache key -> guide fetch time, persisted, `{}` for short-EPG-only and pre-1.2.1 files); `fetchEpgForChannel` treats a servable entry as stale when a stamped feed has a newer in-memory guide or a refresh is in flight for it (the refetch awaits the shared in-flight download, so the new data cannot be missed mid-download); a failed generation refetch serves the old entry instead of a retry row (stale-while-revalidate). The generation map is threaded through the fetch records (`_fetchXtreamEpg(WithAbort)` / `_fetchFeedPrograms` / `_fetchM3uEpg` gain a 7th field; genuine-empty entries are stamped too, so a refresh can heal a channel that had no guide). D2: the tvgId-null gate in `_fetchM3uEpg` is deleted — id-less M3U channels go through `_fetchFeedPrograms` and match by name like Xtream (this reverses the 1.2.0 §41.3(c) decision, which kept the gate). D3: `_CachedXmltvGuide` dedupes by (channel, start) at construction (first-wins), covering both the download and persisted-file paths.
- 42.3 (tests) — the research probes folded into `test/services/iptv/iptv_epg_reliability_test.dart` (now 15 tests: D2 group with the loopback HttpServer harness incl. the C4 control, D3, D4, D7; D1/D8/D9/C unchanged). Failing-first on the 1.2.0 base: probe +7 -5 (D2/D3/D4/D7 fail); on the fix the probe is +11 -1 (only the D8 plain-retry deviation fails) and the reliability file is 15/15. Full `test/services/iptv/` 518/518 (base 513 + 5 new; the 1.2.0 tester's 516 included their own 3-test probe file). Guide 16/16, EPG-cancel 1/1, mini-player 8/8 + 6/6 + 1/1, screens 13/13 (About guard extended to 1.2.1), EPG area 16/16, EPG area screens 6/6. Analyze 53 = base, 0 in changed files. Slang regen (en-only addition: `changelog2287`; flat-map reorder noise, content-identical). Deviations from the research doc: (a) D3's "report new/mod counts in the coverage diagnostic" is NOT built — it needs feed-vs-feed import deltas across refreshes, a diagnostic feature no probe pins; the dedupe itself is at import as specified. (b) D7 is the in-memory generation stamp only — a cold start serves a persisted entry stamped from a previous launch's feed without comparing against the feed file (bounded by the entry TTL, as today); the cross-launch check would need a file-mtime read on the cache-serve path. (c) `cachedEpgForChannels` (search sheet, cache-only by design) does not apply the generation check — the guide is the source of truth for freshness.
- Version: 1.2.1-2.25.0+195. Changelog `about.changelog2287`. UNPROVEN (phone-only): day picker +4..+7 shows data on the Fold (D4 is the in-harness candidate cause); already-visited channels pick up a refreshed feed on his phone; an id-less M3U channel shows programmes by name; no duplicate rows in his guide; non-English strings for the new screens (en-only keys, slang falls back to base locale).

## 43. EPG efficiency — I8 parse-time window filter + I9 conditional fetch (v1.2.2-2.25.0, versionCode 196)

- 43.1 (the report) — RESEARCH_EPG_AREA.md §6 items 9–10 (I8/I9). I8: the retained programme list is the ~180–203 MB floor (§34 26.3 control measurement) — programmes outside the guide window are parsed but never shown; filter them at parse time so they are never retained. I9: every refresh re-downloads + re-parses the whole feed even when unchanged; panel support UNPROVEN (§5).
- 43.2 (the fix) — I8: `parseXmltvStream` gains `windowFromEpoch`/`windowToEpoch`; the filter sits in `tryBuild()` (the single yield point for both self-closing and end-element paths), overlap semantics (`stop > from && start < to`, same as `_mapXmltvPrograms`); the window is `[now − 7d − 2h, now + 8d + 2h]` (`_epgParseWindow` in iptv_live_tv.dart) — 7d is the max daysToLoad/pastDays, +24h covers the update-interval TTL slop while a feed is live, ±2h covers the max ±120 min time offset (offset shifts programme times at map time); fixed maxima, no settings read at parse time; applied in `_loadXmltvGuide` AND `_readPersistedXmltv`; the read-time `_mapXmltvPrograms` filter stays. D4 tail merge window is within [now, now+7d] — covered. I9: new record `XmltvFeedFetch = ({bytes, notModified, etag, lastModified})` in xtream_api.dart; `XtreamApi.getXmltvFeed({etag, lastModified, timeout, abortTrigger})` sends If-None-Match/If-Modified-Since, 304 → notModified + empty stream + response validators, other non-2xx → the same refusal/error path as before; `getXmltvStream` is now a delegating wrapper (a first fetch has no validators, so it never 304s); `_m3uFeedStream` → `_m3uFeedFetch` (same conditional logic for user URLs; the short-lived client is closed by a `_closeClientOnDone` stream wrapper when the body stream ends/errors/is cancelled — a Future-returning fetch cannot close the client in a finally, or it would close before the body is read); fetch closures are now `XmltvFeedFetcher` (`Future<XmltvFeedFetch> Function(Future<void>?, {bool useValidators})`); validators are loaded per attempt in `_epgSourceAttempts`/`_providerSourceAttempt` and captured in the closures (`_providerSourceAttempt` is now async — the `epgSources()` call site awaits it); `_loadXmltvGuide` fetches first — a 304 with a stored guide reuses it (fetchedAt bumped to now so the TTL restarts and D7 per-channel entries re-map from the in-memory feed, validators saved, persisted file mtime touched best-effort), a 304 with no guide anywhere retries once without validators (a second 304 throws exactly like a failed download — a server cannot 304 a request that sent none); validators stored in `IptvEpgSourcesStore` (`iptv_epg_feed_validators:<connectionId>`, JSON map cacheKey → {etag, lastModified}; both-null save deletes the row); `clearEpgCache` clears validators (the backstop would recover anyway). `force` (D8) still sends validators — a 304 is a valid answer to a forced retry.
- 43.3 (tests) — NEW `test/services/iptv/iptv_epg_efficiency_test.dart` (10 tests). I8: boundary overlap (7 programmes, keeps [SpanStart, Inside, SpanEnd]), no-window keeps everything, wide-span Trex-scale (5,000×192 over now−30d, stride 450 min: unfiltered 960,000, filtered 240,000 = expected exactly; parse 32,226 ms; peak RSS delta 0 MB in-harness — the same headroom artifact as §34, the count is the assertion). I9 fetch-level loopback (real XtreamApi): first fetch sends no validators + returns them; second fetch with validators → 304 + zero body bytes; changed feed → 200 + new body/validators; no-validator server → plain fetch. Store: validators round-trip per cacheKey, both-null save deletes the row, clearValidators removes all. I9 integration (support + loopback server, real getXmltvFeed, fake short-EPG [] + scripted channels): 304 revalidation skips download + re-parse (0 body bytes on 2nd request, feed file content unchanged, third fetch serves with no third download); 304 backstop (validators without a guide re-fetch fresh: 3 requests, 2nd with If-None-Match, 3rd without). Failing-first on the 1.2.1 base: the probe file fails to load (new APIs undefined). Two probe-test fixes during the build: the integration server logged every request including player_api.php 404s — now logs xmltv.php only; the per-channel write is fire-and-forget (unawaited), so deleteChannelFile waits for the file to land before deleting (a flake, not a pass). "Zero re-parse" is structural (the 304 branch returns the existing guide before the parse loop) + outcome assertions (0 bytes, unchanged file, same programmes). Full `test/services/iptv/` 528/528 effective (527 + build13 bench timing flake, 6/6 standalone — the known flake, same class as §34). EPG suites 52/52 (area 16 + reliability 15 + cache 21). Guide + screens/iptv 49/49. Mini-player runnable files 25/25; the two known hangers (iptv_build17_mini_player_test 0 tests in 300 s, live_tv_mini_player_first_frame_test 7 pass then hangs on 8th) reproduce identically to base per §34 and were not re-run in this build (one attempt was interrupted by a runtime restart with no output — consistent with wedging, not confirmed). Settings/library screens 15/15. Analyze 53 = base, 0 in changed files (3 new issues found during the build and fixed: epgSources() not awaiting the now-async _providerSourceAttempt — a real bug, the provider would always have been listed; 2 use_null_aware_elements in saveValidators; 2 in the probe file). Slang regen (en-only addition: changelog2288; flat-map reorder noise, content-identical). Deviations from the research doc: (a) the I8 retained window is [now−7d−2h, now+8d+2h], not the doc's [now±7d] shorthand — the doc's window is the *requested* window; the *retained* window must additionally cover the 24h TTL slop (a feed fetched at F serves requests up to F+TTL+7d) and the ±120 min time offset, else a feed served stale near the end of its TTL would lose the +7d day. (b) I9's "zero re-parse" is proven structurally + by outcome, not by a parse counter. (c) A dynamically generated xmltv.php likely sends NO ETag/Last-Modified, so I9 may never fire for the provider feed — it helps static third-party URLs and any panel/CDN that sends validators.
- Version: 1.2.2-2.25.0+196. Changelog `about.changelog2288`. UNPROVEN (phone-only): retained-memory reduction on the Fold during a cold guide open; whether the panel honours conditional fetch (mechanism proven via fake server only — real-panel support stays UNPROVEN under the ban); whether a 304 ever fires against the real panel; non-English strings for the new screens (en-only keys, slang falls back to base locale).

## 44. EPG silence fix — Reload always reports, Update now really refreshes, denial named, poison invalidated (v1.2.3-2.25.0, versionCode 197)

- 44.1 (the report) — RESEARCH_EPG_LOAD.md (base bc97412c7) + probes R1–R5/W1, the owner's phone verdict 2026-11-13 ~12:27 EST: guide STILL EMPTY, "Reload tv details doesnt apear to do anything when clicked and update now runs to quick to be getting epg details". RC1: Reload throws its failure away — the tap is wired but the handler has no try/catch; `reloadTvDetails()` re-fetches the channel list FIRST, which 403s on his network, and the throw escapes before the cache bust and before any report dialog (the error escapes the `unawaited()` future). RC2: Update now performs ZERO network I/O, then claims "Guide updated" — `_updateEpgNow` only calls `refreshEpgCache()` (cache clear); the fetch is deferred to the next guide open, where it fails with no report. RC3: pre-1.2.1 poisoned empty entries serve silently — `_noteEpgAttempt` sits after the cache-serve early return, and 1.2.1's D7 check cannot heal them (pre-1.2.1 entries have `feedGenerations: {}`, so `_isFeedGenerationStale` returns false); the 05:38 1.1.1 D1 silent-empty entries are served on every guide open with no stamp. RC4 (contributing, not an app bug): the panel denies this connection (403 on every call, escalating to timeout). Follow-up from the owner ~12:43 EST: the separate connection was MOBILE DATA (the 403s/timeouts were a mobile-data issue; he got it working), Reload DID report on 12:42 EST ("26151 channels · 0 with guide · 26151 without guide") — the list loads, the EPG is still 0 — so the report must ALSO name the FEED outcome (feed 403 / timeout / loads-but-matches-nothing / loads-with-matches), and Reload must show visible working feedback (a spinner) WHILE it runs, not just the report at the end.
- 44.2 (the fix) — RC1: `reloadTvDetails()` busts the cache FIRST (before the list re-fetch, so poison clears even when the list fails), catches the list failure and carries it on the result (`channelListFailureKind`/`channelListFailureReason`, classified denied/notAnswering/failed by `classifyEpgFeedError`), still tries the feeds after a list failure, and never throws — the handler also has a belt-and-braces try/catch that builds a failure report instead of letting anything escape the tap. The feeds are loaded with `force: true` (D8: an explicit user retry bypasses the 5-minute negative cache, so Reload actually re-tries a feed that failed minutes ago). RC2: NEW `IptvLiveTvSupport.updateEpgNow()` — busts the cache AND loads the enabled feeds once (`force: true`), returns `IptvEpgUpdateNowResult`; the settings handler shows the outcome in a report dialog instead of the "Guide updated" snackbar. It never touches the catalog: no channel-list re-fetch, so its report names each feed's own size rather than match counts (see deviation (c)). Both actions stamp "Last attempt" (D9: a feed load is an attempt). Feed outcomes: NEW `IptvEpgFeedOutcome`/`IptvEpgFeedOutcomeKind` (`loaded` / `loadedNoMatch` / `denied` / `notAnswering` / `deferred` / `failed`) + shared `epgFeedOutcomeLine()` rendering, built by `_feedOutcomes()` from `_loadEnabledFeeds` — which now returns `(loaded, failures, deferred)` instead of dropping failures/deferrals. RC3: a stored entry with ZERO programmes and NO feed generations counts as a miss and refetches — with the gate in deviation (a). Item 3 (denial surfacing): the per-source Failed chip + reason already existed and now records failures reliably (see (b)); NEW guide banner (`guide_tab.dart`: `_iptvEpgLastError` kept from the last non-cancellation fetch error, `_showIptvEpgDeniedBanner` when something failed, nothing loaded, nothing still loading) — "The panel denied access — the guide couldn't load" / "The panel is not answering — the guide couldn't load" / "The guide couldn't load", with a Retry-all that force-retries every failed visible channel (D8). Working feedback (the owner's 12:43 requirement): the Reload row shows a spinner while it runs (`_reloading` state on the sources screen + a new optional `trailing` on `SettingNavigationTile`, cleared BEFORE the report dialog appears); Update now uses a NEW `_WorkingNavigationTile` wrapper (the settings screen is a StatelessWidget, so the working state lives there) with the same spinner. `SettingNavigationTile`'s `trailing` overrides the chevron when set. Version: 1.2.3-2.25.0+197. Changelog `about.changelog2289`. Slang regen (en-only additions: `changelog2289` + ~15 `epg*` keys).
- 44.3 (tests) — the research probes folded into `test/services/iptv/iptv_epg_load_test.dart` (now 12 tests: R1–R5 + NEW R6 ×5 Reload names the feed outcome [list 403 + feed 403 → both named denied; list loads + feed loads with matches → loaded + match counts; M2 feed loads but matches nothing → loadedNoMatch; empty feed → loadedNoMatch; feed timeout → notAnswering] + NEW R7 ×2 Update now attempts + reports [working feed downloaded + reported loaded with no match count; failing feed reported denied + status Failed]) and `test/screens/settings/iptv_epg_load_reload_test.dart` (now 3 tests: W1 + NEW W2 Reload spinner shows during the reload and clears when the report appears + NEW W3 Update now spinner + report dialog naming the empty feed; W3 needed `ensureVisible` before the tap — the tile sits below the fold on the default viewport). NEW guide banner test in `test/screens/livetv/guide_tab_iptv_test.dart` (denied banner appears with both rows failed, Retry-all refetches and clears it; the harness fake gains a `failXmltvError` override so the feed can fail with a 403). The existing `iptv_screens_test.dart` Update-now expectation ("Guide updated" snackbar) is updated to the report dialog (empty-feed line) — it pinned the exact behaviour RC2 removes — and the About guard is extended to 1.2.3 (present, above 1.2.1). Failing-first on the 1.2.1 base: service probe +3 −2 (R1, R2 fail; R3/R4/R5 pass), W1 fails — matching the researcher exactly. On the fix: load 12/12, reload widget 3/3, guide 17/17. Full `test/services/iptv/` 530 total (518 base + 5 researcher probes + 7 new), 528 pass in-suite + 2 known timing flakes (build13 bench, R2 scorer) that pass standalone (build13 6/6, R2 7/7) → effectively 530/530. Screens 35/35 (`iptv_screens` 13 incl. About guard 1.2.3, EPG area screens 6/6, EPG-cancel 1/1, mini-player 8/8 + 6/6 + 1/1). EPG regression: area 16/16, reliability 15/15, cache 21/21 (52/52). Analyze 53 = base, 0 in changed files (one dead helper `_epg` removed from the probe file to land exactly on 53). Deviations from the research doc: (a) the RC3 poison rule is gated on the connection having at least one EPG source — a connection with no source at all (M3U without an EPG URL, no added sources) legitimately writes empty+unstamped entries (`_fetchFeedPrograms` returns [] with no generations when attempts is empty), and refetching those would loop pointlessly; a cancelled fetch also writes an empty+unstamped entry and correctly refetches on revisit (the cancellation meant no data was ever fetched). (b) `_recordSourceFailure` now stores the URL-free message (`epgFeedErrorReason`) instead of `error.toString()` — a `MediaServerHttpException` appends the request host+path to its toString, and the panel URL must never surface in the UI or be persisted in a status. (c) Update now reports feed-level outcomes only (no match counts): `iptvChannels()` can hit the network when cold, so Update now must not call it; Reload (which has the list) names match counts, and the empty-feed case is still named ("loaded, but the feed is empty"). (d) Reload passes `force: true` to `_loadEnabledFeeds` (D8 explicit-retry bypass). (e) The guide banner is driven by the guide's own live fetch errors, not the persisted source statuses — it shows while the guide is open and every visible fetch failed; the persisted Failed chip on the sources screen is the durable record. (f) The Update-now spinner lives in a `_WorkingNavigationTile` wrapper because `IptvSettingsScreen` is a StatelessWidget. (g) Reload/Update-now stamp "Last attempt" — without it, "Last attempt: Never" would be wrong after an Update now that downloads. (h) When the channel list fails, Reload reports feed-level outcomes (no match counts — a match count means nothing without a list). Out of scope, untouched as instructed: D1 throwing, D8 force retry, the 5-minute negative cache, the "Last attempt" stamp.
- Version: 1.2.3-2.25.0+197. Changelog `about.changelog2289`. UNPROVEN (phone-only): Reload reporting a feed 403 vs timeout vs loaded-but-0-matches on his panel; the spinner visible on a real slow reload; the poisoned entries on his phone refetching (his Reload's cache bust likely already cleared them); the guide banner appearing on a real denial and its Retry-all working; the Update-now report on his panel; non-English strings for the new lines (en-only keys, slang falls back to base locale). REBASE NOTE: rebased onto the 1.2.2 tip cc7dd607 (parent, 2026-11-13) — conflicts resolved as expected: `iptv_live_tv.dart` (kept both the I9 fetch comment and the 1.2.3 `_LoadedEpgFeeds` typedef), `en.i18n.json` + generated strings (both changelog2288 and changelog2289 kept, slang regenerated), `pubspec.yaml` (1.2.3-2.25.0+197) + About (both entries, newest first), FORK (both §43 and §44 kept).

## 45. Catalog memory — visible-only memos, disk + index pruned, pressure drop (v1.2.4-2.25.0, versionCode 198)

- Why: the owner's Fold (2026-11-13): 7 memory-pressure events in ~18 min, Startup RSS 579MB, running max 1418MB; the image cache evicted to 0 each time while the memo heap held all 302,114 rows (77% hidden). Research: RESEARCH_CATALOG_MEMORY.md (M1–M3 probes). the owner's asks: "aren't you supposed to only hold the categories that we selected and does it have to be in full blown memory or in a cash file [cache file]?" + "That's probably why it's struggling on low-end devices like the Google streamer" + (scope change) prune hidden rows from DISK too, so the cache shrinks (~594 MB → ~140 MB at his hidden share) + when un-hiding a pruned category, show a notice with a Refresh action right there (in-app, never a system notification).
- 45.1 (item 1 — pressure drop) — NEW `IptvClient.dropCatalogFromMemory()` (nulls the 3 memo futures + loaded flags + partials + category futures + channel maps + series-info futures; deletes NO disk/index data, bumps NO generation, sends NO notification) + NEW `IptvLiveTvSupport.dropEpgFromMemory()` (clears `_epgCache` + `_xmltvCache` in memory only, bumps `_epgVersion` so the guide drops its merged-programs cache and re-fetches visible rows lazily; does NOT delete persisted EPG, does NOT set `_epgCleared`, touches NO "Last updated" stamp; keeps the failed-feed negative cache + playback-gate deferrals) + NEW `MultiServerManager.dropIptvCachesFromMemory()` (both, every connection). Wired in `main.dart`: `didHaveMemoryPressure` (unconditional) + the RSS watchdog (separate from the image-cache ≥8MB floor — the owner's case was image cache 0–6MB while RSS was 700MB — same 60s cooldown gate). `main.dart` also moves `_serverManager = MultiServerManager()` before `addObserver` so the pressure callback can never read the late final before assignment.
- 45.2 (item 2 — visible-only memos) — NEW `VisibleCatalogFilter` (`lib/services/iptv/xtream_api.dart`, top-level, plain values so it crosses into a `compute` isolate): the same rule as `IptvClient._isHiddenCategory` (id switch or category initial), built from the category list (sync) or the disk category names (disk paths). NEW filtered isolate parsers `parse*BodyVisible` + off-thread re-encoders `encode*BodyVisible` (decode + filter + re-encode in the isolate — a main-isolate re-encode of 200k VOD rows froze the app ~1.3s, build #13). The warm-ups, the disk-load branches and the first-load network branches all parse/filter through it; the per-category crawl publishes visible rows only (`_loadVodStreams`/`_loadSeries` take the filter). The sync publishes visible rows only (`_publishSyncedCatalog` via `_visibleFetchedRows`) and the partial guard compares VISIBLE-fetched vs VISIBLE-indexed (comparing the full fetch against the visible land would never fire — a half-catalog fetch would pass as complete and clobber a good index). The guard's denominator is the rows the index holds for categories visible RIGHT NOW (NEW `IptvSearchIndex.visibleRowCount`) — the stored count is from the last sync, so when the hidden set grew since, comparing against it would cry partial on a complete fetch and leave the type due forever. The Enter-path disk fallbacks (`_vodRowsForEnterSearch` / `_seriesRowsForEnterSearch` / `_liveRowsForEnterSearch`) also parse VISIBLE-only: on a cold start before the warm-up lands they used to scan the full disk file (200k rows, 77% hidden) on every Enter search.
- 45.3 (disk + index prune — the owner's decision, supersedes the v1.1.0 Option B "disk stays full" split, §37.2, and the research doc's §7 split) — the disk file holds visible rows only (sync writes the isolate-encoded visible body; first-load writes encode off-thread; an all-hidden type writes an empty-array file, not no file — sync AND first-load loaders both, and `_readDiskCatalog` serves a successfully-parsed empty file instead of deleting it, §45.8e). The index prunes at write time: `writeIndexTypeInBackground` skips hidden rows AND deletes rows in hidden categories after the upsert (new `upsertType(deleteCategoryIds:)` param, same-transaction FTS+items delete — the merge keeps rows the fetch did not return, so a skip alone would leave them lingering); `rowCount` is now the visible landed count. The wire still downloads the full lists (panel sends whole lists in one call — unchanged). ONE switch for the whole prune (the owner's two-mode decision, 2026-11-13 ~14:19 EST — "Low RAM" default on first install, "Instant search" opt-in with a more-RAM/bigger-cache disclaimer): `IptvClient.pruneHiddenCatalogRows` (static, default true; `lib/media/iptv_client.dart`, next to `_hiddenCategories`) — every STORAGE filter reads the hidden sets through `_pruneHiddenCategoryIds` / `_pruneHiddenCategoryInitials` (memos via `_visibleCatalogFilterFor`, sync publish + guard via `_visibleFetchedRows`, sync + seed isolate params, sync disk-encode filter, guard `visibleRowCount`); the mode setting itself is a SEPARATE small build after 1.2.4 — it makes this flag conditional on the mode and restores the full cache via a re-sync when Instant search is picked (a re-sync with the switch off lands the full rows again — T5 proves it). The DISPLAY filter (`_isHiddenCategory`, search/browse read-time filters) stays on the raw hidden sets: hidden categories stay hidden on screen in both modes; the switch only decides what is STORED.
- 45.4 (the trade-off, accepted by the owner) — un-hiding a category now requires a re-sync, because its rows are gone from disk/index until the next catalog download. This is the same breakage that forced the v1.0.16 → 1.1.0 revert (§37.2) — the guard against repeating it is the un-hide notice, not silent re-appearance: NEW `lib/widgets/iptv_unhide_refresh_notice.dart` (`maybeShowUnhideRefreshNotice`) + NEW `IptvClient.unhiddenCategoriesNeedRefresh` (awaits the toggle's re-derive, true only when the un-hidden categories have no rows in the fresh memos) — called from `IptvCategoriesScreen._persist` (ids + initials resolved from the loaded rows) and `LiveTvScreen._toggleChannelCategory` (per-category id). The notice is a snackbar with a Refresh action (forced sync, reports done/failed). Rows still on disk come back in the toggle's background re-derive with no network.
- 45.5 (toggle re-derive) — `IptvClient.updateConnection` snapshots the hidden sets, swaps, and on a hidden-set change fires `_rederiveMemosFromDisk()`: the three private disk warm-ups with `force: true` (re-parse + swap the memo when done; the stale memos keep serving until then, so build #17's instant toggle is preserved — NOT the public warm-ups, which would re-fire the FTS seed). The re-derive notifies when it lands so the tabs reload with fresh memos.
- 45.6 (id resolution) — NEW `IptvSearchIndex.resolveById` + `fetchItem` index fallback (movie/series/live/episode series-row): a favourite/resume hidden after it was saved still opens until a sync prunes the row. NEW nullable `categoryId` on `IptvFavoriteItem` + `IptvResumeItem` (backward-compatible; populated at save time from the MediaItem / the playback snapshot stash) + NEW `IptvClient.itemNeedsCatalogRefresh` (true only when the snapshot's category is visible now but the item does not resolve — a refresh would bring it back; false when still hidden or legacy no-category).
- 45.7 (stamped-0 rule) — a complete land with every category hidden stamps 0 rows; that is a deliberate empty land, not a cold type. The up-to-date skips (`_syncSearchIndex`), `skipColdTypes` (needs `lastSync == null` too) and `_refreshCatalogViaSyncIfDue` now judge by the stamp, not the row count — or an all-hidden type would re-sync on every bind/browse. NO schema bump (version stays 2): a pre-1.2.4 full index/disk is a SUPERSET, not lossy — the memos filter it at read time until the next sync prunes it, and search filters hidden rows at read time too.
- 45.8 (known behaviours / follow-ups, NOT built) — (a) a first-launch network fallback before the sync lands may briefly hold/write hidden-by-initial rows (the disk category names do not exist yet, so the filter has no initial map); the sync publish + next sync prune heal it. (b) No upgrade-time prune: existing full disk files shrink on the next sync (weekly/due/manual/refresh), not at bind — the memory fix lands at first bind via visible-only memos. (c) Items 3–6 of RESEARCH_CATALOG_MEMORY.md §7 (serialize/defer bind, file paths to isolates, per-category disk files, disk-paged browse) are NOT built — they are the follow-ups if the 2GB numbers need them. (d) The 1.2.3 Reload path (`refreshLiveChannelsFromNetwork`) still writes the raw body + publishes full rows — the rebase must route its disk write + memo publish through the filtered paths, or Reload re-grows the disk file and re-fills the live memo. (e) A successfully-parsed empty disk file is served, not deleted (`_readDiskCatalog`): it is the deliberate all-hidden empty-array file (§45.3/§45.7) — a transient empty panel answer never writes a file, so an empty file on disk can only be deliberate; corruption still throws and falls back to the network. (f) The search diagnostics' hiddenMatches/hiddenInitials read 0 / empty after a prune: a pruned row is not stored, so it can never match a search to be counted as hidden (the read-time filter still hides rows from a legacy full index/disk). (g) The no-op encode shortcut (§45.2): when nothing is hidden, the first-load and sync disk writes store the raw body straight — the off-thread encode's isolate round trip copies a ~40MB body across the port and blocks the main isolate ~300ms at Trex scale (build #13 bench caught it: hub 402ms vs 115ms on base; after the shortcut, hub 204ms, page 2 187ms). (h) REBASE NOTE: the About changelog entry for 1.2.4 (`changelog2290`, newest first) is added at the top of `about_screen.dart` — the rebase must insert the 1.2.2 (`changelog2288`) and 1.2.3 (`changelog2289`) entries between 1.2.1 (2287) and 1.2.4 (2290), and the screens About guard must gain the 1.2.2/1.2.3 version lines in order.
- 45.9 (2GB measurement, the owner 2026-11-13 — measured on the Trex-scale fixture, `test/services/iptv/iptv_catalog_memory_test.dart` M2, standalone single-file runs) — BASE (1.2.1): disk 59MB, index DB 85MB, index rows full (55,253 / 200,225 / 46,636), rssAfterWarmups 915MB, rssAfterSeed 805MB, bind peak delta 391MB in-suite / 459MB standalone. FIX (1.2.4, two runs): index DB 18MB, index rows visible-only (10,677 / 39,169 / 8,852), rssAfterWarmups 753–859MB, rssAfterSeed 498–512MB, bind peak delta 455–525MB. Disk cache 59MB → 11.5MB (D1). VERDICT — 4GB Streamer: the STEADY STATE fits (rssAfterSeed ~500MB, at the ≤~500MB target; the retained memos are ~90MB visible instead of ~399MB full) but the BIND PEAK does not (455–525MB delta, over the ≤~700MB peak target once the engine/splash/Discover/EPG are counted on top — the peak alone is most of a 4GB box's headroom, and it is unchanged by items 1–2). 2GB Fire Stick: NO, items 1–2 alone do NOT reach 2GB comfort — the peak is the blocker, not the steady state. The peak is dominated by the TRANSIENT whole-body copies (3 warm-ups + FTS seed concurrently, each copying a full disk body into its isolate); items 1–2 cut what is RETAINED, not what is copied. Follow-ups needed for the peak: item 3 (serialize + defer the bind work — the research doc's <200MB bound assumed "one body copy at a time") and item 4 (pass file paths to isolates instead of bodies). Item 6 (disk-paged browse) would cut the steady state further on the biggest catalogs. The peak-delta metric itself is noisy in-process (455 vs 525MB on identical fix runs; 200–459MB across base runs), so M2 asserts the deterministic wins (visible index rows, index DB <30MB) and reports the peak as a measurement, not a bound. `isLowEndHardware` (≤2.2GB) is FALSE on the 4GB Streamer but TRUE on a 2GB Fire Stick, so the TV efficiency items keyed off it already fire on Fire Sticks; the 3–4GB boxes need the item-8 gate fix (follow-up, not this build).
- 45.10 (tests, `test/services/iptv/iptv_catalog_memory_test.dart`, all pass standalone) — M1 visible-only memos (39,169 / 8,852 / 10,677 rows, 0 hidden matches); M2 RSS + visible index (see 45.9); M3 index serves a category page from disk (50+50 rows, hidden category count 0); D1 pruning sync shrinks the disk 59MB → 11.5MB; T1 toggle re-derives from disk with no network; T2 un-hide signal fires only when pruned; T2b the notice widget shows with a Refresh action and reports done (widget test — all catalog work inside `tester.runAsync`, real async; the Trex-scale sync is replaced by the small panel for the Refresh leg, the restore being proven by D1+T2); T3 pruned favourite/resume resolves gracefully + refresh signal; T4 partial guard stays correct when the hidden set grows; T5 the prune switch off keeps the full catalog (Instant search) and a re-sync restores/prunes it; P1 pressure drop sheds the memos and they reload from disk. Harness lessons: panel fakes go through `testXtreamApi` (httpClient), not `testHttpClient`; `_searchIndex()` memoizes its future, so toggling `debugDisableDisk` mid-test cannot un-open a warm index; `Future.delayed` in a `testWidgets` body is fake (never fires without a pump) — use `runAsync`; a snackbar queued behind another needs a pump WITH a duration before it is built; the prune switch is static — the file tearDown resets it.
- Tests — `test/services/iptv/iptv_catalog_memory_test.dart` (M1/M2/M3 folded in as named regression tests + disk-shrink proof + toggle re-derive + un-hide notice signal + pruned favourite/resume + pressure drop): TBD after the run. `flutter analyze` 53 = base, 0 in changed files.
- Version: 1.2.4-2.25.0+198. Changelog `about.changelog2290`. UNPROVEN (phone-only): the Fold's RSS after the fix; the un-hide notice + Refresh on his phone; an all-hidden type not re-syncing every bind; the pressure drop firing on a real pressure event.

### 34. Streaming EPG parse + lazy feed (R3) + day-picker window + prune + first-paint abort (v1.0.18-2.25.0, versionCode 190)
- Why: a Trex-scale guide feed (5,000 channels x 192 programmes = 960,000 programmes, ~151 MB XML) cost ~1.45 GB peak RSS and ~30 s on the production cold-start path (measured on base: 29,659 ms, peak RSS 2,126 MB, +1,450 MB above baseline) — the feed was held ~4x at once (whole UTF-16 String, isolate copy, DOM tree, result list). Every guide browse of a channel whose persisted feed was stale also cost one spurious `xmltv.php` download + whole-document parse even when short-EPG answered the row (R3). And the day picker offered today + 7 days while the fetch window was 4 days from now, so days +4...+7 could never show data. Research: `~/workspace/tmp_work/plezy-tv-research-26-epg/RESEARCH_26_EPG.md` (base dd402ea37) + probes `probe_26_streaming_test.dart` (P1) + `probe_26_lazy_feed_test.dart` (P2 + P2b). Failing-first verified on a temp base worktree: P1 fails to load (`Method not found: 'parseXmltvStream'`), P2 fails behaviorally (`Expected: <0>, Actual: <1>` for xmltvCalls), P2b passes. Zero new panel requests; fetch cadence unchanged; R3 reduces volume.
- 26.1 (streaming parser) — NEW `parseXmltvStream(Stream<List<int>> bytes) -> Stream<XmltvProgram>` in `lib/services/iptv/xmltv_parser.dart`, using the `xml` 7.0.1 chunked event API (`Stream<String>.toXmlEvents(validateNesting: true)` over `utf8.decoder`); `convert` / `parseEvents(String)` are never used on the feed (they materialise everything). Yields one programme per `<programme>` element as it closes, in document order; same tolerance as `parseXmltv` (bad programme skipped, never fatal; self-closing programmes, CDATA, entities, nested markup in titles all handled); only a structurally unparseable document fails (truncated/empty). `parseXmltv` is kept (small feeds + existing tests). P1: 960,000 programmes yielded from the lazily-generated 151 MB fixture in 18,605 ms, peak RSS delta 0 MB (DOM baseline: 35,887 ms parse, ~1.45 GB peak on the production path).
- 26.2 (stream the download end-to-end) — NEW `XtreamApi.getXmltvStream({timeout, abortTrigger}) -> Stream<List<int>>` (AbortableRequest, socket abort preserved; 120 s timeout bounds the send + each quiet gap between chunks, so a slow connection that keeps delivering still completes; error bodies buffered only for the refusal record, as `_getText` does; gzip already decoded by the transport — verified by the loopback gzip test, server gzips, client must still parse, `Accept-Encoding` contains gzip). `getXmltv` is reimplemented on top of the stream (one network path). `_loadXmltvGuide` consumes the stream through `parseXmltvStream` and tees raw bytes to a temp file (`feed_<key>.tmp`, same directory so the promote is a rename); the temp file is promoted to `feed_<key>.xml` only on successful parse completion — the "only a parseable feed is persisted" invariant stands; on failure/cancel the temp file is deleted, the previously persisted feed is kept, the partial in-memory guide is discarded, and cancellation does NOT poison the 5-min negative cache (the #28 rule, extended to the parse). `compute(parseXmltv, body)` is gone from both feed paths (no remaining call site in `lib/`). The M3U path (`_m3uFeedStream`) now uses an AbortableRequest with a short-lived client closed on end/error/cancel — the old bare `http.get` buffered the whole body and its abort only raced the get. Synchronous file I/O in `_loadXmltvGuide`/`_readPersistedXmltv` (sync create/write/rename/delete, `_readFileInChunks` in 64 KB sync chunks): async dart:io never completes inside testWidgets fake async (proven by probe: an awaited `File.exists()` hangs, `existsSync()` works), and the fetch path is awaited by widget tests. Concurrent-call dedup added in `_xmltvGuideForKey`: a caller that arrives while another started the download/refresh shares the in-flight future instead of starting a second download teeing to the same temp file.
- 26.3 (stream the persisted-file read) — `_readPersistedXmltv` opens the file as a byte stream (`_readFileInChunks`, 64 KB sync chunks) through `parseXmltvStream`; same corrupt-file rule (delete, return null). P3 cold-start measurement (scratch test, deleted after the run; fresh 151,471,587-byte persisted feed, short-EPG empty): **18,965 ms, 14 programmes served, peak RSS delta 0 MB in-harness** vs base **29,659 ms, +1,450 MB peak (2,126 MB total)**. The 0 MB in-harness delta is a measurement artifact, not a zero-cost claim: the app process's heap already has enough committed headroom (baseline 293 MB) that the retained list fits inside it without new page commits — a control test isolating the retained list (960k `XmltvProgram` objects + byChannel index, same shapes) measured **180 MB RSS delta** in a small-heap process, inside the research's 150–250 MB estimate. The transient ~1.2 GB (String + isolate copies + DOM) is gone; the ~180 MB retained list + indexes remain as the floor — a disk-backed guide index is the follow-on if that floor is still too big, out of scope here.
- 26.4 (R3 lazy feed) — `_fetchXtreamEpgWithAbort` no longer starts the feed future unconditionally: the feed is fetched only when `get_short_epg` misses (empty) or fails for the channel, after the short-EPG answer. In-flight dedup, stale-while-revalidate, and the #28 playback gate all stand (a short-EPG miss while playing still defers the feed). The M3U branch inherits the fix through the same `_xmltvGuideForKey`. The pinned test `get_short_epg serves the channel; the xmltv.php feed still loads in the background` is REPLACED (not deleted): it now asserts the feed is NOT fetched when short-EPG answers (2 s settle, then `xmltvCalls == 0`); the failing-feed test likewise expects 0 calls while short-EPG answers. Trade-off, as the research stated: a miss now starts the download only after short-EPG answers empty/fails (up to ~15 s later in the worst case), accepted — the miss path already waits on the feed, the per-channel cache means the penalty is paid once per channel per TTL, and fewer panel requests is the standing priority.
- 26.5 (conditional fetch — NOT built): investigate-first outcome. Panel support for `ETag` / `Last-Modified` on `xmltv.php` is unverifiable without a panel call (banned), and no headers-returning seam was added. R3 already removes the spurious downloads, so the remaining bonus is small; record the finding and close it. Do not build this without panel evidence.
- 26.6 (prune) — NEW `_pruneStalePersistedEpg(ttl)`, fire-and-forget after each per-channel write: deletes per-channel JSON files older than the update interval (recursive listing — files live in `iptv_epg/<conn>/`); manual-only (null ttl) never prunes; feed files (`feed_*.xml`) are never touched (own TTL logic). Best-effort, never breaks the guide.
- 26.7 (day-picker window) — `fetchEpgForChannel` now widens its fetch window to the REQUESTED window (`[from, to]` from the guide's `_gridStart`/`_gridEnd`, which derive from the picked day) instead of only `[now - pastDays, now + daysToLoad]`; the settings window remains the default for the current day. The feed already holds the data; no extra download. The old service test "days window bounds the fetch" is replaced with the new rule (requested window bounds the result). DEVIATION from the research acceptance (guide-tab day +6 UI test): not added — the guide passes the picked day's window straight into `fetchEpgForChannel` (`guide_tab.dart:1142`), so the service-level 26.7 test (day +6, programmes render, 0 feed calls on a fresh persisted feed) covers the layer that changed; the picker-to-grid wiring is covered by existing guide tests.
- 26.8 (first-paint abort) — `_fetchXtreamShortEpg` now fires a local `AbortController` when the 15 s first-paint timeout hits, so the underlying 60 s request aborts instead of holding a socket nobody watches. The abort is local to the call (firing the channel's own controller would trip the caller's `throwIfAborted()` and break the feed fallback).
- Tests: Rebase: cherry-picked 8f63ccfa onto f06949d89 (v1.0.16); 6 conflicts resolved by hand (pubspec version, About entries, iptv_screens_test guards, en.i18n.json changelog blocks, both generated strings files via `dart run slang` regen); re-versioned 1.0.15+187 → 1.0.18+190; `git diff 8f63ccfa` shows only the 1.0.16 chain's changes + the version strings + the resolutions. Probes failing-first on a temp base worktree (P1 fails to load, P2 fails `Expected: <0>, Actual: <1>`, P2b passes); on the fix tree the new files pass — streaming 9/9 (P1: 960,000 programmes in 18,605 ms standalone / 20,760 ms in-suite, peak RSS delta 0 MB; 7 unit cases; gzip loopback) and lazy-feed 6/6 (P2, P2b, 26.7 day +6, 26.8, 26.6, 26.3). Full `test/services/iptv/` **464/464** (04:39) on the rebased tree — 449 from the 1.0.16 base + 15 new #26 tests; the build13 bench flake passed in-suite this time. Screens `test/screens/iptv/` 23/23 (incl. About guard 1.0.18). R2 enter-rule search 2/2 first try. Guide livetv per-file on the rebased tree: 93/93 across the 15 passing files (+ 7 in the first-frame file before it wedges, 100 tests passing in total); the two known hangers re-checked on the rebased tree (`iptv_build17_mini_player_test.dart` 0 tests in 300 s, `live_tv_mini_player_first_frame_test.dart` 7 pass then hang on the 8th test at 04:41) reproduce IDENTICALLY to base — pre-existing, not #26. Analyze 53 = baseline (49 vendored wakelock errors + 4 lint in test files), 0 in #26 files. Rebased again onto 786b8971c (v1.0.17 tip) after 1.0.17 shipped: cherry-picked f6341f0e3; same 6 conflicts resolved by hand, keeping BOTH sides (pubspec 1.0.18, About entries 1.0.18 above 1.0.17, iptv_screens_test guards for both versions — the 1.0.18 guard had to be ordered after the 1.0.17 guard since it references `newest17`, caught by a file load failure and fixed; en.i18n.json changelog2281 + changelog2282, generated strings via `dart run slang` regen — the regen also reorders a few unrelated switch entries, the same slang nondeterminism as the 1.0.17 chain's own regen, semantically identical); `git diff f6341f0e3` shows only the 1.0.17 chain's changes (79b5183e4 + 786b8971c) + the resolutions; all #26-only files byte-identical. Re-verified on the rebased tree: streaming 9/9, lazy-feed 6/6, full iptv 474/475 in-suite (build13 bench flake, 6/6 alone) = effective 475/475 (04:29), screens 23/23 (incl. BOTH About guards 1.0.17 + 1.0.18), R2 enter-rule 10/10, analyze 53 = baseline, guide livetv per-file 98/98 across the 16 passing files, both hangers identical to base (mini_player +0 at 04:55, first_frame +7 at 04:45). Fakes in 16 existing test files gain a `getXmltvStream` override built on their canned `getXmltv` (the production path streams; the fakes do not need to). About guard extended to 1.0.18 (present, above 1.0.16). Version: 1.0.18-2.25.0+190, changelog `about.changelog2282`, About entry added, slang regenerated. UNPROVEN (phone-only): actual peak RSS on the Fold during a cold guide open; battery cost of the ~19 s parse; behaviour on slow mobile data; whether the ~180 MB retained list is acceptable on-device; Trex guide size was never measured (151 MB fixture is a model); panel ETag/Last-Modified support; guide day +6 rendering on a real viewport.

## 46. EPG matching fix — display-name parsing, case-insensitive + numeric tvg-id, jellyfin-strength normalization, loose fallback, uniqueness gating, Reload diagnostics (v1.2.5-2.25.0, versionCode 199)

- Why: the owner's phone (2026-11-13 ~15:56 EST, LTE): Reload reported "26151 channels · 0 with guide · 26151 without guide" + "Provider guide: loaded, but matched none of your 26151 channels". The feed LOADS but matches ZERO channels — the problem moved from feed-not-answering (1.2.3) to feed-loads-but-matches-nothing. Research: RESEARCH_EPG_MATCHING.md (`~/workspace/tmp_work/plezy-tv-research-epg-matching/`): the matcher stripped only `|XX|` tags + lowercased + stripped non-alphanumerics (no HD/FHD/4K strip, no US-/CA: prefix strip, no accent folding); the parser NEVER read `<channel>`/`<display-name>` — the name fallback compared the playlist name against the feed's raw channel id, never its human name. Ranked cause #1: feed ids in a different namespace than the panel's epg_channel_ids.
- 46.1 (diagnostics first, Fix 1) — NEW `XmltvParseStats` (`lib/services/iptv/xmltv_parser.dart`): `programsSeen` (pre-window), `programsYielded` (post-window), `channelIds` (distinct `<channel>` ids), `channelDisplayNames` (id → display names, merged across duplicate `<channel>` elements), `programChannelIds` (every programme channel attr seen, pre-filter). Both `parseXmltv` and `parseXmltvStream` accept `stats:`; the stream parser records channel ids/names + counts before the I8 window check. NEW `IptvEpgMatchTiers` / `IptvEpgFeedSample` / `IptvEpgUnmatchedSample` (`lib/services/iptv/iptv_epg_sources.dart`); `IptvReloadTvDetailsResult` gains `matchTiers`, `feedSamples` (cap 5, first loaded feed, sorted by xmltvId), `sampleUnmatched` (cap 5, list order), `epgChannelIdCount`; `IptvEpgFeedOutcome` gains `feedProgramsParsed`, `feedProgramsRetained`, `feedChannelsParsed`. `reloadTvDetails` resolves every channel through the cross-feed resolver and counts per-tier matches + no-match reasons, logs one `appLogger.i` line with tiers + feed stats + samples + epg-id count (lands in the Logs export); `updateEpgNow` logs the feed stats. The Reload dialog renders the match breakdown, unmatched breakdown, per-source feed stats, feed sample, unmatched sample, epg_channel_id count.
- 46.2 (display-name parsing + guide indexes, Fix 2) — `_CachedXmltvGuide` gains `displayNames`, `programsParsed`, `channelsParsed`, `channelIdsSeen` (union of `<channel>` ids + programme channel attrs — an id whose programmes all fell outside the window still exists for the loose fallback); the 304-reuse path carries the existing guide's stats forward; `_readPersistedXmltv` parses with stats. NEW indexes (all `late final`, programme references not copies — a few MB at Trex scale): `byDisplayName`, `byChannelFolded` + `foldedChannelIds` (unique folds only), `byNumericId` + `numericChannelIds` (unique only), `byLooseName`, `displayNameChannels` / `normalizedNameChannels` / `looseNameChannels` (key → distinct feed ids), `mixedChannelIds` / `looseMixedChannelIds`. Helpers `nameCandidatesFor` / `looseCandidatesFor` (union of indexes, minus mixed ids).
- 46.3 (match chain rewrite, Fixes 3/5/6/7) — `_matchInGuide` now returns a non-null `_GuideResolution` record `{programs, matchedXmltvId, matchMethod, sourceId, blockedReason, blockedCandidates}` (never null — a non-match names its reason). Tiers: (1) manual (unchanged, falls through when empty); (2) tvg-id exact; (3) tvg-id case-insensitive via unique folds; (4) numeric (`int.tryParse(channelKey)` vs unique numeric feed ids); (5) name via `nameCandidatesFor(normalizeEpgChannelName(channelName))` — mixed ids removed, country-gated (playlist country via `epgChannelCountry`; candidates with no country or the same country kept; none left → 'foreign'), plain preferred over "+1" timeshift copies, unique → 'name', >1 left → 'ambiguous'; (6) loose-name fallback ONLY when an earlier tier identified a feed id that brought no in-window programmes — `looseEpgChannelNameKey` (tier tags stripped, sports→sport), country required (playlist country OR the identified id's `.xx` suffix), unique serving candidate → 'name-loose'. No-match reasons: 'ambiguous' / 'foreign' / 'matched-but-no-programmes' / 'tvg-id-not-in-feed' / 'no-tvg-id' / 'no-feed'. NEW `_resolveChannelAcrossFeeds` (first match wins across loaded feeds in priority order; else the first feed's blocked resolution) — used by `_channelHasFeedMatch`, `channelsWithoutGuide`, `channelEpgDetail`/`_probeLocalMatch`, Reload tier counting. Fetch path treats 'name-loose' as `feedByName`.
- 46.4 (normalizer, Fix 4 — jellyfin-tentacle `channel_names.py` ported, `lib/media/iptv_live_tv.dart`) — `_epgNameWords`: unicode fold (247-entry table: superscripts + Latin-1/Extended-A accents → base; æ→ae, œ→oe, ß→ss, þ→th, Ĳ→ij; full-width → ASCII), HTML entities decoded, `|XX|`/`XX|` tags stripped, prefix strip (2-letter codes + USA/EXYU/LATAM/ARAB, delimiters `|]):` + dash-followed-by-whitespace), `@Feed` + `.xx` suffix strips, `&`→` and `, `+`→` plus `, quality markers dropped as whole words (hd fhd uhd sd hq lq 4k 8k hdr hevc h264 h265 raw 1080p 1080i 720p 576i 50fps 60fps — "HDNet" keeps "hdnet"). Loose key additionally strips tier tags (VIP:/NOW:) and reads sports→sport. Helpers: `epgChannelCountry` (72 ISO 639-1 language-only codes; "EN:" names English, no country; uk→gb, usa→us), `epgFeedIdCountry`, `epgFeedChannelCountries`, `epgDisplayNamesAreOneChannel` (jellyfin `_one_channel`: identity = words minus timeshift minus generic words; brand-prefix suffix rule; number-only names don't count). CONTRACT CHANGE: `normalizeEpgChannelName('A&E HD')` is now `'aande'` (was `'aehd'`); `'US| COMEDY CENTRAL HD'` is now `'comedycentral'` (was `'comedycentralhd'`). DEVIATION from the template (verified by running the cloned `channel_names.py`): the template's raw key does NOT meet §5 rows 2/3/5/7 — its prefix regex has no dash delimiter and it never strips the `.xx` suffix; the dash delimiter + suffix strip are PlezTV additions. DEVIATION from jellyfin (documented): the loose fallback runs even when a manual mapping exists but brought nothing — PlezTV's manual tier falls through to the auto tiers (long-standing), and the loose tier is one of them; jellyfin's override never falls through at all. NOT built (explicitly not recommended): fuzzy/Levenshtein auto-matching; no community feed as the fix.
- 46.5 (per-channel reasons + Assign, Fixes 7/9) — `IptvEpgNoGuideReason` gains `ambiguous`, `foreign`, `matchedButNoPrograms`; `IptvChannelWithoutGuide` gains `candidates` (cap 3 shown as subtitle); `channelsWithoutGuide` resolves per channel and reports the provider feed's block reason (falling back to the global reason when no feed is loaded); `channelEpgDetail`/`_probeLocalMatch` return the resolution so the detail carries the per-channel reason (probe now returns `({detail, resolution})`). `IptvEpgFeedChannel` gains `displayNames` (populated by `epgFeedChannels`); the Assign picker shows the first display name as the title (raw id in the subtitle) and searches normalized (id + display names). `normalizeEpgChannelName` is now public (not `@visibleForTesting`) — the Assign screen uses it.
- 46.6 (feed-health guard, Fix 8) — `epgFeedOutcomeLine`: `loadedNoMatch` + `feedChannelsParsed == 0` → "loaded, but the feed contains no channels"; retained == 0 + parsed > 0 → "loaded, but all {parsed} programmes fall outside the loaded window"; retained == 0 → "loaded, but the feed contains no programmes". (The existing `loaded` + `feedChannels == 0` case already covers Update-now.)
- 46.7 (tests) — NEW `test/services/iptv/iptv_epg_matching_1_2_5_test.dart` (22 tests): §5 table (failing-first on the 1.2.4 base — 6 of 9 rows did not meet), parser display-name + pre/post-filter stats, country/identity helpers, integration via a fake provider feed (folded tvg-id, numeric, name-via-display-name, ambiguity + foreign gating with without-guide reasons/candidates, TNT loose fallback, matched-but-no-programmes, mixed id never name-matched, empty-feed + outside-window health lines, the combined Reload diagnostics test with per-tier counts/samples/epg-id count, Assign display names + normalized search). UPDATED by design: the build17 normalize group (new expectations); the beIN proving case in `iptv_epg_area_test.dart` — feed id `'beIN Sports 1'` now matches by name (`'beinsports1'` both sides), so the proving case moved to feed id `'beIN 1'`.
- Version: 1.2.5-2.25.0+199. Changelog `about.changelog2291`. UNPROVEN (phone-only): the guide actually filling on the owner's phone (this build is the matching fix only — it does not claim the guide is fixed or 2GB comfort); Reload tier counts on the real feed; the loose fallback on a real split feed id.

## 47. Catalog memory, items 3+4 — serialize + defer the bind, file paths to isolates — plus debug log export (v1.2.6-2.25.0, versionCode 200)

- Why: RESEARCH_CATALOG_MEMORY.md §7 items 3+4 (the 1.2.4 build did items 1–2: visible-only memos + disk/index prune). The M2 probe on the 1.2.5 base measured the bind at Trex scale (55,253 live / 200,225 VOD / 46,636 series, 32 hidden initials): peak RSS delta 279MB in-suite, **512MB standalone**, steady-state growth +81MB after the bind. The bind fired the three warm-ups + the FTS seed CONCURRENTLY (four isolates, each holding a whole-body copy), and every disk read copied the tens-of-MB body through the isolate message channel.
- 47.1 (item 4, file paths) — NEW `IptvCatalogDiskCache.filePath(connectionId, dataset)` (`lib/services/iptv/iptv_catalog_cache.dart`): the on-disk path when the file exists, null when absent or the disk is disabled; same miss semantics as `read`. NEW `XtreamApi.parseLiveStreamsFileVisible` / `parseVodStreamsFileVisible` / `parseSeriesFileVisible` (`lib/services/iptv/xtream_api.dart`): `compute` with `_VisibleFileParseParams(path, filter)` — the isolate reads the file itself, so the main isolate never holds the body and the port carries only the path + filter. NEW `IptvClient._readDiskCatalogFromFile` (`lib/media/iptv_client.dart`): same miss semantics as `_readDiskCatalog` (no file is null; corrupt file is logged, deleted and null; an empty parsed list is still a valid catalog — the deliberate all-hidden file, §45.3); `fetchedAt` comes from `File.stat`. The 9 catalog call sites switch to it (the three `_load*Cached` loaders, the three `_rowsForEnterSearch` cold fallbacks, the three `_warm*CatalogFromDisk` warm-ups); the 6 category loads stay body-based (small). `IndexWriteParams` gains nullable `catalogFilePath` / `categoryFilePath` (`lib/services/iptv/iptv_search_index.dart`); `writeIndexTypeInBackground` reads the files itself when paths are set (the network sync keeps passing bodies). `_seedSearchIndexFromDisk` now passes paths + the file mtime as `syncStampMs` (same stamp semantics as the read's `fetchedAt`).
- 47.2 (item 3, serialize + defer) — NEW `IptvClient.warmUpCatalogsSequentially({bool waitForFirstFrame = true})` (`lib/media/iptv_client.dart`): live warms first (the guide needs it), then the first frame (`SchedulerBinding.instance.endOfFrame`, 500ms timeout so unit tests without a frame loop cannot hang), then VOD + series only when RSS leaves room, then it returns. The RSS gate is half the RSS-watchdog threshold (45% of total RAM, clamped 512MB–1.5GB — the same scale `main.dart` evicts at): ~460MB on a 2GB stick, ~768MB on the Fold. A pressured device logs `IPTV: bind warm-up deferred VOD/series` and loads each type on demand from disk instead (the cold fallback). Test-visible `bindPhaseLog` records the phases in order (`live`, `first-frame`, `vod`, `series` — or `deferred-memory`); test-visible `debugBindWarmRssBudgetBytes` forces the gate. The public `warmUp*Catalog` wrappers stay as-is for their other callers; the sequential method uses the private disk warm-ups directly so it does not re-trigger the seed. NEW `MultiServerManager._bindIptvCatalogs` (`lib/services/multi_server_manager.dart`): the sequential warm-up, then `ensureSearchIndexSynced` (seed + sync) — one chain, never concurrent; a warm-up failure still reaches the sync. `addIptvConnection` replaces the three concurrent warm-ups + concurrent sync with `unawaited(_bindIptvCatalogs(client))`. The prune switch default is UNCHANGED and the two-modes setting is NOT built here (later small build).
- 47.3 (skip-before-parse) — `_parseVisibleList` (`lib/services/iptv/xtream_api.dart`) now checks the category id off the raw map (`json['category_id']?.toString()`) BEFORE parsing the row: all three models carry `category_id?.toString()` with no transformation, so the outcome is identical and the ~77% of rows in hidden categories never become typed objects. The post-parse check stays as a safety net. Measured effect is small (the transient is the `jsonDecode` itself, see 47.5) but it is free and it also shrinks the encode path, which shares the function.
- 47.4 (debug log export, the owner ~2026-11-13 16:25 EST) — NEW `lib/services/debug_log_export_service.dart`: `buildDebugText({required String baseLogsText})` appends `== Settings ==` (every portable setting from `SettingsExportService.buildExportMap`, rendered as sorted `key: value (type)` lines), `== Memory ==` (RSS now/max, device total RAM, low-end flag) and `== Last crash ==` (`LastCrashStore.read()` or `none recorded`) to the Logs screen's formatted text (which already carries app version + device + startup record + logs, errors and stack traces). Privacy, three layers: the settings come only from the closed portable registry (endpoints and credentials are never in it), the renderer drops `isSensitivePrefKey` keys and renders any URL-looking value as `[URL withheld]`, and the whole file goes through `LogRedactionManager.redact` before it is written. `exportToFile` mirrors `SettingsExportService.exportToFile` (file picker, Android TV falls back to the app docs dir); file name `pleztv-debug-YYYYMMDD-HHMMSS.txt`. NEW Logs action (`lib/screens/settings/logs_screen.dart`): a download action next to Upload/Copy, same `_hasDiagnostics` gate, success/error snackbars. NEW i18n: `logs.exportDebugFile` / `logs.exportDebugDialogTitle`, `messages.debugLogExported` / `messages.debugLogExportFailed`.
- 47.5 (tests + numbers) — NEW `test/services/iptv/iptv_catalog_bind_1_2_6_test.dart` (7 tests): M1 (sequential warm-up holds visible rows only: VOD 39,169 / series 8,852 / live 10,677, hiddenMatches 0), M2 (bind chain peak), M3 (index serves a paged category query from disk; hidden category now correctly 0 rows — the 1.2.1-era probe expected >0, before the 1.2.4 prune), item-4 file/body parser parity on all three types + `filePath` miss/disable semantics, item-3 ordering (live → first-frame → VOD → series) + the RSS gate (budget 0 → `deferred-memory`, search still serves visible rows from disk). NEW `test/services/debug_log_export_service_test.dart` (5 tests): settings rendering + sort order, sensitive-key drop + URL withholding, file-name shape, memory section, full text keeps the sections and redacts a registered panel URL + a `password:` value. M2 NUMBERS (Trex-scale fixture, 50ms RSS sampler): 1.2.5 base peak delta 279MB in-suite / **512MB standalone** (absolute peak 839MB, steady-state growth +81MB); 1.2.6 fix **180–251MB** across runs (standalone 211/217/251MB, in-suite 180MB; absolute peak 635–691MB, steady-state growth +26MB) — a ~59% cut. The bound is 300MB: the number varies with heap/GC timing in the shared test process, and the remaining transient is the `jsonDecode` of the 40MB VOD body itself — the floor for any parse of that file. Going below 200MB needs item 6 (disk-paged browse: never parse the whole VOD file) or a streaming JSON parser. Device projection for a 2GB stick: engine floor ~200–250MB + ~211MB bind spike + ~26MB memos ≈ ~490MB peak vs the ~900MB watchdog threshold, steady state ~350–450MB vs the 400–550MB target band — the bind fits, but the phone is the proof.
- Version: 1.2.6-2.25.0+200. Changelog `about.changelog2292`. UNPROVEN (phone-only): the bind peak / Fold RSS after the fix; the first-frame deferral on a real slow startup; the RSS gate firing on a real pressured 2GB stick; the debug file on his phone (contents + the picker on TV).

## 48. Two modes — Low RAM (default) / Instant search (opt-in) (v1.2.7-2.25.0, versionCode 201)

- Why: the owner, 2026-11-13 ~14:19 EST — "Low RAM" ON by default on first install, "Instant search" opt-in with a disclaimer that it uses more RAM and a larger cache file. Low RAM = 1.2.4 behaviour (visible-only memos, pruned disk cache); Instant search = the full catalog. The mode flips the ONE prune switch, `IptvClient.pruneHiddenCatalogRows` (static, default true since 1.2.4) — the 1.2.4 comment at the switch already described this build.
- 48.1 (mode API) — NEW `IptvClient.catalogModeLowRam` / `catalogModeInstantSearch` (`'lowRam'` / `'instantSearch'`) + `applyCatalogMode(String mode)` (`lib/media/iptv_client.dart`): sets the prune switch from the stored mode; unknown values fall back to Low RAM (pruning on) so a corrupt or future value never silently switches the app to the full catalog. The DISPLAY filter stays on the raw hidden sets in both modes — hidden categories stay hidden on screen; the switch only decides what is STORED.
- 48.2 (setting + startup) — NEW `SettingsService.iptvCatalogMode = StringPref('iptv_catalog_mode', defaultValue: 'lowRam')` (`lib/services/settings_service.dart`, in `_portableOnlyPrefs` so it rides settings export/import and appears in the debug export); the literal is duplicated there because settings_service cannot import the client (import cycle). `main.dart` `_initializeStartup` applies the stored mode before any IPTV client can bind — an existing install with no stored mode reads the Low RAM default, matching the 1.2.4 behaviour it already has.
- 48.3 (settings row + switch) — NEW `SettingSelectionTile<String>` in the Playlist group (`lib/screens/settings/iptv_settings_screen.dart`): title `iptv.catalogMode`, subtitle is the picked mode's description (the Instant search description IS the disclaimer: more memory + a larger cache file; the same text rides the option subtitle in the picker dialog). `onAfterWrite` calls `_applyCatalogMode`: picking the already-active mode is a no-op (no re-sync for a re-pick); otherwise it flips the switch and re-syncs every Xtream catalog in the reused `_CatalogSyncDialog` (the same forced `syncSearchIndex` "Sync catalog now" runs, per-type progress, Cancel stops it, dismissing leaves it running) — the forced re-sync is what makes the memos, disk cache and search index agree under the new mode (the 1.2.4 T5 proof, both directions). No Xtream connections: a snackbar says the mode is saved and applies on the next sync. M3U playlists keep their raw cached text in both modes (the prune governs the Xtream catalog stores); `syncSearchIndex` is a no-op for M3U.
- 48.4 (T4b, 1.2.6 tester follow-up) — `_renderSettingValue` (`lib/services/debug_log_export_service.dart`) now maps List elements through the same `contains('://')` → `[URL withheld]` check as scalar values: a list of server addresses is still a list of server addresses.
- 48.5 (tests) — NEW `test/services/iptv/iptv_catalog_modes_1_2_7_test.dart` (5 tests): the pref defaults to Low RAM on a fresh install; an existing install with no stored mode stays Low RAM; a stored Instant search mode turns pruning off; an unknown stored value falls back to Low RAM; the switch test (T5-shaped, driven through the mode API): Low RAM sync → disk 2 / index 2 / AR movie unresolvable / memo probe 2 rows 0 hidden; Instant switch + forced re-sync → disk 3 / index 3 / AR movie resolves / memo probe 3 rows 1 hidden (scanned but hidden on screen); back to Low RAM + re-sync → 2 / 2 / unresolvable / memo 2 rows 0 hidden. The memo probe is a fresh client on the same disk with the index disabled, so it re-derives the memos from disk under the current mode. NEW T4b test in `test/services/debug_log_export_service_test.dart`: a `url_list` holding a panel URL + `'EN'` withholds the URL and keeps `'EN'`.
- Version: 1.2.7-2.25.0+201. Changelog `about.changelog2293`. UNPROVEN (phone-only): the mode row on his phone; a real switch to Instant search restoring the hidden rows; a real switch back pruning them; About 1.2.7-2.25.0; the updater offering it and not re-offering after install.

## 49. Guide round 2 — EPG matching follow-up (v1.2.8-2.25.0, versionCode 202)

- Why: the owner's 1.2.5 phone verdict (2026-11-13 ~21:09 EST): guide 0 → 3,321 of 26,151 with guide (manual 0, tvg-id 2,816, name 502, loose name 3; no match — tvg-id not in feed 21,211, matched but no programmes 527, foreign 431, no tvg-id 412, ambiguous 249). Only 3,273 of 26,151 carry a non-empty `epg_channel_id`. RESEARCH_EPG_MATCHING_ROUND2.md: the 21,211 bucket is ≥97.9% EMPTY `epg_channel_id`, not a namespace mismatch. Honest ceiling ~4,000/26,151 (~15%) feed-only; ~4,500–6,000 (~17–23%) with short-EPG + one US community source. The guide will never be "full" — never claim otherwise.
- 49.1 (F1, empty-string ids are absent) — NEW `flexibleNonEmptyString` (`lib/utils/json_utils.dart`): `flexibleString` with `""` folded to null. `XtreamLiveStream.fromJson` (`lib/services/iptv/xtream_api.dart`) reads `epg_channel_id` through it; `parseM3u` (`lib/services/iptv/m3u_parser.dart`) folds an empty `tvg-id=""` to null the same way. The Reload `no-tvg-id` bucket now means "no usable id (null or empty)"; `tvg-id-not-in-feed` is real ids only. One-time side effect: the channel-list fingerprint changes for panels with empty ids (one EPG refresh on first load after the update).
- 49.2 (F2, "matched but no programmes" split) — `_matchInGuide` (`lib/media/iptv_live_tv.dart`) now returns `matched-but-no-programmes-declared` (the feed declares the id with zero programmes) vs `matched-but-no-programmes-outside-window` (ANY identified id has retained programmes, all outside the Reload window). `IptvEpgMatchTiers` gains `matchedButNoProgrammesDeclared` / `matchedButNoProgrammesOutsideWindow` (they sum to `matchedButNoProgrammes`); the Reload dialog renders the split (`epgReloadNoProgrammesSplit`) and the result carries `nearestOutsideWindowProgramme` (nearest retained start to now across the outside-window rows, computed in the Reload probe from the first loaded feed's guide — no signature change to `_matchInGuide`; Logs line only, not the dialog). The overview/details keep the single `matchedButNoPrograms` reason — both new codes map to it in `channelsWithoutGuide` / `channelEpgDetail`.
- 49.3 (F3, matches over ALL channels) — NEW `IptvClient.fullLiveChannelsFromLastRefresh` (`lib/media/iptv_client.dart`): `refreshLiveChannelsFromNetwork` captures the FULL live list (hidden rows included) and resolves the key maps (`_xtreamChannelByKey` / `_m3uChannelByKey`) for ALL rows, so Reload can count matches over hidden channels. The published list, disk file and live memo still hold visible rows only (the 1.2.7 Low-RAM invariant); the field is set only by a refresh (Reload is its only caller) and cleared on a failed refresh. Retained cost ~10MB at Trex scale until the next refresh — a deliberate, documented cost of the Reload report, not of normal browsing. Reload resolves the full list into `withGuideAllChannels` / `channelsLoadedAll` on the result; the dialog renders "All channels (including hidden): X with guide of Y" (`epgReloadAllChannels`) and the per-source feed stats line now shows the programme-carrying count next to the declared count (`epgReloadFeedStats` gains `withProgrammes` = `outcome.feedChannels`). `iptvChannels()` now builds rows through shared `_liveTvChannelForStream` / `_liveTvChannelForM3uEntry` helpers so the visible and full lists are constructed identically. M3U full-list capture is implemented symmetrically in the same method (Xtream-tested; the M3U path has no test coverage).
- 49.4 (F4, zero-padded number folding) — `_epgNameWords` (`lib/media/iptv_live_tv.dart`) folds an all-digit word with a leading zero (`01`→`1`, `007`→`7`; `101` untouched; `0`/`00` stay `0`), applied to both keys (strict and loose) so `AR| BEIN SPORTS 01 FHD` meets feed `beIN Sports 1` by name.
