- Why: the owner's Hisense SmartTV 4K FFM (2.4GiB RAM, lowRam:false, Android 12 API 31, PlezTV 1.2.13+207), photos 2026-11-14 ~20:02 EST — three findings in one evening: (a) the Logs screen's Export / Upload / Copy / Clear rows will not highlight or select with the TV remote; (b) Last crash `[uncaught] OutOfMemoryError` in `ByteStream.toBytes` during the Discover hub load (startup RSS ~499MB), and the second run's home stayed EMPTY (VOD + series hub fetches both `connectionTimeout` from `XtreamApi._fetchBody`); (c) the app crashed ~4 more times and the Emergency Debug Log Saver never appeared. Research: `~/workspace/tmp_work/plezy-tv-research-tv-logs-focus/RESEARCH_TV_LOGS_FOCUS_OOM.md` (read-only; no panel probing — login purged, standing ban). The ANR on the run before is the same cause wearing an Android label (main thread stalled under allocation pressure at main_screen, RSS ~522MB); C1 memory stays the lead.
- 57.1 (Logs D-pad focus) — `lib/screens/settings/logs_screen.dart`: the screen's root `Focus` consumed every arrow key to scroll the log list (`_scroll(80)`, returning `handled`) before the event reached the app-level `Shortcuts` → `DirectionalFocusIntent`, so focus stayed pinned on the app-bar back button — the only settings screen that intercepts arrow keys (every other one returns `ignored` for Up/Down). Fix, per the research §1 plan: the arrow branches now scroll ONLY when the primary focus is null or the log-body selection node (`_selectionFocusNode`); when focus is on a control they return `ignored`, so the standard `Shortcuts` traversal moves focus between rows. Belt-and-braces: the app bar's `FocusableActionBar` gets `onNavigateDown: () => _exportFocusNode.requestFocus()` and the Export tile gets `focusNode: _exportFocusNode`. The `SelectionArea` behaviour is unchanged (`_CollapsingSelectionMoveAction` already passes collapsing moves through). Tests (in the existing `app-bar D-pad traversal` group of `test/screens/settings/logs_screen_test.dart`, TV forced on): F1 ArrowDown walks focus back button → Export → Upload → Copy → Clear, past Clear stays on Clear, ArrowUp walks back (seed a log line first — Upload/Copy/Clear are disabled without diagnostics, and a disabled `ListTile` cannot take focus); F2 Enter on the focused Export row runs the export (TV off, fake `FilePickerService` delegate records the call; fileName starts with `pleztv-debug-`, extensions `['txt']`, ~8 [runAsync 100ms + pump] rounds for the real-async export chain); F3 arrows still scroll the log body while focus is on the selection node (120 seeded lines, offset 0 → 80 → 0). Failing-first: on the base F1/F2 fail (focus never leaves the back button); F3 passes on the base (guards the gate against regressing desktop keyboard scrolling).
- 57.2 (saver: ANY crash counts, TEMPORARY) — the owner ~2026-11-14 20:08 EST: the trigger is now any crash, not just startup crashes — three crashes, no matter what or when, and the app offers the debug option. The rule will be narrowed back later when crashes are less of an issue. Root cause (research §4a, proven in code): the good-start marker is written when the first catalog bind settles (`markGoodStartWhenStable`, MainScreen first post-frame callback), but the TV's OOM happens in the Discover hub load AFTER that — the hub load is not part of any bind chain — so every crash looked like a good start and `_updateFailedStartCounterSync` reset the counter to 0. Fix (`lib/services/last_crash_store.dart`): the `hadMarker → 0` short-circuit is gone — `count = previousAbnormal ? _readCounterSync(directory) + 1 : _readCounterSync(directory)` (cumulative since ~21:08 EST — a clean exit leaves the count untouched) — and the marker is no longer even consumed there (still written by `markGoodStart`, so narrowing back later is a one-condition revert). Abnormal-exit detection is unchanged (`markLaunched` reads `last_exit_state.txt`; Dart uncaught/OOM, native crash, LMK kill, ANR kill all count; only the saver flow resets (save or Continue startup). Cumulative: a clean exit in between leaves the count untouched (the owner ~2026-11-14 ~21:08 EST: "Any 3 crashes, ever"). Copy change (research §4b — the old wording is wrong under the new rule): `emergencyDebugLogSaver.body` is now "PlezTV has crashed several times. …" (was "failed to start several times in a row"; the "in a row" went with the cumulative rule, the owner ~21:08 EST); the title, buttons, and the dump-summary "Failed starts: N" line are unchanged. Comments updated (`main.dart` `_bootstrapApp` + `_recordLaunchAndCountFailedStarts` doc, `_runAutoDumpSafeMode` doc). Tests (`test/services/auto_dump_test.dart`): `goodLaunch()` now models stable + CLEAN exit; NEW `stableThenKilledLaunch()` (stable, marker written, dies with no clean exit — the TV OOM shape); T-A2 rewritten (2 failed + stable-then-killed → next launch counts 3 → saver; on the base it returns 0); NEW T-A8 (3 stable-then-killed in a row → 0,1,2,3 → saver); T-A9 rewritten (cumulative: a clean exit does NOT reset the counter — 2 crashes, clean exit, next crash → 3 → saver). The 1.2.13 W1 widget test's exact-copy literal is updated to the new body.
- 57.3 (hub-load streaming — the OOM root cause) — `lib/services/iptv/xtream_api.dart` + `lib/media/iptv_client.dart`: `_fetchBody` buffered the whole catalog body (`http.Response.fromStream` → `ByteStream.toBytes` → one giant `Uint8List`), then the body was decoded to a `String` (copy 2), retained in `lastVodStreamsBody` (copy 3), and copied across the isolate port for parsing (copy 4) — and the 300MB cap was checked AFTER buffering. The 1.2.10 file-streaming path (`_fetchBodyToFile` + `get*StreamsToFile`) was only used by the sync, never by the hub path. Fix: the unfiltered `getVodStreams` / `getSeries` now stream straight to a fresh temp file (`_newHubTempFile`, sync `createTempSync` — a mkdir is a metadata op and the load must also work under `testWidgets`' fake async) via `getVodStreamsToFile` / `getSeriesToFile`, then parse from the file in the isolate via NEW `parseVodStreamsFile` / `parseSeriesFile` (unfiltered — the first-load coverage guard needs FULL rows; hidden categories count as covered, FORK §45, so the visible file parse cannot serve it). The main isolate never holds the body; the oversize cap is now enforced on the streamed bytes by `_fetchBodyToFile`, and a slow-but-delivering connection is not cut off at 120s total (per-chunk-gap timeout instead). Per-category calls are small and keep the buffered path. The temp-file path is stashed as NEW `lastVodStreamsFilePath` / `lastSeriesFilePath` (same synchronous-read rule as `last*StreamsBody`; the next unfiltered call deletes the previous temp dir first, so at most one stale file lingers — a diagnostics probe that ignores the path leaves one behind in the system temp dir). `getLiveStreams` is UNCHANGED (buffered; the sync already streams live; same fix pattern applies as a follow-up). `seriesCatalogDiagnostics` keeps calling buffered `getSeries` (bounded 10s probe, only counts rows). The disk write in `_loadVodStreamsCached` / `_loadSeriesCached` now prefers the file: NEW `_persistHubDownload` (fire-and-forget + generation-guarded like `_writeDiskText`) moves the temp file straight to the disk cache when nothing is hidden, else the isolate writes the visible-only file from it via NEW `writeVodStreamsFileVisible` / `writeSeriesFileVisible` (the build #13 main-isolate `jsonEncode` freeze is avoided in both branches); the raw-body and re-encode branches stay for test fakes and callers without a file. The temp file is deleted when the load is not persisted (partial, crawl, empty, all-hidden deliberate-empty). This breaks the §2 vicious circle: once a full load completes through the file path the disk file exists, so later cold starts serve disk instead of re-fetching. The crawl fallback (per-category buffered calls on an empty unfiltered answer) is unchanged. No test-fake churn by design: fakes override `getVodStreams` / `getSeries` and never touch the file path, so their canned rows flow through exactly as before (`last*FilePath` stays null → existing disk-write branches).
- 57.4 (hub timeout fallback) — `_loadVodStreamsCached` / `_loadSeriesCached`: a failed network load now re-reads the disk cache and serves the last-known-good rows (source label `'disk-stale'`, plus the usual background refresh-if-due) instead of an empty home — the first read may have missed a file a concurrent sync landed since. A miss on the re-read still throws. The disk-first hit path already served cached rows; the fallback covers the miss-then-land window.
- 57.5 (low-end serialization) — `lib/services/data_aggregation_service.dart`: the hub fan-out ran with `concurrency = 3`, so the VOD + series fetches ran concurrently (both TV errors stamped the same second) — two whole bodies buffered at once. Now `DevicePerformance.isLowEndHardware ? 1 : 3`; the 3-wide window stays on capable hardware. No dedicated test (a one-line gate on a flagged value; the existing aggregation bridge tests run with the flag false → concurrency 3, unchanged).
- 57.6 (tests) — NEW `test/services/iptv/iptv_hub_streaming_1_2_16_test.dart` (B1–B3, real `XtreamApi` + fake `http.BaseClient` panel, same shape as the 1.2.10 one-parse test): B1 Trex-scale VOD hub load (199,762 rows, 3 of 4 categories hidden) — `lastVodStreamsBody` stays null, hub loads, disk file lands with exactly the 49,941 visible mov1 rows, no `plezy_hub_*` temp dir survives; B2 panel lands the VOD disk file mid-fetch then throws `TimeoutException` (→ `connectionTimeout`, the TV's exact error) — the hub still serves those rows; B3 series hub load — `lastSeriesBody` stays null, disk file holds exactly the 10 visible rows (cat2's 7021, 7022 pruned). Failing-first: B1/B3 fail on the 1.2.13 base (`last*StreamsBody` set); B2 fails on the base (the load throws — no fallback re-read). The disk poll tolerates a mid-write partial read (the persist is fire-and-forget). NO RSS BOUND (same honest-negative rule as 1.2.10/1.2.12): the peak RSS during a Trex-scale load is dominated by the isolate's single parse heap in both cases — the deterministic proof is the null-body assertion + identical lands. Widget-test note: the hub load's temp-file creation and cleanup are sync I/O (`createTempSync` / `deleteSync` / `renameSync`) precisely so the path also works under `testWidgets`' fake async; `compute` file parsing runs in a real isolate. Not run yet (runner owned by the 1.2.15 bundle builder at write time) — new tests 3 (B1–B3) + 3 (F1–F3) + 3 (T-A2 rewritten, T-A8, T-A9) + W1 copy update, all written, none run.
- Version: 1.2.16-2.25.0+210. Changelog `about.changelog2302` (4 entries). UNPROVEN (phone-only): the Logs rows highlighting with the real TV remote; the TV surviving a hub load without OOM (RSS + no crash); the home loading from disk after a slow panel; the saver appearing after 3 crashes of any kind on the TV; About 1.2.16-2.25.0; the updater offering it and not re-offering after install. OPEN: the background-LMK-kill consequence of the temporary broad rule (a background kill counts, since no clean exit is written — the refinement is to consult the native previous-exit report at startup, not part of this change).

## 58. 1.2.15 phone-verdict fixes + queued hardening (v1.2.17-2.25.0, versionCode 211)

- Why: the owner's 1.2.15 phone verdict (Fold, 2026-11-15 ~00:27 EST, debug dump `pleztv-debug-20261115-002738`): guide 3,443/26,151 (tvg-id 2,815, name 605, loose 23; no-tvg-id 21,483); feed downloaded fine (88.3MB, SHA `a410c333…`, 4,418 channels with programmes, 274,931 kept); RSS 581/845MB (peaks lead — the owner 2026-11-15: judge memory by PEAKS, not average); startup 1.3s; previous exit = package update (the 1.2.15 install, not a crash). Five defects, bundled into ONE release per the owner's bundling rule, plus two queued hardening items.
- 58.1 (R2 M3U check times out) — `lib/media/iptv_live_tv.dart` `diagnoseM3uTvgIds` called `_client.xtreamApi.getM3u()` on the default 30s timeout; the whole-playlist download timed out right after the 88MB feed download, and the raw `TimeoutException` text ("Future not completed") reached the user. Fix: `lib/services/iptv/xtream_api.dart` `getM3u` now passes `timeout: 120s` to `_getText`. Leak found while fixing: `MediaServerHttpException.from` copies `TimeoutException.message` verbatim into `message`, and `epgFeedErrorReason` returned it — fixed in `lib/services/iptv/iptv_epg_sources.dart`: timeout types (`connectionTimeout`/`receiveTimeout`, plus raw `TimeoutException`) now map to `t.errors.reasonTimedOut` ("the connection timed out"). Tests (`test/services/iptv/iptv_epg_verdict_1_2_17_test.dart`): a timed-out playlist check reports plain words, never the raw timeout; `getM3u` survives a 45s-slow download (fakeAsync + MockClient).
- 58.2 (R6 "0 of 200" misleading) — `sampleGuideCoverage` samples the FIRST 200 channels and counts only already-fetched rows; on a fresh start all 200 are "never fetched", but `epgReloadGuideCoverageSample` had NO never-fetched bucket. Fix: the string now reads "Guide rows already showing programmes before this reload: ${withPrograms} of ${sampled} sampled (quick guide: ${shortEpg} · big guide: ${feed} · no data: ${none} · never fetched: ${notFetched})" and the dialog builder (`lib/screens/settings/iptv_epg_sources_screen.dart`) passes `notFetched: sample.notFetched`. Test (`iptv_epg_verdict_1_2_17_new_api_test.dart`): the rendered string names the bucket (`never fetched: 200` on a fresh-start sample).
- 58.3 (Reload dialog overflows 435px) — the Reload dialog content was a plain `Column` (mainAxisSize.min), NO scroll view; 1.2.15's added lines pushed it over. Fix: NEW public widget `ReloadReportContent` in `iptv_epg_sources_screen.dart` (`SingleChildScrollView` wrapping header + lines), used by both the Reload dialog and the M3U-check dialog. Test (`test/screens/settings/iptv_reload_report_content_1_2_17_test.dart`): header + 20 lines in a 300px box scrolls instead of throwing (position assertion — finders see off-screen widgets, so the test asserts the last line starts below the box and scrolls into it); empty-lines layout covered.
- 58.4 (feed file did not persist — tmp race) — `_xmltvTempFeedFile` built a DETERMINISTIC tmp name per connection (`feed_<key>.tmp`); two concurrent feed loads raced — first renamed tmp→xml, second hit `PathNotFoundException` at its rename (and its promote deleted the first's just-promoted file, so the next cold start re-downloaded 88MB). The race does NOT come from two Reloads: concurrent Reloads share ONE download through the `_xmltvGuideFutures` in-flight map (proven by probe). The racing path is the window slide: `_slideGuideWindow` calls `_startXmltvDownload` WITHOUT registering in the futures map, so two concurrent slides for one connection tee to one tmp. Fix: `_xmltvTempFeedFile` now builds a UNIQUE tmp per load (`feed_<safe>_<microseconds>_<seq>.tmp`, static `_xmltvTempFeedSequence`); NEW `_pruneStaleXmltvTempFiles` (best-effort, sync I/O; deletes same-connection `feed_*.tmp` files older than 1h only, never a fresh in-flight tmp) runs when a tee opens. The promote failure itself is swallowed (best-effort by design) — the test pins the file outcome, not the throw. Test (`iptv_epg_verdict_1_2_17_test.dart`): a metadata-only Reload with persistence disabled seeds the in-memory guide with no file, then two concurrent channel fetches slide their windows (each finds its channel counted but row-less and re-downloads through the unregistered slide path) — both complete and the feed file persists with both shows. On the base the loser's promote deletes the winner's file and its rename throws (swallowed), so the file is gone. Harness note: the legacy-`SharedPreferences` mock does not cover `IptvEpgSourcesStore.loadStatuses`, so the failure-recording path (`_recordSourceFailure` → `loadStatuses` → legacy `getInstance`) throws `MissingPluginException` in tests — the tmp-race test is written so no load fails and that path never runs.
- 58.5 (Live TV slow — R5 rebuild storm) — mechanism proven in code: every late short-EPG answer bumped `epgVersionNotifier` → the guide tab did a FULL `setState` → `_guideRows` regrouped all 26,151 channels (twice per build) AND the version check in `_ensureIptvEpg` busted every visible row's loaded state so all re-fetched. Fix: `_bumpEpgVersion({String? channelKey})` in `lib/media/iptv_live_tv.dart` — a null key (playback-stop, drop, clear) still increments `_epgVersion`; a channel key does NOT touch it. The notifier value is now a monotonic `_epgBumpSequence` (still `ValueNotifier<int>`); NEW getter `lastEpgBumpChannelKey`; `_storeLateShortEpg` passes its channel key. The tab listener (`lib/screens/livetv/tabs/guide_tab.dart`) reads the key: null → `setState` as before; scoped → NEW `_healIptvEpgRow` (scans `widget.channels` for the matching key + identical support, drops only that row's `_iptvEpgLoaded` / `_mergedProgramsCache` entries, re-fetches it from the support cache). `_guideRows` is memoized (`_cachedGuideRows` + `_cachedGuideRowsChannels`, identity check — the same assumption `didUpdateWidget` uses); one retained 26k-row list replaces a fresh allocation per build, so peak memory does not rise. Tests: support level (`iptv_epg_verdict_1_2_17_test.dart` R5: a 2s-late answer heals the row from the background request — `shortEpgCalls['100']` stays 1 — and `epgVersion` does not move; a `clearEpgCache` still moves it) + new-API (`iptv_epg_verdict_1_2_17_new_api_test.dart`: a channel-scoped bump names the channel, a whole-guide bump names none). Widget-level proof (GuideTab: A's late answer heals only A's row, B is never re-fetched) is UNPROVEN in-harness: the rows' feed fallback runs `_windowFeedIdsFor` → `iptvChannels()` → search-index warm with real async disk I/O, which never completes in `testWidgets` fake-async (and the fetches' futures, created in the fake zone, stay stuck even across real-time `runAsync` windows and drive calls). The support-level contract test stands instead.
- 58.6 (category-cast hardening) — `XtreamCategory.name` + `XtreamEpisode` / `XtreamEpgProgram` string casts were whole-list `.map().toList()` throw hazards (same class as the series `backdrop_path` bug that dropped 45,991 rows). Fix (`xtream_api.dart`): `flexibleString` (coerce, don't cast) in `XtreamCategory.fromJson` (name), `XtreamEpisode.fromJson` (title, containerExtension, plot), `XtreamEpgProgram.fromJson` (title, description via `_decodeBase64Text`); NEW static `_parseCatalogList` (`parseFlexibleJsonListCounted` + `appLogger.w` when rows drop), used at `getLiveCategories` / `getVodCategories` / `getSeriesCategories` (via `_asObjectList`), `getShortEpg` listings (`listingMaps.toList()` — do NOT pass raw `listings`, the Map case needs `.values`), `parseCategoriesBody`, and `XtreamSeriesInfo` episodes. Tests: a numeric `category_name` is coerced (`'42'`), the list survives; a mistyped episode field is coerced, the season survives; a mistyped EPG title is coerced (`'12345'`), the answer survives (list-level, fakeAsync + MockClient).
- 58.7 (first-load-throw hardening) — uncaught throws with no disk cache + a failing panel (fresh install). Fix: `startPlayback` (`iptv_live_tv.dart`: `iptvChannels()` wrapped in try/catch → return null + log; the caller already handles null); `IptvEpgNoGuideScreen._load` (plain message via NEW string `epgNoGuideLoadFailed` + Retry button — `epgReloadUnexpectedFailure` was the wrong string for this screen); `itemNeedsCatalogRefresh` (`lib/media/iptv_client.dart`: `fetchItem` wrapped → return false + log; the notice is a convenience, fail closed). Verified already guarded, no change: `fetchChannels` via `forEachLiveTvServer` (body errors → `onError` → plain message + error state/snackbar); resume list and favourites resolver already try/catch `fetchItem` + snapshot fallback; `search_screen.dart` updateItem and `title_copies.dart` already guarded. Tests: `startPlayback` returns null when the channel list cannot load; the refresh check fails closed when the catalog cannot load.
- 58.8 (testability) — `_readPersistedEpg` now returns null immediately when `debugDisablePersistedEpg` is set, like every other persisted path (`_readPersistedXmltv`, `_pruneStalePersistedEpg`, `_xmltvFeedFileSize`, `_persistedEpgChannelKeys`, `_saveFeedValidators` guard, feed-tee guard). Without it the awaited real async file check never completes in `testWidgets` fake-async and a widget-test fetch hangs before short-EPG. Production behaviour unchanged (flag defaults false).
- Version: 1.2.17-2.25.0+211. Changelog `about.changelog2303` (7 entries). Tests: NEW `test/services/iptv/iptv_epg_verdict_1_2_17_test.dart` (9 tests) + `test/services/iptv/iptv_epg_verdict_1_2_17_new_api_test.dart` (2 tests — does NOT compile on base: `lastEpgBumpChannelKey` + the R6 `notFetched` parameter are new) + `test/screens/settings/iptv_reload_report_content_1_2_17_test.dart` (2 tests — does NOT compile on base: `ReloadReportContent` is new). Failing-first: File A compiles on base `fe6e62b86` and fails 0/9 there (every pinned behaviour absent), passes 9/9 on the fix tree. Full iptv suite (`test/services/iptv/`, 659 tests = 648 on 1.2.16 + 9 + 2 new): 657/659 in one run; the 2 failures (`iptv_build13_diagnosis_test.dart` B2 slow-load repro, `iptv_build_r2_scorer_test.dart` bench-median <1000ms) both pass standalone (9/9, 7/7) — timing flakes under parallel-shard load, not regressions (the bench is a wall-clock median; neither test's code path is slowed by this change). Full `flutter analyze`: 53 issues = base 53, zero issues in touched files (one `prefer_is_empty` info in the new test file was found and fixed; re-verified at 53). UNPROVEN (phone-only): cold-open loading; feed data arriving in ~15–40s; R6 coverage on a real fresh start; R2 M3U check against the real panel; About 1.2.17-2.25.0; the updater offering it and not re-offering after install; the Live TV scroll/loading feel on the Fold (the R5 fix is proven at support level only — see 58.5); the feed file persisting across a real cold start (no 88MB re-download).

## 59. Round-2 memory + CPU build — all 31 fixes, build-all decision (v1.2.18-2.25.0, versionCode 212)

- Why: the owner's decision 2026-11-15 ~03:41 EST — build ALL 31 fixes from the round-2 research report (`~/workspace/tmp_work/plezy-tv-research-memory-cpu-round2/RESEARCH_MEMORY_CPU_ROUND2.md`, 473 lines; S-1–S-16 spike/peak + C-1–C-15 CPU). Judge memory by PEAKS (max RSS), not average — kills happen at peaks; no fix may raise any peak; no fix may move work onto the main isolate or add per-frame work. 17 BUILT, 9 PARTIAL (built part + documented remainder), 5 NOT BUILT with reason. Kotlin changes + Kotlin unit tests are UNPROVEN in-harness (no Android SDK locally; local Gradle build blocked — cloud build is the route).
- 59.1 (S-1 feed parse off the main isolate) — BUILT. The ~25 s main-isolate XMLTV parse per feed load/slide moved to a worker: top-level `_XmltvFeedParseRequest` + `_parseXmltvFeedFile` before `class IptvLiveTvSupport` (`lib/media/iptv_live_tv.dart:636`), static `_parseFeedFile`, test flag `debugDisableIsolateFeedParse`. `_loadXmltvGuide` restructured: download + tee to temp file + SHA on main, isolate-parse the temp file BEFORE promoting it (failed parse deletes temp, keeps old persisted feed, records `_xmltvFailedAt` unless cancellation). `_readPersistedXmltv` calls `_parseFeedFile` with `await` (load-bearing). Parse path verified translation-free.
- 59.2 (S-2 stop the sync memo publish) — BUILT. Isolate job `writeIndexTypeInBackground` (`lib/services/iptv/iptv_search_index.dart`) writes the visible disk file incrementally (one row's JSON at a time through a temp file renamed into place) and returns only counts. `IndexWriteResult.visibleRowsJson` removed; `_typedRowsFromJson` deleted from `iptv_client.dart`. `_publishSyncedCatalog` rewritten as `_refreshCatalogAfterSync(type, categories)`. Reload path untouched. This is R-2's first step (round-1 report §6), not a duplicate. BUG FOUND AND FIXED DURING VERIFICATION: the first form wrote one `sink.write` per row — ~200k separate drain entries the sink drains one per event-loop turn, ~33 s at Trex scale (199,762 VOD rows) for the disk write alone, a 4x sync slowdown vs the old one-shot write. It broke the build13 Trex-scale bench (the movie sync exceeded the 30 s test timeout: second-launch, disk-read-only, and search-wrapper tests failed; the C-5 seed kick also surfaced an unhandled `MissingPluginException` from `_runIndexWrite` in the search-wrapper test — see 59.21). Fixed by chunking the write (2048 rows ≈ 300 KB per chunk, `await sink.flush()` per chunk): streams at ~1 s with one chunk resident, so the no-whole-body memory win stands and the bytes are identical to `jsonEncode(visibleRowsJson)`. Bench file green again (5/6 standalone; the 1 failure is the first-launch wall-clock bound, 517 ms vs 400 ms on the throttled VM — base fails the same test at 3767 ms).
- 59.3 (S-3 serialize heavy jobs) — PARTIAL. New `lib/services/iptv/iptv_heavy_work_gate.dart` — global FIFO priority mutex (`IptvHeavyWorkPriority.guide` > `.sync`; FIFO within a priority; no queue timeout by design; deadlock-free by construction). Wraps: sync isolate job, index seed, three M3U `compute(parseM3u, …)` calls, feed parse (`_parseFeedFile`, guide priority). NOT gated (out of scope): hub loads, Plex sync, updater, health probe, resume chain.
- 59.4 (S-4 RSS watchdog + pre-emptive trim + telemetry) — BUILT. (a) Watchdog in `lib/main.dart` changed from fixed `Timer.periodic` to self-rescheduling one-shot timer — 5 s poll while `IptvHeavyWorkGate.instance.isBusy`, else normal period (30 s / 15 s low-end). (b) Pre-emptive trim via `onStart` hook: `IptvHeavyWorkGate.trimImageCacheBeforeHeavyJob()` (clears only non-live image cache when RSS > half watchdog threshold) + `IptvLiveTvSupport.trimEpgCacheBeyondWindow()` (evicts oldest half of `_epgCache`, cap 64 → target 32) — CORRECTION (1.2.18 fix agent): the EPG half is UNCONDITIONAL (runs on every heavy-job start/post-parse regardless of RSS), unlike the image half; the "when hot" name covers the image half only. Adding an RSS gate to the EPG half was NOT done: it would change the steady-state cache bound (64 instead of 32 between hot periods), which is a behaviour change, not a wording fix. Hooked into both `_parseFeedFile` callers and sync/seed/M3U gate runs. (c) Telemetry: `rssMbAfter` + IPTV stats in `lib/utils/memory_pressure_breadcrumbs.dart` (new static `iptvMemoryStatsProvider`; provider registered in `main.dart`) and in the watchdog log line. `MultiServerManager.iptvMemoryStats()` aggregates; `IptvClient.memoryStats` returns a record; support gained production getters `epgCacheEntries` / `retainedFeedPrograms`. Deviation: memo *bytes* are not reported — no byte counter exists, so loaded flags are the honest proxy; the RSS-after number is the proof.
- 59.5 (S-5 dedupe accumulator) — BUILT. `_XmltvGuideAccumulator._seen` is `Set<int>` of `Object.hash(channelId, startEpoch)` (`lib/media/iptv_live_tv.dart:~5300`).
- 59.6 (S-6 slide coalescing) — BUILT. Slide coalescing via `_xmltvSlideFutures` map (`:830`), `_guideCoversIds` static helper (`:1857`), `_slideGuideWindow` restructured (`:1870`) with `_slideGuideWindowLocked`. A second slide joins the first only when it covers the same ids; otherwise it waits then slides after (serialized, never concurrent). Do NOT join in-flight downloads. TEST UPDATED FOR THE NEW CONTRACT: the 1.2.17 tmp-race test (`iptv_epg_verdict_1_2_17_test.dart`) required both slides to download the feed CONCURRENTLY (`expect(feedControllers.length, 2)`); under S-6 serialization only one download is ever in flight, so the test was rewritten as "Feed slides serialize (1.2.18 S-6)" — the second slide must NOT start a download while the first is in flight, both slides complete without error, and the persisted file contains both shows (the second slide either re-downloads after the first or re-parses the just-persisted file). The 1.2.17 unique-tmp-name fix stays as defense.
- 59.7 (S-7 scroll-aware image pausing) — BUILT (`lib/widgets/optimized_media_image.dart`). New `_ScrollPausedImage` StatefulWidget wrapping the two image branches in `_buildResolved` (outside `blurArtwork`). Subscribes to nearest scrollable's `position.isScrollingNotifier` via `Scrollable.maybeOf(context)` in `didChangeDependencies`. While scrolling, a tile that has never shown its image holds the placeholder; on settle the real image builds and decodes. A tile that already showed its image (`_hasShown` flag) is never gated again — no flicker. Images outside any scrollable behave exactly as before.
- 59.8 (S-8 SQLite cache + post-spike trim) — BUILT. (a) `PRAGMA cache_size = -4096;` (4 MB per connection) added after `busy_timeout` in both `IptvSearchIndex._open()` and the background-isolate open in `writeIndexTypeInBackground` (`lib/services/iptv/iptv_search_index.dart`). (b) Post-spike trim after a feed parse: `_trimCachesBeforeHeavyJob` renamed to `_trimCachesIfHot(String job, {bool after = false})` in both `iptv_live_tv.dart` and `iptv_client.dart` (7 call sites); post-parse trims added at both feed-parse completion sites in `iptv_live_tv.dart`. Note (1.2.18 fix agent): despite the `IfHot` name, the EPG half-trim is unconditional — only the image-cache half is RSS-gated (see §59.4 correction).
- 59.9 (S-9 playback pre-emption mid-type) — NOT BUILT. Requires splitting the VOD/series isolate job into per-category chunks with per-chunk partial-guard accounting; the playback gate (`_waitForIdleUnlessAborted`, `iptv_client.dart:1420+`) pauses only between types. The per-type guard is the protection against the panel's daily partial lists (a partial-kept type blocks the catalog repair — see the comment at :1420), and reworking it mid-build without device proof risks lost rows or endless re-sync. The gate already bails between types, and S-3's serialization already prevents foreground overlaps. The 100–250 MB saving is real but rarer (background sync + foreground TV playback overlapping) and device-only to prove.
- 59.10 (S-10 sequence the playback-start spike) — PARTIAL. (a) Dart trim-before-open BUILT: new public `IptvClient.trimCachesIfHot(String job, {bool after = false})` delegating to private `_trimCachesIfHot`, new public `MultiServerManager.trimIptvCachesIfHot()` looping every connection, called before the route push in both video-open choke points — `navigateToVideoPlayer` (`lib/utils/video_player_navigation.dart`) and `navigateToLiveTv` (`lib/utils/live_tv_player_navigation.dart`). Same trim as the heavy-job trim (image half RSS-gated, EPG half unconditional — see §59.4 correction); gentler than `dropIptvCachesFromMemory` (nothing dropped outright). Covers offline playback too. (b) Kotlin start-reduced + widen-after-first-frame NOT BUILT: the existing restore poll in `MpvPlayerCore.kt` runs every 30 s (`RESTORE_POLL_MS`) with a 60 s quiet window (`RESTORE_QUIET_MS`) after the latest narrowing — far too slow for a first-frame widen — so a new fast-widen path plus a first-frame trigger would be needed, carrying #2314-class rebuffer risk. No in-harness proof exists (needs 4K direct-play device measurement).
- 59.11 (S-11 ApiCache caps) — PARTIAL. (a) Cap BUILT (`lib/services/api_cache.dart`): Drift table `api_cache` had no row-count, byte, or age cap. New public constants `maxVolatileRows = 500`, `maxVolatileBytes = 64 MB`, `volatileTtl = 7 days`; `put()` now calls best-effort `_enforceCaps()` after the upsert (failures swallowed with `appLogger.w`, never fail the put): expired unpinned rows deleted first, then oldest unpinned rows until both caps hold — rows are oldest-first so a single prefix cut satisfies both (reads only keys + `LENGTH(data)` via `customSelect`, never the blobs). Pinned rows (offline metadata) exempt from every cap. Read behaviour unchanged (`get` still serves any-age rows as the offline fallback). (b) "Never cache a concatenated whole-list response" NOT BUILT: `jellyfin_client/parts/browse.dart:961` (`fetchChildren`) and `:1191` (`fetchFolderChildren`) cache the whole drained list, and `media_server_client.dart:1125` (`fetchWithCacheFirst`) caches entire hub/detail responses — but the whole-list cache IS the offline fallback for those lists, so deleting the put breaks offline browsing of seasons/folders; the safe form (cache per page + reassemble on read) is a bigger refactor of the fetch read paths. The (a) cap bounds the damage instead.
- 59.12 (S-12 BIF + trickplay + ExoPlayer) — PARTIAL. (a) BIF cap BUILT (`lib/services/bif_thumbnail_service.dart`): `_maxFileBytes` now RAM-aware via `DevicePerformance.totalMemBytes`: 16 MB on ≤4 GiB RAM, else 50 MB mobile / 200 MB desktop. Pure testable function `@visibleForTesting static int maxFileBytesFor({required int? totalMemBytes, required bool isDesktop})`. (b) Trickplay decode cap NOT CHANGED — investigated, already done at render: `timeline_slider.dart` `_ScrubFrameView` already decodes sheets at tooltip size × DPR via `ResizeImage.resizeIfNeeded(_cacheDimension(sheetW, devicePixelRatio), …)`; adding `memCacheWidth/Height` to the `CachedNetworkImageProvider` would CONFLICT with the render-time `ResizeImage` (SDK asserts). (c) ExoPlayer tightening (`LoadControlPolicy.kt`) NOT BUILT — needs a 4K direct-play regression test proving no rebuffer regression (#2314 risk); no such test can be written in-harness.
- 59.13 (S-13 dispose offscreen tabs) — NOT BUILT. LRU tab retention (cap 2–3 mounted tabs) is MEDIUM risk: disposing a tab loses scroll position, guide window, and search text unless preserved via providers, and TV D-pad focus state is the sharp edge (a disposed tab's focus tree is gone on revisit, and there is no harness proof for focus restore). The saving is steady-state retention (15–45 MB estimated), not a peak — the peak work is already covered by S-1–S-4. `main_screen.dart:1183–1187` IndexedStack and `_mountedTabs` (only ever added to) are unchanged.
- 59.14 (S-14 lazy pinned-metadata hydration) — PARTIAL. Bulk pinned decode removed, full deferral declined. (1) New `DownloadManagerService.getPinnedMetadataScopes({activeProfileId})` (`lib/services/download_manager_service.dart`) returning only the per-server profile scopes (shared owner-server resolution extracted into private `_ownedServerIds`, used by both it and `getAllPinnedMetadata`; the bulk method itself kept unchanged for its two tests). (2) Provider init (`_loadPersistedDownloads`, `lib/providers/download_provider.dart:439`) and `refreshMetadataFromCache` (`:2298`) now pass `(items: const <String, MediaItem>{}, scopesByServer: scopes)` — per-item `lookupMetadata` (the existing fallback in `_hydrateDownloadMetadata`) resolves each owned download's row, bounded by download count, not pinned-row count. (3) `_loadDownloadCollections` gains a per-collection `lookupMetadata` fallback when the (now empty) pinned map misses. (4) New `_hydrateMissingParents` helper: after `_loadParentMetadataFromMap`, resolves missing show/season (or artist/album) parent rows per item via `lookupMetadata` so stored container metadata (year/summary/clearLogo) still loads. NOT done: "decode metadata per item on first Downloads open" (full deferral) — would break the deliberate post-launch population of sync-rule titles + downloads list (`refreshMetadataFromCache` runs at startup in `lib/main.dart:2681` post-launch refresh, `lib/screens/main_screen.dart:765` on servers connected, `:1775` on profile-switch re-bind, and `download_provider.dart:214` on profile reload). The list is already `SliverList.builder` (paged in the Flutter sense). Test: "pinned metadata scopes resolve without the bulk pinned decode (1.2.18 S-14)" in `test/services/download_manager_service_test.dart` — full file run 100/100 pass.
- 59.15 (S-15 page the big in-memory lists) — NOT BUILT (premise stale / structurally blocked). (a) Episode-list paging ALREADY BUILT: `_fetchAllEpisodes` (`lib/screens/media_detail_screen.dart:3063`) loads only the first 200-episode page (`_episodesPageSize = 200`) via `_fetchFlattenedEpisodePage`, with load-more on scroll (`:2483`); `_episodes` mirrors `_allEpisodes.items`. The report's "flattens all episodes" premise is stale. (b) Cap `_fetchAllPlayableDescendants` with a streaming/iterative launcher NOT BUILT: it already pages via `drainPages` (server-side `Recursive: true`); the result list IS the playback queue, and the launcher (`lib/services/jellyfin_sequential_launcher.dart`) structurally needs the full list for shuffle + `resolveStartIndex` (play-from-here) — streaming defers the hold without reducing it and breaks shuffle/start-item; a hard cap would silently truncate "play all". (c) Page playlist/collection loads ALREADY BUILT: both `fetchAllPlaylistItems` (200/page) and `fetchAllCollectionItemsPaged` (100/page) in `lib/services/playlist_items_loader.dart` already use `drainPages`.
- 59.16 (S-16 ExoPlayer trim narrowing) — BUILT (Kotlin; compile + Kotlin tests UNPROVEN in-harness, CI runs them). ExoPlayer is the optional backend (mpv is default). (1) `LoadControlPolicy.targetBufferBytesForTrimLevel(steadyTargetBytes, level)` (`android/app/src/main/kotlin/com/pleztv/app/exoplayer/LoadControlPolicy.kt`, needs `import android.content.ComponentCallbacks2`): mirrors `DemuxerBudget.forTrimLevel`'s level mapping — RUNNING_CRITICAL + COMPLETE → `MIN_TARGET_BYTES` (32 MiB floor, never below); RUNNING_LOW + BACKGROUND + MODERATE → `maxOf(MIN_TARGET_BYTES, steady/2)`; else (RUNNING_MODERATE, UI_HIDDEN) → null. Time floors (`minBufferMs`, playback thresholds) untouched. Plus `RESTORE_POLL_MS = 30_000L`, `RESTORE_QUIET_MS = 60_000L`, `RESTORE_HEADROOM_FACTOR = 4L` in the policy (self-contained; `DemuxerBudget` is mpv-package-internal). (2) `ObservingLoadControl` gains volatile `targetBufferBytesCap` + cached `loadAllocator` (captured in `onPrepared` via `delegate.getAllocator(playerId)`, cleared in `onReleased` along with the cap; `reset()` deliberately does NOT touch the cap). `shouldContinueLoading` returns false when `delegate` says no OR `loadAllocator.totalBytesAllocated >= cap`; cap reads fail open (try/catch → delegate's answer). (3) `ExoPlayerCore.onTrimMemory(level)` (new `import android.os.SystemClock`; fields `exoBufferRestoreRunnable` + `lastExoNarrowAtMs` next to `observingLoadControl` `:308`): only ever narrows (`wanted >= currentCap` → return); logs via `emitLog("info", "memory", …)`; arms `scheduleExoBufferRestore()` — Handler poll that restores the cap (sets null) once past the quiet window AND `currentMemoryInfo()` (new helper via `activity.getSystemService`) shows `!lowMemory && availMem - threshold >= 4 * (steady - cap)`; re-arms itself otherwise. `dispose()` already calls `handler.removeCallbacksAndMessages(null)`, which cancels the restore runnable. (4) `ExoPlayerPlugin` wiring mirrors `MpvPlayerPlugin`: `ComponentCallbacks2` (`memoryCallbacks`) registered on the application context in `onAttachedToEngine`, unregistered in `onDetachedFromEngine`; `onLowMemory` forwards `TRIM_MEMORY_RUNNING_LOW` (same reading as the mpv side). Kotlin tests added (unrunnable locally): `LoadControlPolicyTest.trimLevelsNarrowTheTargetWithoutDroppingBelowTheFloor` and `ObservingLoadControlTest.theRuntimeByteCapStopsLoadingAboveTheCap` (`FakeLoadControl` now holds ONE stable `DefaultAllocator(true, 64*1024)` instance instead of creating one per `getAllocator` call). Proof is device-only (`adb shell am send-trim-memory <pkg> RUNNING_CRITICAL` during ExoPlayer playback; debug export RSS + rebuffer count).
- 59.17 (C-1 cache the channel list + key→index map) — BUILT. Cached guide channel list + key→index map in `lib/media/iptv_client.dart` (`_cachedGuideChannels`, `_cachedGuideChannelIndex`, `_cachedGuideIndexLastSync`, `_cachedGuideIndexRowCount`, `_invalidateGuideChannelCache()`). New `guideChannelsWithIndex()`; `_windowFeedIdsFor` in `iptv_live_tv.dart` (`:1795`) uses it. Invalidation at `clearCatalogCache`, `dropCatalogFromMemory`, `refreshLiveChannelsFromNetwork`, `_rederiveMemosFromDisk`. Extends R-6 with the CPU evidence (round-1 report §6).
- 59.18 (C-2 one long-lived worker isolate; never return typed lists) — NOT BUILT. The no-typed-list half is already covered where it matters: S-2 (sync/seed return counts, disk write in the worker), 1.2.16 (visible file write returns void), S-1 (feed parse returns only retained rows, ≤64 channels). The REMAINING typed-list returns feed callers that NEED the list: Xtream catalog parses (`xtream_api.dart:593,606,643` + `parse*StreamsBody` + file variants) build the catalog memo / feed the first-load coverage guard (hidden categories count as covered, FORK §45) and caller-side filtering; M3U parses (`iptv_client.dart:3156,3172,3202`) build `_m3uEntries`. Removing the return means re-architecting those callers onto the disk index — a bigger change than C-2's isolate swap, touching hub load + reload + coverage guard. The long-lived isolate itself is NOT built: the remaining win is ~10–30 ms spawn per `compute()` call, and a persistent isolate adds lifecycle + error-propagation + restart surface with no harness proof for production behaviour (isolate death on memory pressure, port leaks). S-3's gate already serializes the jobs, which was C-2's other goal.
- 59.19 (C-3 matcher maps each candidate's programmes once) — BUILT (deviation: exactly-equivalent predicate scan, NOT the slot bitmap the report suggests). In `_matchInGuide`, double map replaced with a predicate scan over `guide.rowsFor(feedId)` — the bitmap has edge-case false positives and ignores the timeshift offset.
- 59.20 (C-4 guide height) — BUILT (`lib/screens/livetv/tabs/guide_tab.dart`). `_guideContentHeight` no longer loops all 26k rows per horizontal-scroll frame. New `double? _cachedGuideContentHeight`, computed inline in the `_guideRows` getter; `_guideContentHeight` returns the cached value when `identical(rows, _cachedGuideRows)`.
- 59.21 (C-5 seed-then-search on the cold path) — BUILT (`lib/media/iptv_client.dart`). New `_coldSearchSeedInFlight` set + `_kickColdSearchSeed(type)` helper: when an as-you-type search finds a cold index, it kicks the per-type disk seed in the background — once per type (deduped, cleared on completion), serialized with the sync through `_runIndexWrite`, fire-and-forget via `unawaited`, never throws. Kicked in all three cold fallbacks (`_searchMovies`/`_searchSeries`/`_searchLive`), right after `trace.cold = true;`. Awaiting the seed for the current keystroke was rejected (seed takes seconds, would blow the section budget). Hardening found during verification: the first form's `unawaited(_runIndexWrite(...).whenComplete(...))` let `_runIndexWrite`'s own throw (it resolves the database path before the seed runs — `MissingPluginException` from `path_provider` in the search-wrapper bench test) escape as an unhandled async error; the kick is now an async closure with try/catch around the whole `_runIndexWrite` call, so it truly never throws. The bench regression itself was S-2's per-row sink write (see 59.2), not the kick — proven by bisect (kick disabled, test still hung).
- 59.22 (C-6 decode at display size, no supersample) — PARTIAL (`lib/utils/media_image_helper.dart`). (1) Supersample 1.0× for grid tiles: `_supersampleFor` now returns 1.0 for everything except `ImageType.heroLogo` (keeps old taper from 1.5 to 1.0 past `_supersampleDensityTarget` 3.0). `artworkFilterQuality` derives from the same predicate, so grid tiles now paint bilinear (`FilterQuality.low`). At DPR 3 (Fold) the supersample was already 1.0, so the owner's Fold sees no change; TV sees softer posters (accepted trade). (2) Backdrop decode cap 1080p on ≤4 GB: new `static bool get _isLowRamDevice`; `getMemCacheDimensions` art case has `ImageType.art when _isLowRamDevice => (1920, 1080)`. FETCH side (`roundDimensions`, shared by all types) deliberately NOT capped. (3) Per-source disk cache split NOT BUILT — `PlexImageCacheManager` is a shared singleton (3000 objects / 14 days) referenced in 14 places across 7 files; splitting needs separate CacheManagers/dirs/migration — medium refactor with cache-invalidation risk. Tests updated: `test/utils/media_image_helper_test.dart` (poster-supersample tests rewritten; new 4K-on-3-GiB art-cap test using `DevicePerformance.debugReset(totalMemBytes: 3 << 30)`) and `test/widgets/optimized_media_image_test.dart` (filter-quality test updated; two provider-dimension tests updated after proving via temporary stash they failed on new code but passed on base; tests pin `tester.view.display.size = 1920×1080`). After updates: media_image_helper + optimized_media_image tests 36/36 pass.
- 59.23 (C-7 software-decode visibility + cheap SW options + no mediacodec-copy retry) — PARTIAL. (a) BUILT — log `hwdec-current` + frame-drop counts into the debug export: new file `lib/utils/last_decode_stats.dart` (static holder: `hwdecCurrent`, `frameDropCount`, `decoderFrameDropCount`, `updatedAt`; `recordHwdec` returns true on change; `recordDrops` nulls leave last-known alone; `summaryLine()`; `@visibleForTesting debugReset()`); `PlayerNative` video init observes `hwdec-current` (`await observeProperty('hwdec-current', 'string')` in the extras next to `secondary-sid`); `PlayerBase.handlePropertyChange` new `case 'hwdec-current':` records into the holder + logs once per flip; overlay service `_emit` records `stats.hwdecCurrent` + both drop counts; debug export summary (`lib/services/debug_log_export_service.dart` `buildSummarySection`) adds `LastDecodeStats.summaryLine()` after the memory line. (b) NOT BUILT — retry `mediacodec-copy` once on corrupt-input errors: premise wrong (mediacodec-copy doesn't fix corrupt input — the stream itself is bad); no detection→reload machinery exists; reload-loop risk; no harness proof. The existing `onVoFormatRejected` → GL vo path already handles the format-rejection fallback. (c) ALREADY BUILT — software-decode indicator: the performance overlay already renders the decoder row (`performance_overlay.dart:82` `_metric(t.performanceOverlay.decoder, _stats.hwdecFormatted)`, "Software" when hwdec-current is 'no'; `performance_stats.dart:294-298`); the report's research missed it. (d) BUILT (Kotlin) — cheap software-decode options at video-core init in `MpvPlayerCore.kt`: `setOption("vd-lavc-skiploopfilter", "all")`, `setOption("vd-lavc-fast", "yes")`, `setOption("vd-lavc-threads", lavcThreadCap().toString())` where `lavcThreadCap() = minOf(4, Runtime.getRuntime().availableProcessors().coerceAtLeast(1))`; plus `lowres=1` in `decoderOptions` when `totalMemBytes() <= 2L shl 30` (new `totalMemBytes()` helper via existing `memoryInfo()?.totalMem`, placed before `setOption("vd-lavc-o",...)`; user's own vd-lavc-o text still wins on duplicates via `DecoderOptions.compose()`). These are lavc-only options, inert under hardware decode — "only on the SW path" by mechanism, not by gating (the hwdec-current hook fires after the decoder already opened, too late to help the current file). A user's mpv.conf line for any of them wins. Kotlin compile UNPROVEN in-harness. Tests: `test/utils/last_decode_stats_test.dart` (5 tests) — all pass; combined run with `player_open_test.dart` = 49/49 pass.
- 59.24 (C-8 loading bar + now-indicator without full-tab setState) — BUILT (`lib/screens/livetv/tabs/guide_tab.dart`). (1) Loading bar: new `ValueNotifier<bool> _epgLoadingNotifier` (initial false); only the bar widget is wrapped in a `ValueListenableBuilder`. Post-frame `setState` in `_ensureIptvEpg` replaced with notifier writes. Third use at :1399 in `_showIptvEpgDeniedBanner` now reads `!_epgLoadingNotifier.value`. (2) Now-indicator: new `ValueNotifier<int> _nowTickNotifier`; `onRefreshTick` bumps it instead of full-tab `setState`. Both notifiers disposed in `dispose()`. Old `_isIptvEpgLoading` getter removed. Verified safe: programme cells have no "now" highlight; only per-minute UI is the now-indicator line. BUG FOUND AND FIXED DURING VERIFICATION: the first form wrote `_epgLoadingNotifier.value = true` directly inside `_ensureIptvEpg`, which runs during a row build — flipping a notifier mid-build marks the bar's builder dirty during build ("setState() called during build"), failing 3 build22 mini-player stream-UA tests (13/13 on base). Fixed by deferring the write to a post-frame callback that re-reads the in-flight set (`_epgLoadingNotifier.value = _iptvEpgInFlight.isNotEmpty`) — a fetch that already landed cannot leave the bar stuck on. Build22 green again (13/13).
- 59.25 (C-9 parameterised cache-pause-wait per live tier) — BUILT (a setting, not a blind cut). (1) `SettingsService.liveLowLatencyZapping = BoolPref('live_low_latency_zapping', defaultValue: false)` next to `enableHardwareDecoding` (`lib/services/settings_service.dart:634`), registered in `_resetAndPortablePrefs` (group one: reset *and* exported). (2) `Player` factory (`lib/mpv/player/player.dart:407`) gains `liveLowLatency = false`, passed to `PlayerNativeAndroid(liveLowLatency: …)` and `PlayerNative(liveLowLatency: …)`. (3) `PlayerNative` gains `final bool _liveLowLatency` (main ctor + audio ctor sets false; `PlayerNativeAndroid({super.hardwareDecoding, super.liveLowLatency})` in `lib/mpv/player/platform/player_native_android.dart:15-17`). (4) `player_native.dart:546` now `if (isLive)...['cache-pause-initial=yes', 'cache-pause-wait=${_liveLowLatency? 2: 6}']` with comment (#2711: 6 s hold-back exists because segments land late; cutting it re-introduces stalls on jittery Wi-Fi). (5) `video_player_screen.dart:1758` reads the setting next to `enableHardwareDecoding`; `:1864` passes it to `Player(...)`. (6) Settings UI tile in `lib/screens/settings/playback_settings_screen.dart` next to `_hardwareDecodingTile()` (`:450`, `SettingSwitchTile`, mpv-only via `if (!exoActive)`) + i18n keys `liveLowLatencyZapping` / `liveLowLatencyZappingDescription` in `lib/i18n/en.i18n.json` + slang regen. Setting only affects mpv (ExoPlayer path doesn't use `cache-pause-wait`). A/B measurement (zap 6 vs 2, rebuffer counts) is device-owed. Test: "MPV holds back one live segment when low-latency zapping is on (1.2.18 C-9)" in `test/mpv/player_open_test.dart` — passes.
- 59.26 (C-10 decoder-failure memo) — BUILT. (1) Kotlin (1 emit): `MpvPlayerCore.onVoFormatRejected()` now also calls `delegate?.onEvent("vo-format-rejected", null)` (`PlayerDelegate.onEvent(name, data: Map<String, Any>?)` — null data allowed). (2) New Dart store `lib/services/decoder_failure_memo.dart` extending `BaseSharedPreferencesService` (pattern copied from `StorageService`): one JSON blob under prefs key `decoder_failure_memo_v1`; `failureThreshold = 2`; `memoTtl = 7 days`; in-memory map is source of truth, reads sync off memory, writes update memory first then persist fire-and-forget; `shouldStartOnGlVo(key)`, `recordRejection(key)`, `recordSuccess(key)` (a clean `hwdec-current=mediacodec` clears the count), `clearAll()`, `debugFailuresFor(key)`, `debugResetForTesting()`; static `currentChannelKey` (channel currently playing live; set by live screen at launch/zap, cleared on dispose); `instanceOrNull` sync accessor; corrupt blob starts empty instead of throwing. Deviation: codec is deliberately not stored — the decision is per channel, not per codec. (3) Player plumbing: base `Player.open` (`lib/mpv/player/player.dart:93`) + `PlayerNative.open` + `PlayerAndroid.open` (ExoPlayer ignores the hint) gain `bool startOnGlVo = false`; `PlayerNative.open` threads it to `_loadReplacement` which adds `if (startOnGlVo)...const ['vo=gpu']` to the loadfile options (per-file policy arbiter can still raise the vo, e.g. DV reshape to gpu-next). `PlayerBase.handlePlayerEvent` new `case 'vo-format-rejected':` records a rejection for `DecoderFailureMemo.currentChannelKey` (null outside live playback); `PlayerBase.handlePropertyChange` `'hwdec-current'` case (59.23a) also calls `recordSuccess` when value is `'mediacodec'`. (4) Live screen: `_openLiveStream` (`lib/screens/video_player/parts/live_tv.dart:361`) gains optional `String? channelKey` — when non-null sets the static holder; consults the memo via `memoKey = channelKey ?? DecoderFailureMemo.currentChannelKey` + `(await DecoderFailureMemo.getInstance()).shouldStartOnGlVo(memoKey)` and passes `startOnGlVo:` to both `player.open` call sites (`:392` plain, `:403` clock/time-shifted). Zap site (`:835`) passes `channelKey: channel.key`; launch site (`lib/screens/video_player/parts/playback_start.dart:121`) passes `channelKey: channel.key`. Recovery/catch-up/subtitle re-opens (`:317`, `:528`, `:583`) pass nothing — channel unchanged, holder still names it, and the hint is still computed from the holder. Dispose (`lib/screens/video_player_screen.dart:2452`) clears the holder when `widget.isLive` (so a later VOD rejection can never land on a stale channel). (5) Manual reset: `SettingNavigationTile` in the Player group of `lib/screens/settings/playback_settings_screen.dart` (next to the C-9 tile; mpv-only via `if (!exoActive)`; delete icon as `trailingIcon`; `onTap: () => unawaited(_clearDecoderMemo(context))` which awaits `clearAll()` then shows a snackbar if mounted) + i18n keys `clearDecoderMemo` / `clearDecoderMemoDescription` / `clearDecoderMemoDone` + slang regen. Tests: `test/services/decoder_failure_memo_test.dart` (6 tests: one rejection doesn't memo/two do; clean hardware decode clears; entries survive restart through prefs; entries older than 7 days expire on load via a directly-written stale blob; `clearAll` forgets every channel; corrupt blob starts empty) — 6/6 pass; `test/mpv/player_open_test.dart` "MPV starts a memoed channel on the GL vo (1.2.18 C-10)" (`open(Media(uri), isLive: true, startOnGlVo: true)` → options contain `vo=gpu`) — passes. The event→memo path (Kotlin emit → event channel → `handlePlayerEvent`) is UNPROVEN in-harness (needs the native core); the store + open + settings tile are tested. BUG FOUND AND FIXED DURING VERIFICATION: adding `startOnGlVo` to `Player.open` broke every test fake that overrides `open` (Dart requires the override to accept the new named parameter) — the build22 file failed to COMPILE, and 15 more fake overrides across `test/mpv/`, `test/screens/`, and `test/services/` were fixed the same way (`bool startOnGlVo = false` added; fakes without an explicit `open` or with `noSuchMethod` needed nothing). `flutter analyze` confirms zero issues in touched files.
- 59.27 (C-11 display-resample on phone live) — BUILT. `video-sync=display-resample` (+ `interpolation`, `tscale=oversample`) on phone + live only, as file-local loadfile options in `player_native.dart` (after the cache-pause line), gated on `isLive && _isLiveDisplayResamplePhone` where the gate is `Platform.isAndroid && !PlatformDetector.isTV()` (TVs switch HDMI modes via FrameRateMatcher instead; iOS/other platforms keep mpv's default). Deviation from the report (which names MpvPlayerCore init): the core cannot know live/VOD at init time, and the live signal lives in the loadfile options next to `cache-pause-wait` — same mechanism, exact gating. Test hook `@visibleForTesting static bool? debugLiveDisplayResamplePhoneOverride` (null = real platform check; tests run on a Linux host where `Platform.isAndroid` is false). Tests (2, both pass): "MPV adds display-resample options for live opens on phones (1.2.18 C-11)" and "MPV keeps mpv defaults for live opens off phones (1.2.18 C-11)". Proof is device-only (25 fps live channel on the Fold at 120 Hz; the owner's eyes are the real judge).
- 59.28 (C-12 guide scroll cost) — BUILT (`lib/screens/livetv/tabs/guide_tab.dart`). (1) `scrollCacheExtent: const ScrollCacheExtent.pixels(500)` on both `CustomScrollView`s (needs `import 'package:flutter/rendering.dart' show ScrollCacheExtent;` — plain `cacheExtent:` is deprecated in Flutter 3.47.9). (2) `RepaintBoundary` around `_buildProgramRow(…)` in the grid's `itemBuilder`. (3) Vertical cross-scroll sync throttled: `NotificationListener` no longer `jumpTo`s per frame — new `_syncChannelColumnToGrid(pixels)` stores the latest offset (`_pendingChannelColumnOffset` + `_channelColumnSyncScheduled`) and applies one post-frame jump. Horizontal listeners left as-is.
- 59.29 (C-13 blur rasterization) — PARTIAL (visual-identical form). Every live `ImageFiltered` blur now rasterizes once into a `RepaintBoundary` and is reused — 7 sites: `media_card.dart:1244`, `episode_card.dart:180`, `lib/screens/libraries/folder_tree_item.dart:368`, `lib/screens/video_player/widgets/player_prompt_overlays.dart:401`, `lib/widgets/video_controls/widgets/media_selector_thumbnail.dart:71` (all sigma-12 spoiler-hiding blurs), `collection_detail_screen.dart:419` (sigma-40 header backdrop), `now_playing_screen.dart:934` (sigma-60 full-screen backdrop; boundary inside the static `Opacity`). Plus `blurArtwork` in `optimized_media_image.dart` (sigma 30). Findings: `blurArtwork` is compiled OUT of normal builds (`kBlurArtwork = bool.fromEnvironment('PLEZY_BLUR_ARTWORK')`, default false, set nowhere) — that part of C-13 is moot. The static-gradient-scrim swap (a visual change) was NOT done — needs the owner's verdict. Tradeoff: each boundary retains one cached raster layer.
- 59.30 (C-14 background timers + resume refetch) — PARTIAL. (a) LAN stale-cleanup timer BUILT: `lib/services/companion_remote/lan_discovery_service.dart` — `Timer.periodic` 2 s → 30 s. Beacons arrive every 3 s, stale timeout 10 s, so a dead host lingers up to ~30 s longer in the picker; a live host is never stale. (b) Resume-refetch visibility gate BUILT (`lib/screens/discover_screen.dart` `didChangeAppLifecycleState`): `refreshIfStale()` + `refreshContinueWatching()` on resume now run only when `_isTabVisible`; safe because `onTabShown()` already calls `_discover.refreshIfStale()`; desktop focus-gain refresh (#1646) still fires when visible. (c) NOT BUILT: lazy Sentry init — `_enableSentry` defaults to false and `ENABLE_SENTRY` appears nowhere in `.github/`, so Sentry init is dead code in shipped builds; deferring it saves nothing and would lose early-startup crash capture. NOT BUILT: drop immediate Plex-home `_refreshAll()` on startup when disk cache is fresh (`lib/profiles/plex_home_service.dart:168,172`) — no freshness signal exists (cache JSON is a bare user list, no timestamp; SharedPreferences has no mtime), and adding one requires shadowing the commit/rollback machinery for a single skipped refresh per launch. NOT BUILT: IPTV-bind-state gate for the resume refetch — CORRECTION (1.2.18 fix agent): a bind-in-flight signal DOES exist (`_iptvCatalogBindFutures`, `lib/services/multi_server_manager.dart:207`, public `awaitIptvCatalogBinds()` `:1018`, used by `lib/screens/main_screen.dart:594`); the honest narrower reason it was not wired in is that the existing signal is IPTV-catalog-bind-only, not a general bind signal, so wiring Discover's resume refetch to it is still cross-subsystem work. Analyze clean on both touched files.
- 59.31 (C-15 Kodi 1080p-UI trick) — NOT BUILT (investigated). Flutter exposes no simple render-scale knob: the engine sizes its raster surface from the embedder's display metrics, so capping it needs embedder/surface work (the one plausible seam — `SurfaceTexture.setDefaultBufferSize` on the existing `FlutterTextureView` (`PlezyActivity.kt:131,742,886`) plus spoofing the viewport metrics the engine reads — fights the engine's own surface management, which re-sets the buffer size on surface changes, and risks a mis-sized/offset UI on rotation/resume). A Dart-side `devicePixelRatio` override does NOT change the raster surface (only layout) — it would not deliver the claimed raster saving. The Kodi premise does not transfer: Kodi's GUI is CPU-rastered; Flutter/Impeller rasterizes on the GPU, so the 4K raster cost is GPU fragment work, not the CPU raster Kodi avoids. Unknown interaction with the `vo=mediacodec` plane sizing (separate SurfaceView, physical-pixel math), plus the text-sharpness tradeoff. HIGH risk, spike-only, device-only proof on the Hisense. Do not promise this.
- Version: 1.2.18-2.25.0+212. Changelog `about.changelog2304` (14 entries, plain words; corrected during the build — the draft overclaimed S-13 tab disposal and S-10's Kotlin widening, both NOT built, and missed C-9/C-11/S-7/S-14/S-16). Tests: NEW `test/services/decoder_failure_memo_test.dart` (6) + `test/utils/last_decode_stats_test.dart` (5) + `test/services/download_manager_service_test.dart` S-14 test (1) + `test/mpv/player_open_test.dart` C-9/C-10/C-11 tests (4); updated `test/utils/media_image_helper_test.dart` + `test/widgets/optimized_media_image_test.dart` (36/36 after updates); `download_manager_service_test.dart` full file 100/100; `last_decode_stats_test.dart` + `player_open_test.dart` combined 49/49. Full iptv suite (`test/services/iptv/` + `test/media/`, 896 tests): 894/896 in one run; the 2 failures are the known timing-flake class under parallel-shard load on the throttled VM — build13 first-launch wall-clock bound and the R2 scorer bench-median <1000 ms — both pass standalone (1/1, 7/7). Three REAL regressions were found by that suite run and fixed in-tree (see 59.2, 59.21, 59.24, 59.26): S-2's per-row sink write (~33 s at Trex scale), C-5's unhandled `_runIndexWrite` throw, C-8's mid-build notifier write, C-10's 16 fake `open` overrides, and the S-6 tmp-race test rewrite. Full `flutter analyze`: 53 issues = base 53, zero issues in touched files. UNPROVEN (phone/device-only): every Kotlin change (S-16 trim, C-7d lavc options, C-10 `vo-format-rejected` emit) — compile + Kotlin tests await CI/cloud build; C-9 A/B (zap 6 vs 2, rebuffer counts); C-11 (25 fps live on the Fold at 120 Hz); S-16 (`adb shell am send-trim-memory <pkg> RUNNING_CRITICAL` during ExoPlayer playback; debug export RSS + rebuffer count); C-10 event→memo path (needs the native core); the R5 widget-level proof gap from 1.2.17 stands (support-proven + code-inspected only); About 1.2.18-2.25.0; the updater offering it and not re-offering after install; RSS peaks on the Fold vs 1.2.15 (581/845 MB) and 1.2.17.

## 60. Round-3 low-end build — all 61 fixes + OOM streaming + 404/458 + release build (v1.2.19-2.25.0, versionCode 213)

- Why: the owner's decision 2026-11-15 ~03:56 EST — build ALL 61 fixes from the round-3 low-end research report (`~/workspace/tmp_work/plezy-tv-research-lowend-round3/RESEARCH_LOWEND_ROUND3.md`, 285 lines; L-1–L-61), with the OOM streaming fixes (A1/A2) FIRST, the 404/458 full-player unification, and the L-1 release-build switch. Judge memory by PEAKS (max RSS), not average. 52 BUILT, 5 PARTIAL, 4 NOT BUILT with reason, plus A1/A2 and the 404/458 fix BUILT. Kotlin changes are UNPROVEN in-harness (no local Kotlin compile — CI is the proof); device-only behaviour is labelled UNPROVEN with the exact proof owed. The storefront / 3-part guide is RESEARCH ONLY (1.2.20+ line) — NOT built here. Companion app SHELVED, Dispatcharr PARKED, PlezTV Refreshed has no code fork.
- 60.1 (A1 live-list streaming) — BUILT. `XtreamApi.getLiveStreams` no longer buffers the whole list: both call sites (`_loadLiveStreamsCached`, `refreshLiveChannelsFromNetwork`) go through `getLiveStreamsToFile` + `parseLiveStreamsFile` (`lib/services/iptv/xtream_api.dart`, `lib/media/iptv_client.dart`), exactly as 1.2.16 did for VOD/series. The ~60–120 MB transient whole-body buffer is gone.
- 60.2 (A2 playlist streaming) — BUILT. `XtreamApi.getM3u` no longer buffers the whole playlist: `getM3uToFile` + `parseM3uFile` stream to file and parse from file in isolate, 120 s gap timeout (`lib/services/iptv/xtream_api.dart`). The R2 whole-playlist buffer — the most likely 1.2.15 OOM mechanism — is gone.
- 60.3 (dead `getXmltv` deletion + Discord cap) — BUILT. The dead whole-body `getXmltv` path (zero production callers) is deleted, and the uncapped Discord thumbnail `getBytes` call has a `maxBytes`.
- 60.4 (404/458 full-player unification) — BUILT. The rule already lived in the portrait-phone mini-player; the full player had none (a failed catch-up tap dropped the offset and silently re-opened LIVE TV, burning 2 more opens on a busy line). Full player now latches the HTTP status from player logs (`_lastHttpStatus`, `lib/screens/video_player_screen.dart`, set in `lib/screens/video_player/parts/errors.dart:318`, reset at every tune start) — 404 + catch-up shows the missing-recording card naming programme/channel with a Watch live offer (`_watchLiveAfterCatchUpFailure`), 458 shows line-busy/transient and is deliberately NOT in `fatalPlaybackHttpStatuses`. Mini-player behaviour unchanged. Tests in the playback group (20 new, all pass).
- 60.5 (L-1 release build) — BUILT. `.github/workflows/android-release.yml` + `codemagic.yaml` now build `--release --split-debug-info=build/app/outputs/symbols` (was `--debug`); no `--obfuscate` — it would rename Dart symbols in the emergency saver's Last-crash stack traces. Release buildType falls back to debug signing in CI, so install-over-the-top keeps working. R8 GATE UNPROVEN: the `minified` build type exists (`android/app/build.gradle.kts:391`, run with `-Pplezy.testBuildType=minified`) but no CI job runs it and no emulator job exists in the repo — adding one was judged out of integration scope. Owed proof: run `./gradlew connectedMinifiedDebugAndroidTest -Pplezy.testBuildType=minified` in CI or on a machine with the Android SDK + an emulator; verify the #1703 ExoPlayer path + playback suites pass under R8. A successful CI release build only proves R8 shrink completes, not runtime reachability. Note: the `release` buildType has no explicit `isMinifyEnabled` — the Flutter plugin installs minification + keep rules on it, so `flutter build apk --release` DOES run R8.
- 60.6 (L-2 five-tier enum) — BUILT. `enum DeviceTier { survival, low, standard, mid, full }` in `lib/services/device_performance.dart`; `DevicePerformance.currentTier`; thresholds 1536/2048/3072/4096 MiB; `isLowRamDevice()==true` caps at `low`; unknown totalMem → `standard` (L-19 fail-safe, never full); `isReduced` = T0–T2; `debugSummary` map (tier, totalMemMb, isLowRamDevice, is64Bit, heapClassMb, sdkInt, displayWidth/Height, dpr, freeStorageMb). Canonical API for the whole bundle — every other group coded against it. INTEGRATION FIX: the pre-detection default was `full` (field default + getter fallback), contradicting the L-19 rule — changed to `standard`; non-Android early return now sets `full` explicitly (tvOS/iOS/desktop stay full). Startup group's draft thresholds (2252/2867/3789 MiB, null → full) were NOT ported — canonical table wins everywhere.
- 60.7 (L-3 Range resume, EPG feed) — BUILT. Feed download resumes with `Range` + sidecar; the Reload row now shows live progress (`Downloading feed — 12.4 MB of 88.0 MB (14%)`, bytes-only when no total) via public `feedDownloadProgress(cacheKey)` / `xtreamFeedCacheKey` / `hasPendingFeedResume(cacheKey)` on `IptvLiveTvSupport` (`lib/media/iptv_live_tv.dart`), polled by the EPG Sources screen (500 ms), plus a `Partial feed download — will resume on next reload` line (`lib/screens/settings/iptv_epg_sources_screen.dart`). Progress strings migrated to i18n at integration (`epgPartialFeedWillResume`, `epgFeedProgressNoTotal`, `epgFeedProgressWithTotal`). Takeover fix: the guard deadlocked widget tests (`MeteredNetwork.largeDownloadsDeferred()` → cold `sharedCache()` file I/O never completes under fake async) — it now reads the pref from `SettingsService.instanceOrNull` when initialized, falling back to awaited `sharedCache()` only with no instance (background isolate). The old per-run temp dir (`syncTempDir`, `plezy_sync_*`) is gone — resume files ARE the temp files now; the integration merge removed the dead finally-block cleanup that referenced it (it broke analyze until removed).

Split-per-abi BUILT: workflow builds `flutter build apk --release --split-per-abi`, renames outputs to `PlezTV.<tag>-arm64-v8a.apk` / `-armeabi-v7a.apk` / `-x86_64.apk`, uploads + attaches `PlezTV.<tag>-*.apk`; updater matches by ABI (`selectApkAsset` — a theoretical 32-bit x86 device would match the `x86_64` asset, but Flutter builds no 32-bit x86 APK and no shipping device reports `Abi.androidIA32`). Codemagic stays a single fat release APK (manual backup path; updater never reads it). `extractNativeLibs=false` IS SET — `android:extractNativeLibs="false"` on the `<application>` element in `android/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:50` (with a 1.2.19 L-9 comment). CORRECTION (parent, 2026-11-15): the earlier "NOT SET" claim checked the wrong file — `extractNativeLibs` is a manifest attribute, not a Gradle setting, so "absent from build.gradle.kts" proved nothing. The deferral decision is void; the flip ships in 1.2.19. Pre-delivery proof: `zipalign -c -v 4` on each split APK (native .so entries must be stored + page-aligned). Device install-over-the-top still owed as the on-device proof.

- 60.24 (L-20 storage gate) — BUILT. One accessor: `StorageHealth.freeBytes()` (`lib/services/storage_health.dart`) reads `getPerformanceSignals` on `com.plezy/device`, key `freeStorageBytes` — the same call `DevicePerformance` already makes; no second free-space read, no native channel extension. Thresholds as named constants: 500 MB low gate, 200 MB refuse, 512 KB dump cap, 500-object cache eviction. Fail-open on unknown (null never refuses/limits). (a) Image disk cache: 500-object cap when low (`imageDiskCacheMaxObjects`, `applyLowStorageCap()` empties an already-built 3,000-cap manager), wired in `_initializeApplication` after `StorageHealth.refresh()`. (b) Feed skip: `_fetchFeedPrograms` returns empty while low — guide falls back to short-EPG (user-initiated Reload still downloads). (c) Settings row: `StorageHealthRow` in Advanced, renders only while <500 MB free. (d) No full-file catalog rewrite while low (`IptvCatalogDiskCache.write` skips, `writeFilePath` returns null). Refusal: `_syncSearchIndex` refuses below 200 MB before any fetch/temp-dir/index touch (`skipped: 'storage-full'` + per-type progress events); `IptvBackgroundSync.runOnce` refuses from the persisted verdict (stale >24 h reads as unknown). 33 new tests pass. Phone gate (after L-19 lands — it has, in this tree): filled disk must assert the row, feed skip, sync refusal, saver message.
- 60.25 (L-21 saver hardening) — BUILT. Dump cap: `buildDebugText` builds the summary from the FULL log first (verdicts preserved), then caps the log body to the last 512 KB via pure `capLogTail` (walks UTF-16 backwards counting UTF-8 bytes; cuts at a line boundary; `[log trimmed to the last 512 KB …]` note) — this deliberately supersedes the 1.2.14 "nothing gets cut" rule (T-A5 rewritten). Free-space check: `AutoDumpService.writeDump` / `writeDumpToPicker` refuse with plain-words `storageFullMessage` when the dump cannot fit (picker never opens, counter untouched); `DebugLogExportService.exportToFile` same check (returns null). `writeDump` now returns `({String? path, String? storageFullMessage})` — only the saver screen and `auto_dump_test.dart` consume it; any other caller must use `.path`. "Continue without IPTV": saver button (optional `onContinueWithoutIptv`) sets `ActiveProfileBinder.skipIptvBindForSession = true` (static, process-lifetime only, never persisted); `_bindIptv` returns empty when set — no client, no Trex load, Plex/Jellyfin only. Saver counter rule untouched (any 3 crashes, cumulative; resets only when the saver fires and the user saves/continues).

INTEGRATION DECISION: the unknown-memory branches are KEPT (`totalMemBytes == null` → 4096 KiB / 100 ms historic) — a pure swap would break 3 tests incl. a pre-existing R2.5 widget test (expects 100 ms firing) and the sqlite group's pinned unknown tests; in production detection always runs before these paths, and detection-failure (null mem + standard tier) gets the historic 4 MB/100 ms, which is MORE conservative on memory, not a full budget — no L-19 violation in spirit.

- 60.66 (integration notes) — The bundle was built by 8 parallel groups (tier, sqlite, native, playback, startup, proof, net, storage) in own worktrees from 580feb852 and integrated here. Integration decisions: canonical tier API (60.6); tier-table swap with unknown branches kept (60.40); pre-detection default `full` → `standard` (60.6); `tier.label` → `tier.name` bug found in `iptv_background_sync.dart:144` (startup group's analyze claim missed it); all groups' hardcoded strings migrated into `en.i18n.json` + `dart run slang` (net's EPG progress strings, storage's row/saver/dump strings, playback/startup/native tiles); `changelog2305` has 29 plain-words entries (`about.changelog2305`, top of `about_screen.dart`). `git apply --3way` LESSON: a full-patch apply is atomic — one unappliable file silently rolls back the WHOLE patch while still printing "Applied cleanly" per file; always apply with `--exclude` for conflicted files (merge-file them separately) and verify with grep/diff after every apply, never pipe apply output through `head` (SIGPIPE). Full-tree `flutter analyze` = 50 (53 base − 3 fixed r4-test imports − 0 new; 49 are pre-existing vendored `wakelock_plus` noise + 1 pre-existing r4 info). Full `test/services/iptv/` + `test/media/` suite: 951/951 green (run 4, exit 0). The road there found three REAL integration bugs, all fixed in-tree: (a) the L-4 stable resume path (`plezy_resume_<conn>_<type>.json` in the shared system temp dir) is shared by every test process syncing one connection id — concurrent files truncated/deleted each other's resume files mid-sync, rerouting syncs to 'empty-response' (D1/D2, F1, P5b, S6 across runs 1–2). Fix: `IptvClient.debugResumeDirectoryProvider` seam + all 27 sync-running test files point it at their own temp dir (production needs no isolation — one app process, R4 stamp serializes syncs). (b) L-7/L-28 tier caps vs the old fixed values: the short-EPG concurrency test (expected fan-out 4, default standard tier gives 2) and W3/W5 (expected 624 programmes, standard cap 32 holds 32 candidate ids) now pin T4 in-test — the tier mapping itself is covered by `device_performance_test`. KNOWN L-7 FOLLOW-UP (not fixed here — guide-matching surface, needs device proof before touching): each channel contributes TWO candidate ids (feed id + numeric channel key, e.g. `ch0` + `1000`), so the tier cap counts phantoms too — on T2 the effective real-channel coverage is ~16, not the nominal 32. (c) One unreproduced parallel-load failure (run 3: `iptv_epg_matching_1_2_5_test` Reload diagnostics, expected 4 with guide, got 0) — passes standalone (1/1), whole file (22/22), and two combined EPG-heavy runs (84/84 ×2); run 4 green. The build13 Trex-scale bench wall-clock bound (400 ms first-launch) fails only under parallel-shard load (484 ms in run 3) and passes standalone (6/6) — the known timing-flake class, same as 1.2.18's suite.

## 61. Guide slide fix — SQLite feed-row store + Reload slide + feed fills short-EPG holes + honest Reload sample (v1.2.20-2.25.0, versionCode 214)

- Why: the owner's 2026-11-15 ~16:31-16:36 EST phone verdict on 1.2.19 — the guide showed zero Feed chips, the first slide landed ~4 min after the Reload, and the debug log showed 7 feed parses in ~30 s. Design doc `~/workspace/tmp_work/plezy-tv-research-guide-slide-1.2.20/RESEARCH_GUIDE_SLIDE_1.2.20.md` (320 lines). (a) BUILT, (b) BUILT (one deviation, see 61.2), (c) BUILT (two clarifications, see 61.3), (d) BUILT (one deviation, see 61.4). Version `1.2.20-2.25.0+214` (`pubspec.yaml:4`); `about.changelog2306` has 5 plain-words entries (`lib/i18n/en.i18n.json`).
- 61.1 (a) SQLite feed-row store — BUILT. New file `lib/services/iptv/iptv_feed_row_store.dart` (453 lines): one DB per feed cache key next to the persisted XML (`feed_<fileSafeKey>.db`, tmp build `<db>.tmpdb`); tables `feed_meta` (sha256, fetched_at, window, counts, slot origin, xml mtime/size), `feed_channels` (feed_id PK, program_count, slots BLOB, display_names), `feed_programs` WITHOUT ROWID, PK (channel_id, start) — holds EVERY programme in the I8 window, independent of retainIds. `readGuide` via `Isolate.run` returns a `_CachedXmltvGuide`-shaped record (empty retainIds = metadata-only read for the warm start); `isValid` checks user_version==1, SHA when known, XML mtime/size when the XML exists — a missing XML does NOT invalidate (the store keeps serving); 2 s busy timeout, never throws. Writer batches 5,000 inserts/transaction; `finish` commits and atomically renames. C3 OVERLAP CAUSE (determined before building, per the doc): `_xmltvGuideForKey`'s warm-start path had NO in-flight join — `_xmltvGuideFutures` covers downloads and `_xmltvSlideFutures` covers slides only, so N concurrent rows with no in-memory guide each called `_readPersistedXmltv` -> `_parseFeedFile`, producing 7 parse requests in ~2 s; the heavy-work gate serialised them (one at a time), so they drained over ~30 s, and the SlowOp durations (7-23 s) include gate queue-wait time (the stopwatch starts before the gate). Contributing: the relief valve had dropped the in-memory guide at 16:31:54 (`dropEpgFromMemory` -> `_xmltvCache.clear()`), and the Reload leaves the window empty by construction (C2). Residual (unexplained, noted honestly): 8 parse-start tags but only 6 slow-op records. Fix: per-key in-flight join on `_readPersistedXmltv` (`_xmltvPersistedReadFutures`, `lib/media/iptv_live_tv.dart:974` — a joiner may get the first caller's window; the caller always re-slides via `_ensureGuideWindow` when its ids are missing). Store-first warm start (`:3591`) and slide (`_slideGuideWindowLocked :2384`, `_readFeedRowsFromStore :2542`); store built once per download in the streaming isolate pass (`_parseFeedFile :4148`, caller tag `debugLastFeedParseCaller :1133`, `feed-store-build ms=X bytes=Y` log at `:4252`, per-key build guard `_feedStoreBuildsInFlight`); callers tagged download / load / warm-start / slide (`:4058`, `:3768`, `:3614`, `:2409`); SlowOpLog gained `detail` (`lib/services/lowend/slow_op_log.dart`), cadence log gained `durationMs` + `source` (`lib/services/lowend/epg_cadence_log.dart`). Store cleanup: no per-source feed-file deletion path exists (the only wholesale wipe, `clearEpgCache`, deletes the whole EPG directory recursively, so the DB goes with it); the one orphan path was fixed — `_readPersistedXmltv`'s corrupt-file catch now also deletes the store DB, because a missing XML does not invalidate the store and leaving the DB would keep serving the corrupt feed's rows. MEASURED (in-harness, Trex-scale 5,839 channels / 268,594 programmes): writer 2,920 ms / 13.2 MB for the full store (A4b, within the 5 s budget); full download+parse+store fetch 13,197 ms, DB 17.5 MB; per-download store-build log lines 87-295 ms on the small fixture. The 5 s budget is a Fold gate — the in-harness number is a VM number, not phone proof. Tests: `test/services/iptv/iptv_feed_row_store_1_2_20_test.dart` (9, incl. A1/A2/A3/A5/C3/A4a + 3 caller-tag tests) and `test/services/iptv/iptv_feed_row_store_unit_1_2_20_test.dart` (9, incl. A4b timing), all pass; failing-first proven on the 1.2.19 base (0 passed, 5 failed for the original 5).
- 61.2 (b) Reload slides the window — BUILT, with one deviation.
