Shared visible-row registry `_recentGuideFetches` (ring buffer, cap 200, 60 s window) + `_recentGuideFetchKeys` (`lib/media/iptv_live_tv.dart:990`, noted at the top of `fetchEpgForChannel`); helper `_slideReloadWindow` (`:2468`) returns `({slid, channelCount, reason})`; `IptvReloadTvDetailsResult.windowSlide` (`lib/services/iptv/iptv_epg_sources.dart:953`, reasons `store-unavailable | slide-failed | no-feed | null`); `reloadTvDetails` calls it after the match loop, `updateEpgNow` calls it best-effort with log-only reporting; dialog lines in `lib/screens/settings/iptv_epg_sources_screen.dart` (`epgReloadWindowSlideReady` / `epgReloadWindowSlideSkipped`, `lib/i18n/en.i18n.json:3243-3246`). DEVIATION (PARTIAL vs the doc's letter, closest correct thing): the doc says call `_ensureGuideWindow` per key, but per-key calls would replace the window each time (only the last key's rows survive); the build takes the UNION of `_windowCandidateIds` for the registry keys and makes ONE store-only slide (`_slideGuideWindowFromStore :2430`) per loaded feed. Hard rule kept: no valid store -> skip entirely (`store-unavailable`), never a re-parse slide inside Reload. Tests: `test/services/iptv/iptv_reload_slide_1_2_20_test.dart` (B1-B3, all pass; failing-first: did not compile on the base, `windowSlide` undefined).
- 61.3 (c) Feed fills holes in short-EPG answers — BUILT, with two clarifications. New enum `IptvEpgSource.mixed` (last, `lib/services/iptv/iptv_epg_sources.dart:1004`; old persisted files stay readable via the `unknown` fallback); chip `lib/screens/livetv/tabs/guide_tab.dart:3131` ("Mixed") and detail-sheet label `lib/screens/livetv/epg_channel_detail_sheet.dart:86` ("Short EPG + feed"); i18n `epgSourceMixed` / `epgSourceMixedDetail` (`lib/i18n/en.i18n.json:3289-3290`). `_mergeFeedIntoHoles` (`lib/media/iptv_live_tv.dart:2031`, replacing deleted `_mergeFeedTail`) generalises the tail merge: H1 replaces an untitled programme when a feed programme overlaps >=50% of its duration (max overlap wins, ties earliest start); H2 fills gaps >=10 min (trailing gap filled regardless of size when short-EPG hit its limit — the D4 tail case, byte-identical programme list); never overwrites, truncates, or re-times a titled programme; dedupes by (start, title); on non-cancellation exception returns short EPG unchanged. Guard `_feedRowsAvailableFor` (`:1971`): the feed is consulted only when its rows are already available (`_xmltvCache` or a valid store) — no download, no slide, no parse. CLARIFICATION 1 (guard): the AT-LIMIT answer is exempt from the guard — the old D4 tail merge fetched the feed unconditionally for a truncated answer, and D4 requires that byte-identically; the guard governs H1 and interior-H2 only (`:2051`). CLARIFICATION 2 (untitled): `_mapShortEpgListings` maps an empty title to the `unknownProgram` placeholder, so the merge treats a title as untitled when it is empty OR equals that placeholder. `fromMixed` bucket added to `IptvGuideCoverage` (`lib/media/iptv_live_tv.dart`, class `:57`, counted in `guideCoverage`) and to `IptvGuideCoverageSample` (`lib/services/iptv/iptv_epg_sources.dart:322`, counted in `sampleGuideCoverage`) — pulled forward from (d) into (c) because the `mixed` enum broke both exhaustive switches. Tests: `test/services/iptv/iptv_feed_holes_1_2_20_test.dart` (C1-C7, all pass; C7 is the mixed round-trip: a merged entry persists as `mixed` and is served from disk after `dropEpgFromMemory`, with no re-download/re-parse) plus the "Mixed" chip widget test in `test/screens/livetv/guide_tab_epg_source_test.dart` (C7, passes alongside F8); failing-first proven on the 1.2.19 base (C: 3 pass / 4 fail — the file compiles and runs on the base; tester correction 2026-11-15: the earlier 'does not compile' claim was wrong). D4 tail tests in `test/services/iptv/iptv_epg_reliability_test.dart` pass (15/15).
- 61.4 (d) Honest Reload sample — BUILT, with one deviation. `IptvGuideSampleScope {visible, matched, guideOrder}` (`lib/services/iptv/iptv_epg_sources.dart:322`); `IptvGuideCoverageSample.scope` (default `guideOrder`); `sampleGuideCoverage` (`lib/media/iptv_live_tv.dart:4734`) samples the registry first (most-recent first, deduped, up to 200), then the matched channels, then the first 200 in guide order; `_matchedFeedChannels` (`:4691`) computes the matched set pre-bust from the currently cached feed guides (`_xmltvCache` windows via `_windowCandidateIds` with an empty mappings map), stopping at `sampleSize` matches; doc comments on the class and the function fixed (the "visible channels" comment was the lie this item fixes). DEVIATION (PARTIAL vs the doc's letter): the doc says the matched fallback uses "the Reload's own match results (channels with `matchedXmltvId != null`)", but `sampleGuideCoverage` runs FIRST in `reloadTvDetails` — before the bust AND before the match loop (`:5520`) — so those results do not exist at sample time; the pre-bust `_xmltvCache` computation is the closest correct thing. Dialog: the existing key `epgReloadGuideCoverageSample` extended with `mixed` + `scopeLabel` params (`lib/i18n/en.i18n.json:3239-3242`, scope labels `sampled rows on screen` / `matched channels` / `rows in guide order — guide not opened`); call site `lib/screens/settings/iptv_epg_sources_screen.dart:522`; Reload log line also gains `mixed` + `scope`. Test seam `debugClearRecentGuideFetches` (`lib/media/iptv_live_tv.dart:1006`) so D2 can exercise the fallback without waiting out the 60 s registry window. Tests: `test/services/iptv/iptv_reload_sample_1_2_20_test.dart` (D1-D4, all pass; D3 is the guideOrder control, D4 also asserts the rendered dialog line); failing-first proven on the 1.2.19 base (the file does not compile — `mixed`, `IptvGuideSampleScope`, `scope` undefined). Known fuzz (stated honestly, per the doc): the registry covers rows the guide built (viewport + cache extent, ~1 screen beyond), not strictly the pixels on screen.
- 61.5 Verification + UNPROVEN (phone gates). `flutter analyze` = 50 = base (20 new issues found and fixed in-session: 2 errors — an existing verdict test calling the extended i18n key without `mixed`/`scopeLabel` — plus 18 infos/warnings: sqlite3 `dispose()` -> `close()`, single-statement while/if braces, underscore locals, an unused helper/import, and a `prefer_initializing_formals` file-level ignore on the writer constructor, whose public named params map to private fields). W7 TEST UPDATE (not a masking): `iptv_epg_windowed_lazy_test` W7 asserted `debugRetainedFeedPrograms == 0` ("Reload retains no rows") — (b) intentionally retains one window after a Reload (the first-screen slide), so the assertion now bounds it (`<= _windowPrograms` and `< _totalPrograms`, the W2 pattern); the metadata-only intent stands (the match counting loads no rows beyond that one slide). Full suite (serialized, per-directory): `test/services/iptv/` 741/741 all pass; `test/media/ + test/models/ + test/i18n/ + test/utils/ + test/providers/` 1707 pass, ~6 skipped, 3 fail — all 3 pre-existing (verified identical on the 1.2.19 base: download_provider Jellyfin hydration, media_image_helper 4K budget, memory_pressure_breadcrumbs); `test/connection/ + test/data/ + test/database/ + test/focus/ + test/metadata_edit/ + test/mixins/ + test/mpv/ + test/navigation/ + test/profiles/ + test/theme/ + test/watch_together/ + test/widgets/ + test/startup_bootstrap_test.dart` 2171 pass, ~1 skipped, 2 fail — both pre-existing (verified identical on base: cycling_media_backdrop, tv_browse_rail_scroll_bridge); every test file importing the changed modules passes (55/55, incl. the EPG settings/load/reload screens and storage_health); `test/screens/iptv/iptv_screens_test.dart` 12/14 — the same 2 pre-existing failures as the base (catalog-mode row, EPG settings `pumpAndSettle` timeout). `test/screens/` as a whole was NOT run to completion (killed after ~14 min at +233 -5 — the build13/build14 Libraries Trex-scale tests each hit the 10-min timeout; all 5 failures seen are pre-existing, incl. the catalog_search debounce). The remaining un-run area is the non-iptv `test/services/` subdirs, which no changed module touches. Regression nets kept green: `iptv_epg_coverage_sample_1_2_15_test`, `iptv_build26_epg_streaming_test`, `iptv_epg_cache_test` (its "stale feed file" failure under parallel load is the known timing flake — passes standalone 21/21), `iptv_reload_report_content_1_2_17_test`, `guide_tab_epg_source_test`, D4 tail tests. UNPROVEN for the phone (Fold): max RSS vs 606 MB on 1.2.19; time from Reload to rows showing programmes; time from opening the guide to rows showing programmes (cold start); the Reload dialog's sample line and "Guide rows ready" line; a `mixed` row in the wild; the store file size in the debug log; the `feed-store-build ms` number on the Fold (the 5 s budget is measured in-harness only). Trex-scale numbers above are VM numbers, not phone proof.

## 62. Updater auto-detect holes + version line under Download + TV debug-log save to Downloads (v1.2.21-2.25.0, versionCode 215)

- Why: the owner 2026-11-16 ~00:06 EST — "make a build to set up the auto updater to be able to pick between the three, add a second option I asked to be like what version of build right below it", then clarified "Have it try to auto detect which one it needs to grab"; his decision (tapped the option ~00:08 EST) was "Just show the version under the download button" — no manual APK picker. Same night ~00:11 EST — he tried the emergency debug-log save on his Hisense TV and no file appeared: "I need you to save it to like downloads or some shit. This is for the TV." Design doc `~/workspace/tmp_work/plezy-tv-research-updater-abi-1.2.21/RESEARCH_UPDATER_ABI_1.2.21.md` (495 lines). (a) BUILT (one deviation from the doc's sketch, see 62.1), (b) BUILT (one deviation, see 62.2), (c) BUILT. Version `1.2.21-2.25.0+215` (`pubspec.yaml:4`); `about.changelog2307` has 3 plain-words entries (`lib/i18n/en.i18n.json`), plus the About screen now lists 1.2.21 AND the 1.2.20 entry (see 62.3).
- 62.1 (a) Updater holes — BUILT. CORRECTION TO THE BRIEF (found by the research agent, confirmed in code): the brief's premise was wrong — L-9 (1.2.19) ALREADY built auto-detect (`UpdateService.deviceAbiName()` maps `Abi.current()` to `arm64-v8a` / `armeabi-v7a` / `x86_64` / `x86`; `selectApkAsset()` picks the asset whose name contains the device ABI). This build fixes its two holes: U1 — the match was a substring (`contains('x86')` matched the `x86_64` asset, offering a 64-bit Intel APK to a 32-bit Intel device), now a delimited match (`RegExp('(?:^|[-.])' + RegExp.escape(abiName) + r'\.apk$')`, case-insensitive); U2 — a split-only release with no asset for the device fell back to the first asset (a ~100 MB download the installer refuses with `INSTALL_FAILED_NO_MATCHING_ABIS`), now count-first rules: 0 .apk assets → no download (`apkAssetNoMatch: false`, the release simply has no APK); exactly 1 → offer it (fat, `apkAssetAbi: null`); 2+ (split) → ABI match wins, else NO download offered (`apkAssetNoMatch: true`); a tokenless fat asset shipped alongside splits is still offered. New map keys `apkAssetAbi` / `apkAssetNoMatch`; optional `Abi? deviceAbi` seam threaded through `_performUpdateCheck` → `selectApkAsset` (`debugPerformUpdateCheck` accepts/forwards it; production call sites pass nothing — `Abi.current()` stays the detector, NOT `device_info_plus` `supportedAbis` or a platform channel, per the doc's §2.2 proof). DEVIATION (doc's sketch vs the real base): the doc sketched `selectApkAsset(List<dynamic>)` with a `firstWhereOrNull` body and `deviceAbiName()` returning `String?`; the real base is `selectApkAsset(Object? assets, {Abi? deviceAbi})` returning `Map<String, Object?>` with a loop + first-asset fallback, and `deviceAbiName()` returning `String` (`''` for unknown) — the build keeps the base's shape (loop collecting the .apk assets first, then the count-first rules) and updates the doc comments that described the old fallback (the `deviceAbiName` comment and the `_performUpdateCheck` asset comment). The existing test at `test/services/update_service_test.dart:434` asserting the old fallback was UPDATED to the U2 expectation (not deleted). Tests: `test/services/update_service_test.dart` L-9 group extended with U1–U8 (39/39 pass; failing-first on the 1.2.20 base: U1–U4 failed, U6–U8 passed, U5 did not compile — no `deviceAbi` seam).
- 62.2 (b) Version line + no-match message — BUILT. `_versionLine` getter (`lib/utils/update_dialog.dart`): last child of the dialog content Column, directly above the action buttons, visible in all four phases (info/downloading/downloaded/failed): `Download: Version 1.2.21-2.25.0 · arm64-v8a · 95.4 MB` (matched, size known); ABI/size omitted when absent (fat fallback: `Download: Version 1.2.21-2.25.0 · 95.4 MB`); nothing renders when there is no download to offer (no-apk and no-match). New i18n key `update.noApkForDevice` — "This update has no build for this device — open the release page to get the right one." (`noApkAttached` NOT reused — a split-only release with no build for the device is a different dead end from a release with no APK at all). DEVIATION (doc's D1/D3 example vs code): the doc pins the line as `… · 100.1 MB` for the 1.2.20 arm64 size (100,059,051 bytes), but the existing helper `formatDownloadBytes` is 1024-based and renders that size as **"95.4 MB"**, not "100.1 MB" (the doc's number is decimal). The build follows the doc's COMPOSITION rule exactly (label + version + ABI + `formatDownloadBytes(size)`) and asserts "95.4 MB" in D1/D3 with the real size; `formatDownloadBytes` was NOT changed (it also drives the download progress display). No actions-row / button-label / autofocus / downloader changes (Install keeps autofocus in the downloaded phase — asserted in D1). Tests: `test/utils/update_dialog_test.dart` extended with D1–D5 (9/9 pass; failing-first on base: D1×2, D2, D3, D5 failed, D4 control passed). D1b's focus assertion walks the primary focus up to the Install `DialogActionButton` — `Focus.of` on the label's element finds the Material button's inner Focus, which never has focus (`descendantsAreFocusable: false` on the wrapper), so that assertion was rewritten, not the code.
- 62.3 (c) TV debug-log save to Downloads — BUILT. Root cause (verified in code at `d74723048`): `AutoDumpService.writeDump` writes `pleztv-autodump.txt` to the app-specific external files dir (invisible to the user), and `writeDumpToPicker` uses the SAF save picker — absent on Android TV, so the caller fell back to the app-folder write and the user saw nothing saved. New Dart service `lib/services/downloads_media_store.dart` (`writeFile({fileName, bytes, mimeType})`, `debugWriteOverride` seam): stages bytes to a temp file (a MethodChannel call cannot carry a ~500 KB payload reliably), invokes the new Kotlin channel `com.pleztv.app/downloads` method `writeToDownloads` with `sourcePath`/`fileName`/`mimeType`, deletes staging in `finally`, never throws (any failure reads as null → app-folder fallback). New Kotlin `android/app/src/main/kotlin/com/pleztv/app/DownloadsChannel.kt` (following `ApkInstallChannel.kt`, field at `PlezyActivity.kt:168`, attach at `:1241`): MediaStore.Downloads insert with `DISPLAY_NAME`, `MIME_TYPE`, `RELATIVE_PATH = Download/`, `IS_PENDING` during write; deletes any previous same-name file owned by the app first; queries back the inserted row's `DISPLAY_NAME` (MediaStore may suffix on collision) and returns the exact public path; SDK guard `< Q → null` (MediaStore.Downloads needs API 29+; minSdk is 25 — old devices fall back to the app folder); catch-all → null, never throws across the channel. `AutoDumpService.writeDumpToDownloads` (build text + L-21 storage-full check + Downloads write + counter reset on success) and `writeDumpToDownloadsOrAppFolder` (Downloads first, then `writeDump`; a storage-full verdict from the Downloads attempt stands — same disk). The saver screen's TV primary button now calls `_saveToDownloadsOrAppFolder` (was `_saveToAppFolder`); the picker-failure path tries Downloads once before showing the failure hint; the explicit "Save in the app folder" secondary button is unchanged. `DebugLogExportService.exportToFile`: the TV branch tries Downloads first, falls back to `_writeToAppDocuments` (`debugUseDownloadsOverride` seam — the host cannot be a TV); `LogsScreen._exportDebugFile` shows the exact saved path in the success snackbar on TV (`"Debug file saved: <path>"`, generic line elsewhere). Never requests MANAGE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE (standing rule kept). W6 TEST UPDATE (not a masking): `auto_dump_startup_test` W6 asserted the TV primary saved to the app folder — that IS the behaviour this item changes, so W6 now asserts Downloads-first (override returns a fake Downloads path; no app-folder file; counter resets) and the fallback is covered in the new screen test. ABOUT GAP FOUND AND FIXED: the About screen's changelog list never had a 1.2.20 entry — `changelog2306` shipped in 1.2.20 but About still started at 1.2.19 — so 1.2.21 adds both `1.2.21-2.25.0 → changelog2307` and the restored `1.2.20-2.25.0 → changelog2306` (`lib/screens/settings/about_screen.dart`). Tests: `test/services/downloads_media_store_test.dart` (5/5, failing-first: did not compile on base — no service); `test/services/auto_dump_test.dart` DL1–DL5 (22/22 incl. DL1–DL5; failing-first: did not compile on base); `test/services/debug_log_export_service_test.dart` DL6–DL7 (12/12; failing-first: did not compile on base); `test/screens/auto_dump_safe_mode_screen_test.dart` (3/3 — TV Downloads success, TV app-folder fallback, picker failure → Downloads; failing-first proven by stashing the screen change: the 2 Downloads tests failed on the old screen, the fallback passed); `test/services/auto_dump_startup_test.dart` 14/14 (incl. updated W6). DL2's first draft asserted the counter was 3 after 3 `failedLaunch()` calls — wrong arithmetic (the first launch in a fresh test has no abnormal predecessor, so 3 launches yield counter 2); DL1/DL2 now capture the counter before/after instead of pinning the number.
- 62.4 Verification + UNPROVEN (phone gates). `flutter analyze` = 50 = base (3 new infos found and fixed in-session: unnecessary imports — `dart:typed_data` in `downloads_media_store.dart` + its test, `flutter/foundation.dart` in the safe-mode screen test). Failing-first proven for every new test on the 1.2.20 base (see 62.1–62.3). Regression nets: every test file importing a changed module passes except the two verified pre-existing failure sets below — `update_service_test` 39/39, `update_dialog_test` 9/9, `auto_dump_test` 22/22, `downloads_media_store_test` 5/5, `debug_log_export_service_test` 12/12, `auto_dump_safe_mode_screen_test` 3/3, `auto_dump_startup_test` 14/14 (incl. updated W6), `debug_summary_test`, `storage_health_test` and `settings_screen_test` all pass; `test/utils/ + test/i18n/` 893 pass, 2 fail — both pre-existing (recorded in 1.2.20 §61.5: media_image_helper 4K budget, memory_pressure_breadcrumbs); `test/services/iptv/` 740 pass, 1 fail — the R2 scorer latency bench, a known timing flake (passes standalone 7/7, common median 33 ms, rare 75 ms); `test/startup_bootstrap_test.dart` 25/25; `test/screens/iptv/iptv_screens_test.dart` 12/14 — the same 2 pre-existing failures as the 1.2.20 record (catalog-mode row, EPG settings). Two files fail identically on the base (verified by stashing the change and re-running): `test/screens/settings/logs_screen_test.dart` 2 pass / 19 fail (the known logs_screen ×19 class — the failing set is byte-identical to base) and `test/android/network_security_config_test.dart` 3 pass / 1 fail (the pre-existing `releasesUrl` github.com host not covered by the system-only domain list — in the base since before 1.2.21, not touched by this build). UNPROVEN for the phone (Fold + Hisense TV): the updater offers the arm64 APK on the Fold and the armeabi-v7a APK on the TV, with the version line under Download; the dialog fits on TV; install over the top works on both; skip-version and the 6-hour cooldown behave as before; no re-offer after a skip; the TV debug-log save lands in Downloads with the path shown; the Kotlin Downloads channel compiles (CI release build is the proof — no local Kotlin compile in this VM, same as the other channels); the `changelog2307`/`changelog2306` About entries render. Panel sweep: no Trex/panel values (URLs, usernames, passwords, tokens) anywhere in the diff, tests, or fixtures — the only `example.com` URLs are the standard fake-asset placeholders already used by the base tests.

## 63. Phone-log fixes (v1.2.22-2.25.0, versionCode 216)

- Why: the owner's emergency debug log 2026-11-16 ~02:41 EST (1.2.19, Fold) — the saver fired after 3 failed starts; the last real crash was still the old 1.2.15 OOM, but the log showed four live bugs (feed cache write failing, a short-EPG retry storm, a black/frozen HW video path, a "Future not completed" timeout leak) plus a stale previous-exit record. Design doc `~/workspace/tmp_work/plezy-tv-research-phone-log-fixes-1.2.22/RESEARCH_PHONE_LOG_FIXES_1.2.22.md` (370 lines). Second dump 2026-11-16 10:35:59Z (1.2.21, Fold) re-confirmed all four bugs on 1.2.21 (feed rename PathNotFound 00:26:07; the same channel's short-EPG requests aborting 7× in ~8 s at 07:54; yuv420p + the same six AVOption rejections 17:26:01; M3U "Future not completed" 00:27:17) + the stale previous-exit (the header shows the 02:44 package_updated line, not the 05:35 exit) + the same UK Sky Sports foreign samples; no new bug class. EPG 3,396–3,443 with guide (feed drift). 1.2.21 session healthy (startup 226 MB, max 290 MB in the visible window). Provider-side event, NO action: HTTP 403 accessDenied at 05:35:08, the app reported it, restart 05:35:56 — 403 handling is out of scope for 1.2.22. Scope verdict: ALL of it in 1.2.22 (bugs 1–4 + 6; bug 5 investigation-only, no fix); the watchdog did NOT grow past estimate (~90 lines + tests), so nothing slipped to 1.2.23. Version `1.2.22-2.25.0+216` (`pubspec.yaml:4`); `about.changelog2308` has 9 plain-words entries (5 phone-log + 4 EPG, see §64) — +1 backup (§65) + 2 upstream (§66) = 12 total.
- 63.1 (Bug 1) Feed cache write — BUILT. Root cause (verified in code): the promotion `tempFile.renameSync(feedFile.path)` in `_loadXmltvGuide` assumed the destination dir exists, but `_xmltvFeedFile`/`_epgCacheDir` never create it — and `clearEpgCache()` deletes the whole `iptv_epg/<connection>` dir fire-and-forget while a download is in flight, so the rename hit `PathNotFoundException` and the feed was never persisted. New `@visibleForTesting static bool promoteFeedFile(File tempFile, File feedFile, String cacheKey)` (`IptvLiveTvSupport`, before `_xmltvFeedFile`): creates `feedFile.parent` recursive + verifies the temp file still exists before the rename + a plain-words warning on failure ("the downloaded feed file is gone, so the guide will download again next time") + returns bool. The call site in `_loadXmltvGuide` now calls it (the outer try/catch kept). DEVIATION (doc's step 2 not built): the deferred recursive clear was NOT implemented — step 1 alone makes every timing of the race safe (delete during download/parse → the error propagates cleanly through the existing feed-failure path; delete between parse and promote → the temp check fires → plain warning + in-memory serving + re-download next cold start), and a deferred delete is racy against the refresh flow (it can wipe a fresh feed that started after the clear). The doc sanctions step 1 as the fix with step 2 as optional belt-and-braces. XML and the 1.2.20 SQLite row store still rebuild together (a failed promotion leaves an invalid, rebuildable store, never half-present — `IptvFeedRowStore.isValid` requires the XML file). Tests: `test/services/iptv/iptv_epg_1_2_22_feed_cache_test.dart` 1a–1d (4/4 pass; 1d locks the end-to-end landing in).
- 63.2 (Bug 2) Short-EPG retry storm — BUILT. Root cause (verified in code): `_fetchXtreamEpg` created ONE AbortController per call and registered it in `_epgAbortsByChannel[channelKey]` — a second concurrent call OVERWROTE the first's registration — and there was no per-channel in-flight join at the support level (the guide tab's `_iptvEpgInFlight` dedup only covers tab-started fetches). New state near `_epgAbortsByChannel`: `_epgFetchInFlight` (join key → shared future), `_epgFetchWaiters`, `_epgAbortHistory` (pruned to 10 s), `_epgAbortCooldownUntil`. `fetchEpgForChannel` now joins on `'$channelKey|${from}|${to}'` (check-then-register with no await between, so atomic; `force: true` bypasses join and cooldown); the network body lives in `_fetchEpgForChannelNetwork`, whose catch logs a failure exactly once + records the abort once (`_noteEpgAbort`) + rethrows; the D7 generation-stale serve-old fallback moved to the waiter catch. Cooldown: 3 aborts in 10 s → no refetch until the window passes or a tap-retry (`force`), one warning per burst ("aborted N times in 10 s — backing off until …"); during cooldown a non-forced fetch THROWS `MediaServerHttpException(type: cancelled, message: 'EPG fetch paused after repeated cancellations; retrying shortly')` — NOT `[]`, because the guide tab marks `[]` as loaded (no refetch) but treats a cancellation as "not failed, refetch on scroll-back". Cancel: `cancelEpgFetchForChannel` decrements waiter counts and aborts the registered controller only when no waiters remain on any of the channel's fetches (M3U has no waiter → no abort; the shared feed is stopped only by `cancelBackgroundWork`). Cancellation logging demoted to debug (≤1 warning per burst). Real bug found during testing: `unawaited(networkFuture.whenComplete(...))` creates a derived future that errors when the network fetch errors, and nobody handled it → Dart reported it as unhandled; fixed by appending `.then<void>((_) {}, onError: (_) {})` to the cleanup future (same hazard as Bug 4's timeout future). Tests: `test/services/iptv/iptv_epg_1_2_22_short_epg_join_test.dart` 2a–2c + F3 (4/4 pass; 2b waits a real 10.5 s for the cooldown to lift).
- 63.3 (Bug 3) MPV HW video path — BUILT. DEVIATION (doc's prune list vs the real bundled ffmpeg): the dump's AVOption errors fired on the NATIVE h264 decoder (software-fallback path), not a mediacodec open. FIX ROUND (tester NO SHIP): the first version of this paragraph was WRONG — it verified against vanilla FFmpeg 8.0.1, but the bundled ffmpeg is the PATCHED one (mpv-build 3b9f38af, the AAR's lock commit — artifacts.json ties AAR key 2f74669e1ec7 to that commit; series.android applies the patches). Patch 0111 registers `frame_rate` (AV_OPT_TYPE_FLOAT, feeds MediaFormat "frame-rate") and patch 0012 registers `ndk_async` (not `async`) + `priority` on the mediacodec decoder. Corrected decision: `frame_rate` is RESTORED (1.2.21 behaviour — the codec gets the rate from it); `async` is left as-is (the name is bogus, it errors "Option async not found" on every mediacodec open and async decode is never enabled — renaming to `ndk_async` ENABLES async decode, a real behaviour change on API 31+, so it is a follow-up with a phone gate, not 1.2.22). KEEP `ndk_codec`, `dolby_vision`, `dv_p7_mode`, `async`, `priority`, `frame_rate`; DROP NOTHING from `vd-lavc-o` in 1.2.22. (3a) VOD memo: `_openMediaOnPlayer` (`lib/screens/video_player/parts/playback_open.dart`) gains optional `String? decoderMemoKey`; before `openMedia` it sets `DecoderFailureMemo.currentChannelKey = decoderMemoKey` and consults `shouldStartOnGlVo` → passes `startOnGlVo` to `player.open` (mirrors the live path in `live_tv.dart:393–396`); `_openResolvedMedia` passes `decoderMemoKey: 'vod:${metadata.serverId ?? 'local'}:${metadata.id}'`; `video_player_screen.dart` dispose now clears `currentChannelKey` UNCONDITIONALLY (dropped the `if (widget.isLive)` guard — the VOD open sets the same key). Threshold 2 / 7-day TTL / clean-decode-clears unchanged. (3b/3c) Black-frame note: `PlayerState` gains nullable `decoderFallbackNote` + `copyWith` sentinel; `PlayerStreams` gains the `decoderFallbackNote` stream (controller wired in `createStreams()`, closed in `closeStreamControllers()`); `PlayerBase` gains `decoderFallbackNoteText = "This video can't use this device's hardware decoding — switching to the way that works"` + a 3 s timer (`_decoderFallbackNoteDelay`) + `_decoderFallbackPending`; armed on 'start-file'/'file-loaded', set on 'vo-format-rejected' (pending + re-arm, so a late rejection still names itself — the note only ever sets when a rejection actually happened, never for a slow network start), cleared on 'playback-restart' (frame rendered) and on a new file. The screen shows it via `_wirePlayerStreams` (`playback_services.dart`) → `showAppSnackBar` (6 s, same pattern as L-11 triage). Tests: `test/mpv/decoder_failure_memo_1_2_22_test.dart` 3a–3d (9/9 pass; 3d is a source-level guard — the decoder cannot run in the VM, do NOT fake a decoder repro). FIX ROUND: test 3d was rewritten — it now guards that `frame_rate` IS written (the prune was wrong); 9/9 pass. The decoder failure itself remains device-only.
- 63.4 (Bug 4) "Future not completed" — BUILT. Root cause: `diagnoseM3uTvgIds` → `getM3uToFile` → `_fetchBodyToFile` has a 120 s per-chunk-gap timeout, but the timeout arrived re-wrapped (type no longer `TimeoutException`, so `epgFeedErrorReason`'s typed check missed it and the raw text leaked) and `compute(parseM3uFile)` had NO overall timeout. `epgFeedErrorReason` (`lib/services/iptv/iptv_epg_sources.dart:1140`): message-text mapping added BEFORE the typed checks — `error.message` for a `MediaServerException`, else `error.toString()`, lowercased; contains `timed out` or `future not completed` → `t.errors.reasonTimedOut` ("the connection timed out"). Never uses `toString()` for a `MediaServerException` (its toString appends host+path). `diagnoseM3uTvgIds`: new `_fetchAndParseM3uPlaylist(tempPath)` helper bounded by `@visibleForTesting static Duration m3uTvgIdDiagnosticTimeout = 150 s` via `.timeout(..., onTimeout: throw TimeoutException('M3U tvg-id check timed out after 150 s'))`, with a late-error swallow (`unawaited(fetchFuture.then<void>((_) {}, onError: (_) {}))`) so a post-timeout error is never unhandled; the failure log now includes `${e.runtimeType}`. The spinner clears: `_checkM3uTvgIds` (`lib/screens/settings/iptv_epg_sources_screen.dart:655` — the doc's `lib/screens/iptv/...` path was wrong) sets `_checkingM3uTvgIds = false` in BOTH paths before the dialog, and `diagnoseM3uTvgIds` never throws by contract — so the spinner clears as long as the diagnostic returns, which the timeout guarantees. Tests: `test/services/iptv/iptv_epg_1_2_22_m3u_tvgid_test.dart` 4a–4c + F4 (4/4 pass). The exact re-wrapped error type stays unproven until runtimeType logging lands in a dump that reproduces it.
- 63.5 (Bug 5) ANR — investigation-only, NO fix. Most likely cause (main-thread feed parse) already fixed by 1.2.18 (parse moved to isolate); the ANR was in the 1.2.15 session (pre-isolate). No other main-thread block identifiable; no backend ANR report (Sentry ANR detection off, `_enableSentry` defaults false, `lib/main.dart:131`). Its action is the item-6 watchdog.
- 63.6 (Bug 6) Debug-log completeness — BUILT. Root cause (verified in code): `AndroidExitDiagnostics.logPreviousExit()` is called at `lib/main.dart:1283`, inside normal startup — but the saver decision at `lib/main.dart:233–235` happens FIRST, so on a saver launch it never runs and the dump's "Previous exit" line is STALE (it showed the 2026-11-15 09:02 SIGKILL at the 16:29 startup); also gated on the `crashReporting` setting with silent absence. New `lib/services/main_thread_watchdog.dart` (~90 lines): singleton `MainThreadWatchdog.instance`; `Timer.periodic` 2 s heartbeat, `note(label)` API, a ring of (timestamp, label) persisted to a small JSON file (sync, best-effort, last 16 entries); `readRingSnapshot()` (tolerant parse, `[]` on missing/corrupt); pure `summaryLine(ring, {now})` (empty → 'Main thread: no heartbeat record'; gap > 5 s → 'Main thread: unresponsive since <ISO>, last activity <label> (<N>s ago)'; else 'Main thread: last heartbeat <ISO> (<label>)'); `@visibleForTesting debugBeatForTesting` / `debugResetForTesting` / `debugDirectoryOverride`. The watchdog starts ONLY in `_runNormalStartup` (`MainThreadWatchdog.instance.start()`, unawaited) — never on a saver launch, so the previous run's ring survives for the dump. `AndroidExitDiagnostics` gains `readPreviousExitReport()` (channel read + validate, NO logging/sending) + `holdPreviousExitReport()` + a held report consumed once by `logPreviousExit()` (existing signature kept). `markStartupPhase` now notes `'startup:${phase.id}'` into the watchdog first (no-op until the watchdog starts, so a saver launch's phase marks don't touch the previous run's ring). `main()`: reads the report BEFORE the saver decision + holds it + reads the ring snapshot + records the native reason via `_recordLaunchAndCountFailedStarts(previousExitReason:)` → passes report + ring to `_runAutoDumpSafeMode` → the saver screen (`previousExitReport` / `watchdogRing` widget params) → all three write calls (`writeDumpToPicker` / `writeDumpToDownloadsOrAppFolder` / `writeDump`) → `buildDumpText` → `buildDebugText` → `buildSummarySection`. STAMP DECISION (the researcher's open decision, settled conservative): the stamp approach — when crash reporting is off the dump stamps "Previous exit: record omitted (crash reporting off)" instead of silently omitting it; main() still reads the report (the native record is one-shot per exit; not reading it loses it the same as reading+discarding, and the stamp is more informative). SUPERSEDED by §66 U4 in the same release: U4 removes the `crashReporting` setting entirely, so there is no "off" state to stamp — the `crashReportingEnabled` param and the stamp branch were removed with it, the report is now always included, and test 6d was rewritten to assert that (fresh report → the reason is in the dump; log-scan fallback without a report still answers). The rest of Bug 6 (early read, hold, watchdog, flush, persisted reason) is unaffected. `buildPreviousExitSummaryLine` gains `previousExitReport` + `crashReportingEnabled` params (fresh report wins; `formatPreviousExitReport` renders the same fields the log line carries); `buildSummarySection` gains `previousExitReport` + `watchdogRing` params + the watchdog line + the Failed-starts line now carries the persisted reason (`(last exit: <reason>)`). `LastCrashStore` carries `previousExitReason` (String?, in the JSON) through `_readStartupStateSync`/`_writeStartupStateSync` + `markLaunchedAndCountFailedStarts({previousExitReason})` (keeps the previous reason when a launch records none) + `readPreviousExitReason()` (mirrors `readFailedStartCount()`). Flush: public `FileLogOutput.flush()` (`lib/utils/app_logger.dart`, wraps private `_flush()`); `AutoDumpService.buildDumpText` calls it before `readFullLog()`. DEVIATION (doc's second flush site doesn't exist): the manual export (`logs_screen._formatAllLogs`) builds from the in-memory `MemoryLogOutput.getLogs()`, NOT from the file — `readFullLog()` has exactly ONE production caller (`buildDumpText`), so there is no second file-read call site to flush. RuntimeType logging: `readPreviousExitReport` and `logPreviousExit` now log `(${e.runtimeType})` at debug level on failure (same class as Bug 4). Tests: `test/services/debug_log_1_2_22_test.dart` 6a–6d + store-reason + full-dump integration (7/7 pass). NOT testable in the VM: the held-report consumption (the channel read returns early off Android) and the main() early-read wiring itself — verified by analyze + review, and by the device.
- 63.7 Relief valve — AUDIT ONLY, no fix. 0 MB freed ×6 is CORRECT behaviour (caches already empty at fire time). Audit (verified in code): the drop list is `_evictImageCaches()` (`PaintingBinding.instance.imageCache.clear()` + `clearLiveImages()`) + `_serverManager.dropIptvCachesFromMemory()` (for every connection: `dropCatalogFromMemory()` — live/VOD/series memos — + `dropEpgFromMemory()` — guide + per-channel EPG cache), shared by the RSS watchdog path and the native `onTrimMemory` path (`_onNativeTrimMemory`, gated by `evictionForTrimLevel`). Everything else resident is not droppable from Dart: the SQLite feed row store and the disk index are disk, not RAM; MPV's native buffers must not be dropped while playing; the Dart heap frees itself once the references drop. Nothing missing.
- 63.8 Verification. New tests 39/39 across the 8 §63/§64 files (feed-cache 4, join 4, M3U 4, matcher 7, fallback 2, sweep 2, decoder 9, debug-log 7) + backup 4/4 (§65.7) + log-submit 17/17 (§66.8) = 60 new tests, all passing; failing-first verified for every file on base c7a242401 via `git stash push --keep-index` (assertion failures, or load failures for the files that introduce new APIs — feed-cache, M3U, decoder, debug-log, backup, log-submit). `test/services/iptv/` full suite: 761 pass / 1 fail — the 1 is `iptv_catalog_cache_test` ("a write in flight when the cache is cleared does not resurrect the file"), a load-induced flake: the whole file passes standalone 22/22, same class as the known timing flakes. mpv + decoder-memo + video-player net: 2 failures on 344b1d3b3 — `live_media_session_test` ("a replacement stream failing before the live start commits is recovered once it does"), which fails identically standalone AND on base — PRE-EXISTING — and `playback_reload_failure_test` ("TV background suspend: a suspend under the Play Next prompt restores the prompt and its countdown"), a REGRESSION (fails 5/5 on the new commit, passes 4/4 on base — the §63.3 open-path change shifted async scheduling so the pre-existing unmocked `window_manager` channel surfaced; test-harness-only, no user impact). FIX ROUND: the `window_manager` mock was added to that file (sibling pattern) — the file now passes 26/26 on two full runs; the tester's net therefore becomes 543 pass / 1 fail (live_media_session only, pre-existing) — the full net was not re-run in this round; the only net file touched is the fixed one. Auto-dump/startup/debug-log net: 51/51. `test/screens/livetv/` half A: 56 pass / 12 fail; half B: 51 pass / 4 fail — ALL 16 failures proven pre-existing on base in one stash round (cancel test + idle_refetch test fail on base; `guide_tab_iptv_test.dart` +7 -10 on base, the same 10 `pumpAndSettle timed out`; the 4 mini-player failures fail on base — the 3 build17 aspect-ratio tests each time out at 10 min, plus the first-frame wiring test). The L3 sweep therefore does NOT slip: it caused none of them. `test/utils/` + `test/i18n/`: 2 failures, both pre-existing on base (`memory_pressure_breadcrumbs_test` — extra `rssMbAfter` key; `media_image_helper_test` 4K backdrop cap, Expected (1440, 2160) / Actual (1080, 1620)). `test/android/network_security_config_test.dart`: 3/4 — the 1 failure is pre-existing on base (`https://github.com/cartchai/pleztv/releases` in `update_service.dart` not covered by the config; not my code). `test/screens/iptv/iptv_screens_test.dart`: 2 failures, the same 2 as the 1.2.21 record, both failing identically on base. `test/screens/settings/logs_screen_test.dart`: 20/21 — the 1 failure is the app-bar D-pad traversal test, proven pre-existing on base (fails with only the setUpAll SettingsService-init fix applied to the base file). `flutter analyze` (full package, after the §66 work): 50 issues = base (5 new issues found after the §66 work — 3 unused imports + 1 unused field + 1 unused test parameter, all leftovers of the U3/U4 cuts — fixed; re-run confirms 50). Panel sweep re-run after the backup + §66 work: CLEAN — only `panel.example.com` / `example.com` / `example.test` fixture hosts + public CDNs (image.tmdb.org) + i18n prose; no real panel values anywhere in the diff, tests, or fixtures; the one "Trex" hit in `fork_backup_service.dart:54` is a code comment ("the app still never talks to Trex at startup"), not a value. UNPROVEN (phone gates): the 4 log bugs actually fixed on device (feed persists across cold starts; no retry storm; the HW-decode note appears and the VOD memo sticks; the M3U diagnostic completes with a plain phrase); the watchdog ring + fresh previous-exit line in a real saver dump (the held-report consumption and the main() early-read wiring cannot be exercised in the VM — the channel read returns early off Android — verified by analyze + review only); Bug 4's exact re-wrapped error type (runtimeType logging will name it in a dump that reproduces it); the updater + version line + install-over-the-top + TV debug-log save (1.2.21 gates, still owed). FIX ROUND (follow-up commit on 344b1d3b3): corrected the §63.3 frame_rate prune (restored — patch 0111 proves it real; `async` left as-is, the `ndk_async` rename is a follow-up with a phone gate), added the `window_manager` mock to playback_reload_failure_test (26/26 on two full runs), softened changelog2308 entry 12 to scope it to the three removed items (U2 still posts until 1.3.23); `flutter analyze` re-run: 50 = base; panel sweep re-run over the fix-round files: CLEAN.

## 64. EPG matcher bug + layered fallback ("No single basket") (v1.2.22-2.25.0, versionCode 216)

- Why: the owner 2026-11-16 ~02:51 EST — TiviMate matches guide data for (nearly) every channel and he never has to second-guess it, while PlezTV showed 3,426 of 26,150 with guide. His text answer (~03:05 EST) settled it: TiviMate uses the DEFAULT EPG source (the provider's, auto-bound from the Xtream login); he added extra sources but very rarely uses them — the current provider handles most of it. So extra sources are NOT the explanation; same feed, same login — the gap is the matcher + short-EPG handling. Design doc `~/workspace/tmp_work/plezy-tv-research-epg-tivimate-gap/RESEARCH_EPG_TIVIMATE_GAP.md` (282 lines). The ~3,600–3,800 "honest ceiling" claim is DISPUTED (the owner) — do NOT cite it as fact. Priority order: L2 matcher bug + L3 short-EPG first, then L5 diagnostic. L4 (user-added sources) stays opt-in only; do not bundle anything; L4 is manual-assignment only, never an automatic fallback layer for the Trex playlist. Device evidence (Reload dialog, 1.2.20, the owner ~05:40 EST): 26,094 channels · 3,245 with guide · 22,849 without; match manual 0, tvg-id 2,530, name 697, loose 18; no match ambiguous 292, foreign 455, no-tvg-id 21,591, tvg-id-not-in-feed 5, matched-but-no-programmes 506 (all 506 declared-but-unscheduled, 0 outside-window); all channels incl. hidden 8,996 with guide of 55,158. Only 2,902 of 26,094 live channels carry a non-empty epg_channel_id (down from 3,235 in the dumps) — the id tier is shrinking, which makes the L2 name/loose fix and L3 short-EPG more important, not less. Feed: 5,672 channels (4,293 with programmes), 288,348 parsed, 280,102 kept; download 90.2 MB, new download, UA IPTVSmartersPro. Sample: "0 of 200 rows in guide order — guide not opened (short guide: 0 · feed: 0 · mixed: 0 · no data: 3 · never fetched: 197)" — the 1.2.20 sample behaving as designed (falls back to guide order when the guide was not opened, honestly reports never-fetched); this is the coverage problem itself, not a sample bug. The true visible-row number is still owed: it only appears when Reload runs after the guide has been opened. Unmatched sample re-confirms the §64.1 bug in the wild: UK| SKY SPORT NEWS — "Name matches only a guide channel from another country" (the foreign block); plus NL| ZIGGO SPORT 4 HD matched-but-unscheduled, PL| CANAL+ SPORT 2 ambiguous, UK| LONDON LIVE id-not-in-feed (LondonLive.uk).
- 64.1 (F1/F2) Matcher bug — BUILT. Real, traced (UK Sky Sports): `_matchInGuide` (`lib/media/iptv_live_tv.dart:2791`) tier 5 returned `blocked('foreign')` immediately when a channel's only same-key twin was foreign, and that return SKIPPED the loose tier entirely. Trace: playlist `UK| SKY SPORT NEWS` normalizes (main key) to `sky sport news`; feed twin "Sky Sports News" main-keys to `sky sports news` (the main key did NOT fold sports→sport — only the loose key did); tier 5 saw the NZ "Sky Sport News" twin (countries `{nz}`), the country filter emptied the set → `blocked('foreign')`; tier 6 (loose) would have found the UK twin but never ran. Fix: new `@visibleForTesting String sportsFoldedEpgChannelNameKey(String name)` (main words + `_epgLooseWordForms` fold, NO tier-tag strip) next to `normalizeEpgChannelName`/`looseEpgChannelNameKey`; `_channelsByDisplayName` and `_channelsByNormalizedName` now index BOTH the main key and the sports-folded key for each entry (folded added only when different; the loose index was already folded, unchanged); tier 5 queries `nameCandidatesFor(key)` UNION `nameCandidatesFor(foldedKey)` and the early `return blocked('foreign', sorted)` is replaced by recording into a new `foreignNameCandidates` set + `nameCandidates = {}` + fall-through; tier 6 excludes foreign candidates (`looseCandidates.removeAll(foreignNameCandidates)`) and adds in-country loose twins that aren't serving to `identifiedButEmpty` (so the end reports matched-but-no-programmes, not foreign); the end of the function returns `blocked('foreign', sorted)` only after the `identifiedButEmpty` block, before `tvg-id-not-in-feed`/`no-tvg-id`. The uniqueness gate (`ambiguous` — two live in-country twins are never guessed) is untouched, and `normalizeEpgChannelName` itself is NOT changed (existing pinned assertions `normalizeEpgChannelName('AR| BEIN SPORTS 01 FHD') == 'beinsports1'` etc. are untouched). TEST UPDATE (not a masking — deliberate behavior change): `test/services/iptv/iptv_epg_matching_1_2_8_test.dart` F5 ('US - FOX SPORTS 1 HD' → 'Fox Sport 1') and F4+F5 ('AR| BEIN SPORT 01 FHD' singular → 'beIN Sports 1') previously asserted `matchMethod == 'name-loose'`; the F2 fold now matches them at tier 5, so both assert `'name'` (test names updated too). The matched channel is unchanged — only the method moved up a tier, which is exactly what F2 specifies. Tests: `test/services/iptv/iptv_epg_1_2_22_matcher_test.dart` F1, F1b, F1-reverse (lock-in, passes on base), F2, F2b (5/5 in the file before L0; F1b's fixture id must NOT normalize to the key — first draft used `sky-sports-news.uk`, which matched via tier 5 through its normalized ID key, so the id is `uk-999` to isolate tier 6).
- 64.2 (F7) Hole-fill — BUILT. `_mergeFeedIntoHoles` H1: when the whole short-EPG answer is untitled (`titled.isEmpty`), the 50% overlap threshold is dropped — any titled feed programme overlapping the slot (>0) wins (max overlap). Partially-titled answers keep the threshold. The merge returns source `mixed` when `fromFeed > 0` (verified in code). Test F7 in the matcher test file (feed pre-loaded via a prior fetch of channel 103 — the merge guard `_feedRowsAvailableFor` only consults an already-loaded feed). TEST UPDATE (not a masking — deliberate behavior change): `test/services/iptv/iptv_feed_holes_1_2_20_test.dart` C4 ('an untitled programme stays untitled when the feed has no overlap') previously had the feed programme straddling the slot end with a 5-min overlap (<50%, so old H1 rejected it); under F7 that 5-min overlap now fills the hole, so the fixture moved 'Elsewhere' to start exactly when the untitled slot ends (0-min slot overlap, 5-min gap overlap <10 min so H2 still rejects it). The test still pins "the feed genuinely has nothing for the hole" — now at the exact boundary the new rule draws.
- 64.3 (F5) L3 bounded idle sweep — BUILT, did NOT slip to 1.2.23. `lib/screens/livetv/tabs/guide_tab.dart`: sweep state + methods after `_retryIptvEpg` — `_epgSweepIdleDelay = 2 s`, `_epgSweepChannelsPerPass = 24`, `_epgSweepMaxPasses = 5`, `Timer? _epgSweepIdleTimer`, `int _epgSweepPassesDone = 0`; `_scheduleEpgSweep()` (cancel + re-arm 2 s); `_hasUnfetchedEpgChannels()` (skips non-IPTV, loaded, in-flight, failed); `_runEpgSweepPass()` (no-op if unmounted or passes exhausted; if `IptvPlaybackActivity.instance.isPlaying` → reschedule WITHOUT spending a pass; else `_ensureIptvEpg` for unfetched ones up to 24 — `_ensureIptvEpg`'s own loaded/in-flight/failed guards do the filtering; increment passes; schedule another pass if passes remain and unfetched channels remain). Triggers: `initState` end; `didUpdateWidget` on channel change (reset `_epgSweepPassesDone = 0` + reschedule); `_onGuideVerticalScroll` top (reschedule before the `_guideScrolledPastFirstScreen` early return — scrolling resets the idle clock); `dispose` (cancel timer). Tests: `test/screens/livetv/guide_tab_epg_sweep_1_2_22_test.dart` F5a/F5b (2/2 pass; F5b proves the sweep does not run while playing and resumes when idle). Failing-first verified via `git stash push --keep-index`: both F5 tests FAILED on base (`00:12 +0 -2`), then `git stash pop` restored the work. L3 hardening (Bug 2's join + cooldown) is §63.2; the visible-rows + neighbour-margin fetch on open is the existing `_ensureIptvEpg` row-build path, unchanged.
- 64.4 (F6) Layered fallback — VERIFIED IN CODE, locked in by tests, no new code needed. `_loadXmltvGuide` rethrows on download failure; the fallback lives in `_xmltvGuideForKey`'s warm-start path: a persisted feed (row store or XML) serves immediately and a background refresh is started when stale, with failures swallowed (`.catchError((_) {})`). `_loadEnabledFeeds` records per-source failures and skips failed sources (Reload carries them in `feedOutcomes`). `refreshEpgCache()` calls `clearEpgCache()` which DELETES the persisted feed — so it cannot be used to set up the F6 scenario. `fresh()` is `ttl == null || now - fetchedAt < ttl`; a persisted guide's `fetchedAt` comes from `xmlFile.lastModifiedSync()` — so the test ages the persisted feed with `setLastModifiedSync` (default update interval 24 h). Tests: `test/services/iptv/iptv_epg_1_2_22_fallback_test.dart` F6a/F6b (2/2 pass; F6a fetches a NEVER-fetched channel 200 — fetching channel 100 on the cold support hits its per-channel cache and never consults the feed, so no refresh starts).
- 64.5 Per-row "Guide data from" accounting — VERIFIED IN CODE, already built. `channelEpgDetail` returns the cached entry's `matchMethod` and `epgSource`; the details sheet (`lib/screens/livetv/epg_channel_detail_sheet.dart`) shows source/method and has Assign/Clear/Refresh; the Reload dialog has the per-source breakdown (`IptvReloadTvDetailsResult`, `lib/services/iptv/iptv_epg_sources.dart:879`); `sampleGuideCoverage` (1.2.20) splits by source. No new code. F8 needs no new test — already pinned by `test/services/iptv/iptv_epg_coverage_sample_1_2_15_test.dart`.
- 64.6 (L0) Learned mappings, ask-first — BUILT. Verified in code: the only `saveMapping` call on the fetch path's support is inside `assignEpg` (explicit) — no fetch path persists. Only EXPLICIT Assign EPG picks persist automatically; a loose match the user merely taps/watches must ASK FIRST before persisting (parent's decision). New i18n key `epgKeepLooseMatch`: "Keep this guide for this channel" (`lib/i18n/en.i18n.json`, slang regenerated); the details sheet gains `_keepLooseMatch()` (calls `assignEpg(channelKey, xmltvId: matchedXmltvId, sourceId: sourceId)` → snackbar `epgAssigned` → reload) + a full-width `FilledButton.icon` shown only when `matchMethod == 'name-loose' && _assignment == null && matchedXmltvId != null && sourceId != null`. The one-line flip to auto-persist would be: call `assignEpg` inside the loose-match branch of the fetch path instead of showing the button — deliberately NOT done. Real gap found and fixed: an assignment did NOT take effect on the next fetch — `force: true` does NOT bypass the per-channel cache (`fetchEpgForChannel`: `servable` ignores `force`; force only bypasses the feed's 5-min negative cache), so a kept loose match kept showing `name-loose` for up to 24 h. Fix: `assignEpg` and `clearEpgAssignment` now drop the channel's cached entry (new `_dropCachedEpgForChannel`: `_epgCache.remove(channelKey)` + delete the persisted per-channel file, best-effort) so the next fetch re-matches through the mapping. This fixes the existing Assign EPG flow too. Test L0 in the matcher test file (loose match → no assignment; explicit `assignEpg` → assignment with `xmltvId 'uk-999'`; plain fetch → programmes serve + `matchMethod == 'manual'`). L4 (user-added sources) needs no new code in this release: the add-source UI exists (`_addSource`, `lib/screens/settings/iptv_epg_sources_screen.dart:284`), the feed-resolution loop tries each loaded guide with `_matchInGuide` and returns the first match with `sourceId: attempt.config.id` (per-row attribution), and the existing test at `test/services/iptv/iptv_epg_fixes_1_2_14_test.dart:296` covers add-source end-to-end. No bundled community feed; G3 stays cancelled.
- 64.7 (L5) M3U tvg-id diagnostic — BUILT as §63.4 (bounded 150 s timeout, message-text mapping, runtimeType logging, spinner clears). Test F4 in the M3U test file (scripted M3U + feed → checked=3, noId=2, gained=1, gainedInFeed=1, feedLoaded=true, 1 sample `bbc.uk` inFeed).
- 64.8 Verification. Matcher file 7/7 (F1, F1b, L0, F1-reverse lock-in, F2, F2b, F7); fallback file 2/2 (lock-ins — pass on base, as designed); sweep file 2/2 (failing-first on base +0 -2 via stash). Updated old tests: the UPDATED F5/F4+F5 fail on base (base gives `name-loose`) and the UPDATED C4 passes on base (boundary lock-in) — both verified by checking the updated test files out of the stash onto the base tree. The L3 sweep did NOT slip to 1.2.23 — it is built, its tests pass, and the regression comparison proves it caused none of the 16 open livetv failures (all pre-existing on base, see §63.8). UNPROVEN (device-only): whether the panel answers `get_short_epg` for all 26,151 stream ids; whether M3U tvg-ids actually cover the feed; H4 (UA change + Reload compare) is the next device check after the build — a phone check, NOT a code change; the real visible-row coverage number (run Reload on 1.2.22 after the guide has been opened); memory cost of the L3 sweep on the Fold (judge by peaks, must not regress the 606 MB 1.2.19 figure); feed persists across cold starts after the cache-write fix.

## 65. Full backup / restore (v1.2.22-2.25.0, versionCode 216)

- Why: the owner 2026-11-16 ~06:12 EST — the app already has an upstream Plezy backup, but he believes it does NOT back up the fork's stuff. Correct: the audit (65.1) proves the upstream backup covers only the portable settings registry — no connections, no logins, no EPG mappings, no favourites, no resume. Purpose: this is what makes the 1.3.23 move possible — back up on 1.2.22, fresh-install 1.3.23 (new package), restore everything. Cross-build rule (the owner ~06:14 EST): the 1.2.22 backup MUST restore on 1.3.23 across the package change — format is package-agnostic (no package name, no absolute paths), versioned + FROZEN between the two builds, and 1.3.23 ships only after a named cross-build restore test passes (1.2.22 backup → fresh 1.3.23 install). The rename changes ONLY the applicationId/bundle ID, never storage keys, Hive box names, or database filenames. Version `1.2.22-2.25.0+216` (`pubspec.yaml:4`); `about.changelog2308` gains a 10th line for the backup.
- 65.1 Audit — what the upstream backup covers (verified in code at `c7a242401`). `SettingsExportService` (format v1, `plezy-settings-YYYYMMDD.json`, `lib/services/settings_export_service.dart`): exports ONLY `SettingsService.portablePrefs` (+ user-scoped library keys `hidden_libraries` / `library_filters` / `library_order` + `library_*` patterns). Its own doc comment says the exclusions are deliberate: "credentials, runtime state, device paths, history, endpoints, and user-authored player configuration are intentionally absent." Import validates + rolls back and re-scopes `user_{uuid}_` keys to the current user. UI: Settings screen export/import buttons (`lib/screens/settings/settings_screen.dart:652-662`, injectable `widget.settingsExporter` / `widget.settingsImporter` seams). What it does NOT cover (the fork gap, all verified): (a) connections — Drift `Connections` table in `plezy_downloads.db` (the app's single database file, `lib/database/app_database.dart:1434`), Xtream (serverUrl/username/password + hiddenCategoryIds/hiddenCategoryInitials) + M3U (playlistUrl/epgUrl + hidden lists) + Plex/Jellyfin/Emby, passwords/tokens vault-protected (`enc:v1:`) at the registry boundary (`CredentialVault.protectConnectionConfig` / `revealConnectionConfig`, `lib/connection/connection_registry.dart:77,145`); (b) EPG prefs NOT in portablePrefs — `iptv_epg_days_to_load`, `iptv_epg_update_interval_hours`, `iptv_epg_past_days`, `iptv_epg_update_on_start`, `iptv_epg_time_offset_minutes`, `iptv_epg_update_time_minutes`, `iptv_epg_update_on_playlist_change` (already portable: catalog mode, sync interval, wifi-only, catch-up badge, both user-agent keys); (c) EPG per-connection stores (SharedPreferences) — `iptv_epg_sources:<id>` (user-added XMLTV sources), `iptv_epg_added_sources_first:<id>`, `iptv_epg_mapping:<id>` (manual + learned mappings, `IptvEpgSourcesStore`, `lib/services/iptv/iptv_epg_sources.dart:428+`); (d) favourites — `iptv_fav_channels:<id>` + `iptv_fav_items:<id>` (SharedPreferences JSON, `lib/media/iptv_live_tv.dart:793`, `lib/media/iptv_client.dart:1868`); (e) resume — `iptv_resume:<id>` (`lib/media/iptv_client.dart:1903`); (f) profiles — Drift `Profiles` + `ProfileConnections` tables (local `pinHash` in configJson; Plex Home userTokens vault-protected, `lib/profiles/profile_connection_registry.dart:108,131,237-238`). No Hive anywhere in the app (no `package:hive` import) — the "never rename Hive box names" constraint is vacuous here, recorded so the 1.3.23 programmer does not invent one. Database filename that must not change: `plezy_downloads.db`. Storage keys that must not change: every `iptv_*` SharedPreferences key above + every `SettingsService` pref key.
- 65.2 Format (FROZEN v1 — do not change between 1.2.22 and 1.3.23). New service `ForkBackupService` (`lib/services/fork_backup_service.dart`), file `pleztv-backup-YYYYMMDD.json`, top level `{format: 'pleztv-backup', formatVersion: 1, appVersion, exportedAt, platform, prefs, epgPrefs, connections, profiles, profileConnections, epg, favorites, resume}`. Package-agnostic by construction: NO package name, NO absolute paths, NO device-specific paths anywhere (the export carries settings by key and connections by id; restore writes into whatever the current app's storage is). `prefs` is the upstream `buildExportMap` prefs map verbatim (import rebuilds `{formatVersion: 1, prefs}` and calls upstream `applyImportMap` — same validation, same rollback, same `user_` re-scoping to the fresh install's uuid). `connections` carry the DECRYPTED config (`toConfigJson()` after the registry's reveal — the file contains the logins, as briefed); import re-protects via `upsert` → `protectConnectionConfig` under the fresh install's vault key, preserving connection ids exactly (favourites, resume, mappings and EPG stores are all keyed by connection id — a new id would orphan them). `profileConnections` carry the decrypted userToken (empty stays empty). `favorites` / `resume` copy the raw SharedPreferences JSON strings verbatim per connection (byte-faithful; corrupt values are skipped, never crash the import). `epg` per connection: sources (`IptvEpgSourceConfig` toJson), `addedSourcesFirst`, mappings (`IptvEpgMapping` toJson). EXCLUDED deliberately: catalog index + disk cache + EPG feed cache/row store (derived — see 65.3), `iptv_epg_last_updated` / `iptv_epg_last_attempt` (runtime timestamps), `iptv_epg_source_status` / `iptv_epg_channel_fingerprint` (derived), downloads + `DownloadedMedia`/`DownloadQueue`/`ApiCache`/`OfflineWatchProgress`/`SyncRules` (files or operational state, not user state), `tvos_db_recovery_*`, device paths (`custom_download_path`). Unknown future sections are ignored on import; `formatVersion > 1` is rejected.
- 65.3 Catalog index — EXCLUDED, fresh sync after restore (the parent's option, chosen over including it). Trade-off: the export stays small and portable (the index is ~tens of MB of derived rows plus the search index) and never carries stale provider data — but the first use after a restore needs a catalog sync (minutes on Trex scale, same as a fresh login). The sync is user-initiated (restore is user-initiated), so it is consistent with the startup rule (no Trex at startup). After a successful import the Settings screen shows a summary dialog with the per-section counts; the dialog's "Open Live TV settings" button takes the user to the IPTV settings screen, where "Sync catalog now" (`_syncCatalogNow`, `lib/screens/settings/iptv_settings_screen.dart:440`, same progress dialog + Cancel) reloads the catalog. The sync itself is not auto-started — the user chooses when.
- 65.4 Security (hard). The export file contains the logins in plain text (decrypted at export). The app shows a plain-words warning BEFORE exporting ("This backup file contains your logins. Keep it private — never share it, never upload it.") and the export never auto-uploads anywhere (the service makes no network call at all). Tests use SYNTHETIC credentials only — never the real login, never real panel values, never in a report, test, or fixture. The Trex ban applies in full. On TV, export writes to Downloads via `DownloadsMediaStore` (same MediaStore pattern as the 1.2.21 debug-log save) with the app-folder fallback; import uses the file picker (TV import is UNPROVEN — the picker is absent on Android TV; the 1.3.23 move is phone-first).
- 65.5 Import order + verification. `applyBackupMap` restores in this order: connections first (everything else is keyed by connection id) → profiles → profile connections → active profile (restoring it is what gives the prefs import the user scope it needs on a fresh install — `applyImportMap` re-scopes `user_` keys to the fresh install's scope; when no profile is active the prefs section is skipped and counted, never written under a wrong scope) → upstream prefs → EPG prefs → per-connection EPG stores → favourites → resume. Import verifies by re-reading every section (re-list connections/profiles/profile-connections, re-load EPG stores, re-read favourite/resume strings and EPG prefs) and returns per-section counts + mismatches (`ForkBackupRestoreReport`); a section that fails never aborts the rest. The Settings screen shows the counts in plain words ("3 connections · 1 profile · 3 settings · 7 guide settings · 1 guide store · 1 favourites · 1 watch position") plus a line when anything was skipped. The service file was written missing its imports and its closing brace and did not compile — fixed in the same commit (missing `connection_registry`, `profile_connection`, `media_backend`, `media_browser_dialect`, `path_provider`, `services` imports; unused `credential_vault` import removed).
- 65.6 Tests. `test/services/fork_backup_service_test.dart` (4 tests): (a) full round-trip — seed synthetic Xtream + M3U + Plex connections, a local profile + profile connection + active profile, portable + user-scoped prefs, all 7 EPG prefs, an EPG store (1 user-added source — the provider is implicit and filtered on load — precedence, 1 mapping), favourites + resume → export → assert the file has no package name and no absolute paths → wipe (fresh in-memory DB + fresh prefs + a DIFFERENT mock package name, simulating the 1.3.23 applicationId change) → import → assert every section's counts and every re-read value (logins incl. password round-trip through the vault, hidden lists, tokens, mappings, raw favourite/resume strings); (b) `exportToFile` + `importFromFile` round-trip through the `debugDownloadsWriter` / `debugFilePicker` seams; (c) malformed files rejected (`ForkBackupException` for bad JSON, wrong format, formatVersion 99, missing formatVersion); (d) corrupt entries skipped, valid parts still restore (unknown connection kind, corrupt favourites string, corrupt mapping). The cross-package simulation the harness CAN do: separate storage roots + different mock package names — the import never reads PackageInfo at all, so the guarantee is structural (nothing package- or path-dependent is in the file). The separate tester verifies; the 1.3.23 tester repeats the cross-build restore on the real new build.
- 65.7 Verification. `test/services/fork_backup_service_test.dart` 4/4 pass (round-trip, file seams, malformed, corrupt). The tests caught 3 real bugs before the commit: (1) the export wrote an EPG entry for EVERY connection, so the import counted 3 "guide stores" when only 1 held data — fixed export-side (a connection with no user sources, no mappings and default order gets no entry, so the counts and the summary stay honest); (2) the same shape in favourites/resume (null/null entries counted as restored) — fixed the same way; (3) the test itself expected `loadUserSources()` to return 2 sources, but the provider is implicit and filtered on load — only the 1 user-added source comes back (test fixed, service correct). Failing-first: the file fails to LOAD on base (the service doesn't exist there). Cross-package simulation: separate storage roots + different mock package names (`com.example.plezytv` → `com.example.plezytv2`, version 1.3.23-2.25.0); the import never reads PackageInfo at all, so the guarantee is structural (nothing package- or path-dependent is in the file). UNPROVEN: the Settings screen backup tiles have NO widget test — the service-level tests cover the logic and the separate tester + the owner's phone verify the UI; TV import (the picker is absent on Android TV — phone-first); the real 1.3.23 cross-build restore (the 1.3.23 tester repeats it on the real new build); the catalog sync after restore on Trex scale (minutes, user-initiated, never auto-started).

## 66. No traffic to the upstream creator's servers (v1.2.22-2.25.0, versionCode 216)

- Why: the owner 2026-11-16 ~06:43 EST, standing rule — NEVER send anything to the upstream Plezy creator's servers. Reframed ~06:45 EST: "Nothing goes to Plezy, everything comes to us now." Research doc `~/workspace/tmp_work/plezy-tv-research-upstream-traffic/RESEARCH_UPSTREAM_TRAFFIC.md`. The live app talked to the creator's servers in exactly three places, one host (`lib/services/plezy_server.dart:4`, `https://ice.plezy.app`): (1) `/logs` — diagnostic-log upload (U1, REPLACED in 1.2.22); (2) `/auth/*` — OAuth proxy for MAL + AniList tracker sign-in only (U2 — NOT 1.2.22, see below); (3) `/posters` — Discord Rich Presence poster upload, desktop only (U3, CUT in 1.2.22). CORRECTION to the earlier brief: there is NO Plex OAuth proxy — Plex sign-in is direct PIN auth against `plex.tv` / `clients.plex.tv` / `app.plex.tv` (`lib/services/plex_auth_service.dart:53-54,167-169`) and never touches the creator. Everything else the app talks to is fine (plex.tv + subdomains, the user's own Plex/Jellyfin/Emby/Seerr servers, the IPTV provider, `api.github.com/repos/cartchai/pleztv/releases/latest` for the update check — MUST change to `cartchai/plezytv` at the repo move, same for `_githubRepo` and `releasesUrl` in `update_service.dart`, api.trakt.tv, api.simkl.com, data.simkl.in, api.mdblist.com, api.myanimelist.net, graphql.anilist.co, image.tmdb.org, cdn.jsdelivr.net, `wss://{na,eu,asia}.relay.openwatch.party`, youtube.com trailers, dontkillmyapp.com). `about.changelog2308` gains 2 more lines (U1 + U4) = 12 total.
- 66.1 (U1) Log submit → OUR GitHub via a Cloudflare Worker relay — BUILT (replaces, does not just remove, the old upload). Design: the app POSTs the redacted log to OUR Worker; the Worker files the issue on `cartchai/pleztv` (later `plezytv`) with a fine-grained PAT stored as a Worker secret — same pattern as the already-deployed Job Tracker Together Worker (same account, free tier); the token never ships in the app and the app never talks to GitHub directly. The embedded-PAT approach was only planned, never coded — so the Worker design was chosen, not the embedded PAT. Worker contract (documented in `lib/services/log_submit_service.dart`): POST `{title, body}` JSON to the Worker URL; the Worker files the issue and answers 201 `{issueUrl, issueNumber}`; the Worker enforces the 64 KiB body cap defensively and should rate-limit (public endpoint — anyone who finds the URL can file issues; the PAT's blast radius is issues:write on one repo only). New service `lib/services/log_submit_service.dart`: `logSubmitEndpoint = String.fromEnvironment('PLEZTV_LOG_SUBMIT_URL', defaultValue: '')`; `canSubmitLogs`; `maxLogSubmitBytes = 60 * 1024` (GitHub body cap 65,536 chars; the byte budget is conservative); `submitDiagnosticLog({title, logText, sensitiveValues, client, endpointOverride})` → `SubmittedLogIssue({url, number?})` (StateError when no endpoint is configured; redacts then trims; POSTs JSON `{title, body}` via `MediaServerHttpClient`; non-2xx → StateError with status; parses `{issueUrl, issueNumber}`, fallbacks `html_url`/`number`; missing URL → StateError); `buildLogSubmitBody` (splits header/logs at `'\n---\n'`, constrains, wraps in a code fence + footer "_Submitted from PlezTV. The log was redacted on this device before sending._"); `constrainDiagnosticPayload` (the old `constrainLogUploadPayload` algorithm renamed — header intact, newest complete lines, never splits a UTF-8 sequence); `redactDiagnosticLogForSubmit(text, {extraSensitiveValues})` (pure, unit-tested); `collectSensitiveValuesForLogSubmit(AppDatabase db)` (best-effort — a registry that can't be read contributes nothing; pattern redaction still applies). REDACTION — exactly what is stripped, in order: (1) extras literals (longest first, skip <3 chars); (2) `LogRedactionManager.redact` (registered URLs/tokens/secrets + IPv4 masking + `*.plex.direct` server-hash masking + sensitive-field values — authorization, cookie, x-plex-token, x-emby-token, api-key, auth-token, access-token, refresh-token, password, passwd, secret, pin, token, in header/query/JSON/Dart-map forms + URL userinfo); (3) emails; (4) `username=`/`user_name=`/`userName=`/`email=` query params (the manager does NOT cover `username=` — the Xtream login travels as `player_api.php?username=...&password=...`); (5) any URL host outside the allowlist → `https://[REDACTED_HOST]` (allowlist mirrors the fixed endpoints minus `plezy.app`, plus `workers.dev` where our Worker lives: plex.tv, api.github.com, github.com, image.tmdb.org, cdn.jsdelivr.net, api.trakt.tv, trakt.tv, api.myanimelist.net, myanimelist.net, graphql.anilist.co, anilist.co, api.simkl.com, simkl.com, data.simkl.in, simkl.in, api.mdblist.com, openwatch.party, www.youtube.com, youtube.com, dontkillmyapp.com, workers.dev); (6) `enc:v1:` vault blobs; (7) desktop home-dir paths (`/home/<name>/`, `/Users/<name>/`, `C:\Users\<name>\`). `collectSensitiveValuesForLogSubmit` switches on all 4 Connection subtypes (Plex: accountToken, clientIdentifier, accountLabel, active-profile title/username/email/friendlyName/uuid, each server's name/clientIdentifier/accessToken + each PlexConnection's uri + address; Jellyfin/Emby: baseUrl + each baseUrls entry + host, serverName, userName, accessToken; Xtream: name, serverUrl+host, username, password; M3U: name, playlistUrl+host, epgUrl+host) + every Profile.displayName + every ProfileConnection userToken + userIdentifier. RESIDUAL RISK (stated in the code): a bare prose mention of an identifier from a connection/profile that no longer exists in the registry (not in extras) and matching no pattern could survive — e.g. a stale log line naming a deleted server. The in-memory log buffer is short-lived, so the window is small; if zero risk is ever required, submit to a private repo instead. Old service DELETED: `lib/services/log_upload_service.dart` (endpoint const, `uploadDiagnosticText`, `constrainLogUploadPayload`, `maxLogUploadBytes` all gone with it). Logs screen (`lib/screens/settings/logs_screen.dart`): `_uploadLogs` replaced by `_submitLogs` — endpoint pre-check → not-configured snackbar (`t.messages.logSubmitNotConfigured`); best-effort sensitive-values gather via `context.read<AppDatabase>()` (try/catch → pattern-only fallback; LogsScreen is pumped bare in widget tests with no providers, so the catch is the test path); full `_formatAllLogs()` (no maxBytes — the service trims); `showLoadingDialog`; success dialog mirrors the old Log ID dialog but shows the issue URL (label `t.messages.githubIssue`, monospace 14, copy button copies the URL, Close button); failure → `t.messages.logsUploadFailed` snackbar; ctor gains an optional `logSubmitEndpoint` seam (production uses the build-time const; tests pass their fake endpoint). `startup_failure_view.dart`: same replacement (key `startupFailureUploadKey` → `startupFailureSubmitKey`, `_uploading` → `_submitting`, `_uploadDetails` → `_submitDetails`, title 'Startup failure — PlezTV', pattern-only redaction — no DB available there, the record is allowlisted/redacted by design; optional `logSubmitEndpoint` ctor seam). i18n: `logs.uploadLogs` "Upload Logs" → "Submit log to GitHub"; `logs.uploadLogsDescription` → "Sends your debug log — redacted on this device first — to our GitHub repo as an issue, so it comes to us instead of the app maker's server"; `messages.logsUploaded` → "Log submitted"; `messages.logsUploadFailed` → "Couldn't submit the log"; `startup.uploadDetails` → "Submit details to GitHub"; ADD `messages.githubIssue` + `messages.logSubmitNotConfigured` "Log submit isn't set up in this build yet."; DELETE `messages.logId` (only used in the two old upload dialogs). CI: build line 67 now passes `--dart-define=PLEZTV_LOG_SUBMIT_URL=${{ vars.PLEZTV_LOG_WORKER_URL}}` (a repo VARIABLE, not a secret — the URL is public; the PAT lives server-side in the Worker; an undefined variable expands to empty, safe to land before the Worker exists). PARENT STILL TO DO (not code): deploy the Worker; create the fine-grained PAT (issues:write on `cartchai/pleztv` only, stored as a Worker secret — never in the app or repo); set the GitHub repo VARIABLE `PLEZTV_LOG_WORKER_URL` to the Worker URL. Until the variable is set the endpoint is empty and the button explains log submit is not set up — nothing is posted anywhere in that state, and local save/copy always work. (The Codemagic backup builder would need the same define if ever used.) `api.github.com` is already a fixed endpoint (network_security_config.xml + CertificateTrust) for the release check — the app never calls it directly now (the Worker does), so no config change for U1. `plezy.app` stays in both (U2). Tests: `test/services/log_submit_1_2_22_test.dart` R1–R9, trim ×2, body, S1, S2, S2b, S3, S4 (17/17 pass). FALLBACK (not needed — the Worker design shipped): if the GitHub replacement had slipped, the upload would have been CUT in 1.2.22 (local save/copy only) and GitHub landed in 1.3.23.
- 66.2 (U2) Tracker OAuth proxy — NOT CUT in 1.2.22. `OAuthProxyClient` (`lib/services/trackers/oauth_proxy_client.dart`) still posts to `plezy_server.dart`'s base URL for MAL + AniList sign-in only (`POST /auth/start` sends only `{"service": "mal"|"anilist"}`; the proxy holds the client secret and exchanges the code server-side; the device long-polls `GET /auth/result?session=X` for tokens). Cutting it without a replacement in the same build would kill new MAL/AniList sign-ins (existing MAL keeps working — refresh is direct; existing AniList works until its token expires ~1 year, no refresh). 1.3.23 options: self-host the relay (`server/`, Go, in-repo — one-constant change, needs a public host + TLS + our MAL/AniList OAuth apps) or direct flows (MAL direct PKCE needs our MAL app registration; AniList direct needs a bundled secret — Trakt precedent — or a self-hosted exchange). Trakt/Simkl/MDBList are unaffected (direct device-code flows). This is a KNOWN, time-boxed carve-out from the standing rule, not a silent leftover.
- 66.3 (U3) Discord poster upload — CUT. `lib/services/discord_rpc_service.dart`: deleted `_posterUploadUrl`, `_maxPosterUploadBytes`, `_posterUrlCache`, `_CachedUrl`, `posterCacheExpiryFromResponse` + `_defaultPosterCacheTtl`/`_maxPosterCacheTtl`, `_uploadThumbnail`, `_uploadThumbnailAndUpdatePresence`, `_presenceThumbPath`, `_buildTranscodedThumbnailUrl`, `_absolutePosterUrl`, `_buildLargeImageText`, `_cachedThumbnailUrl` field + assignments, `_playbackRevision` field + increments; `_updatePresence` → `largeAsset: null` + comment; `startPlayback` doc rewritten ("Presence is text-only: no artwork is uploaded anywhere."); pruned imports: `plezy_server.dart`, `media_server_http_client.dart`, `media_image_helper.dart`. Real regression found and fixed during testing: the pause test failed after the cut — the old upload's async hop had DEFERRED the start's publish, so a pause issued in the same tick won and no card was ever posted; with the cut, the start published synchronously before the pause ran, so the card flashed. Fix: `startPlayback` now publishes on the next microtask (`unawaited(Future.microtask(_updatePresence))`) with a comment explaining why (pause-in-the-same-tick must win; the music engine binds-then-pauses a restored/car-restricted session). The deferred publish still checks live state when it runs, so a stop/dispose in between leaves nothing posted (`_currentMetadata == null` / `_rpc == null` guards). The fake's `clearPresence()` only increments a counter and never removes from `presences` (append-only log), which is why the test encodes "pause must win" as `presences` empty. Tests: `test/services/discord_rpc_service_test.dart` — deleted the `posterCacheExpiryFromResponse` group (3 tests); added `expect(presence.largeAsset, isNull)` in the 'video keeps the Watching activity' test (8/8 pass). 1.3.23 options: TMDB poster URL when the item has one; self-host relay `/posters`; Worker + R2.
- 66.4 (U4) Sentry — CUT (the DSN was DEAD in shipped builds). `ENABLE_SENTRY` defaults false, no CI job ever sets it, so `SentryFlutter.init` never ran and the hub was a no-op. Deleted from `lib/main.dart`: `_enableSentry`, the DSN constant (value never reproduced in any tool call — removed by line-prefix matching), `_sentryEnvironment`/`_sentryDist` (kept `gitCommit` between them), the `if (_enableSentry)` init block (`SentryFlutter.init` + `_optionalGatePhase(StartupPhase.crashReporting, ...)`) + `_sentryRelease()` + `_sentryNativeDatabasePath()`; `holdForFlush(flushPendingStartupFailure())` → `holdForFlush(acknowledgePersistedStartupFailure())` with a rewritten comment; replaced `_crashReporterReady` + `flushPendingStartupFailure` + `_captureStartupFailure` with `acknowledgePersistedStartupFailure()` (peeks the persisted record; if it exists and is unreported → `markReported(record)`; no send, no params); deleted `_beforeSend` + `_beforeBreadcrumb`; `_logEnvironmentDiagnostics`: deleted the `Sentry.configureScope(...setTag('renderer'...))` line (kept the renderer log line); `logPreviousExit` gate (`if (settings.read(SettingsService.crashReporting))`) → unconditional call + comment; connection-load catch: deleted `Sentry.captureException` + updated comment ("the log line above is the record — there is no crash reporter any more"); startup-bootstrap failure comment updated (flush → local acknowledgement); `_registerAppLicenses` doc (dropped the Sentry references); fatal-error catcher comment (dropped the "(Sentry's, when enabled, chains to this one)" parenthetical); `_primeDiagnosticsVersion` doc ("a value only Sentry consumed" → "a value only the (now-removed) crash reporter consumed"). The `sentry_flutter` import STAYS in main.dart (the kept `Sentry.addBreadcrumb` calls need it). `lib/utils/android_exit_diagnostics.dart`: deleted the `Sentry.captureMessage` block in `logPreviousExit`; deleted `sentryMessageFor` + `_failureReasons` + `_importancePerceptible`; updated the `logPreviousExit` doc (local logs only) + the held-report comment + `markTelemetryReady` doc.
